# V4 Milestone 4 — Provider Ledger & Settlement Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Give every provider («مجری») an append-only, idempotent ledger: a credit when its job is delivered and paid (from the assignment snapshot, D26–D27), «نیازمند تعیین مبلغ» credits for unpriced work (D28), provider prices for the non-print services so new assignments price themselves (D29), a «جبران خسارت مجری» queue for jobs cancelled or taken away during/after production (D30), weekly settlement with payouts and voids that can never exceed the balance, a validated Sheba bank account (D31), the provider app «تسویه» tab, the admin finance screens and the «کیف پول دیجیتال سرو» report with CSV export (D32), all behind three new permissions (D33) — without changing a single customer price or quote (D22).

**Architecture:** A new `modules/ledger` owns the money of providers. Pure modules hold the rules (`ledger-model.ts` kinds/keys/titles/settlement calendar, `sheba.ts`, `credits.ts` credit drafts from a job snapshot, `compensation.ts` queue candidates, `wallet-report.ts` the period report). `LedgerService` is the single write path: credits are keyed upserts (`$setOnInsert` on a unique `key`), admin entries (adjustments, compensation, payouts, voids) additionally take a per-provider **slot** (`ledgerAccounts.rev` compare-and-set + a unique `slot` on the entry), so two concurrent payouts can never both pass the balance check; balances are always derived from the entries (no balance document to drift). `JobsService` sets `Job.ledgerPending` inside the same compare-and-set that moves a job to `delivered` and calls the ledger afterwards (never throws); `DispatchScheduler.tick` re-runs flagged jobs, so a crash between the two writes never loses a credit. Read/action/payout/wallet services sit on top; the price book gets provider-only combos for school binding, theses, flyers, cartridges, repairs and catalog extras (D29), and `providerQuote` prices them.

**Tech Stack:** NestJS 12 (ESM, `.js` import suffixes), Mongoose 9 / MongoDB 8 (standalone — no transactions), ioredis, `@nestjs/schedule`, class-validator, vitest; React 19, Vite 8 (multi-page), Tailwind v4, shadcn/ui, TanStack Query, zustand.

**Spec:** `Digital Sarv- Update.txt` (Master Specification V4 — §41 customer payment ≠ provider payout, §42 gross margin is internal, §66 «Do not implement unnecessary payout complexity in V1») + `docs/v4-design.md` («Ledger & settlement», decisions **D26–D33** binding, plus D8, D16, D20, D22–D25). Read both, plus the M3 plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-16-v4-m3-dispatch.md` (its «Handoff to M4») for the conventions this plan continues.

## Owner decisions for M4

D26–D33 in `docs/v4-design.md` are binding. The rulings below fill the gaps they leave; each is the recommended expert default (owner instruction «decide, don't ask») and is written into the contract in Task 1.

| # | Ruling |
|---|---|
| R1 | **Payout guard = CAS on a per-provider version.** Balances are derived from the entries (Σ `amount` of counted entries), never stored. Every admin-written entry (adjustment, compensation, payout, payout void) takes the provider's slot: read `ledgerAccounts.rev`, compute the balance, insert the entry with the unique `slot = "<providerId>:<rev>"`, then `$inc` `rev`. A second writer on the same slot gets a duplicate-key error, advances `rev` (self-healing after a crash) and re-checks the fresh balance. System credits only add money and take no slot. |
| R2 | **A pending credit is resolved in place, once.** `state: 'pending' → 'counted'` with the amount, by a compare-and-set on `state`. A counted amount is never edited again; corrections are new adjustments (D27). |
| R3 | **D29 provider combos** (provider layer only — a customer version of them is refused): `school|book` (per book), `school|lined` (per lined sheet), `docs|<bw\|color\|mixed>|<single\|double>` (per printed page), `docs|hardcover` (per copy), `flyer|<A4\|A5\|A6>|<color\|mono>|<glossy\|plain>|<single\|double>` (per flyer) + `flyer|design` (once), `cart|<laserBw\|laserColor\|inkjet>` (per cartridge), `repair|<laser\|inkjet\|mfp\|copier\|other>` (service fee per device), `extra|<extraKey>` (per item; also prices the print services' extras). Docs spiral uses the existing `spiral|<rangeId>` provider prices. Customer-only surcharges (spiral colour, school cover colour, docs bind colour / stamp) never form a provider line. |
| R4 | **Repairs:** the `repair|<device>` provider price is the service fee; parts or extra repair work are paid as a manual adjustment with a reason. |
| R5 | **Courier recount after assignment:** school child lines are recomputed at credit time with the snapshot's unit prices × the order's final book counts. If a child was removed or renamed, or a line no longer applies (lined/extras dropped), the production credit is created **pending** with the recomputed amount as `suggested` and the note «تعداد کتاب‌ها پس از تخصیص تغییر کرده است». |
| R6 | **Compensation queue (D30)** lists: cancelled jobs whose `cancel.stage` is `in_production` / `after_production` (the holder), and providers that lost the job by **reassign / unassign** at those stages. A provider's own `failed` is not compensated. One decision per (job, provider), including 0; a provider that later delivered the same job is not listed. |
| R7 | **Bank account:** only admins with `payout_record` set it (`PUT /admin/ledger/providers/:id/bank`; Sheba = `IR` + 24 digits, ISO 7064 mod-97 checksum, Persian digits and spaces accepted); the provider sees it masked and read-only; a payout needs a bank account on file. |
| R8 | **Provider privacy (D32):** the provider statement shows a Persian title per line (order code, kind), the date, its own amount and the payout bank reference — never admin reasons, notes, bases, other providers or customer prices. |
| R9 | **«کیف پول دیجیتال سرو» (D32):** customer charged = Σ `chargedAmount` of paid orders by delivery date; provider cost = Σ counted credits + adjustments by counted date; courier cost = (home pickups + courier deliveries) × the **current** `courier.perTaskFee` (labelled «برآورد», bonuses excluded); share = charged − provider cost − courier cost. Rows per settlement week, range ≤ 366 days, CSV in UTF-8 with BOM. |
| R10 | **Settlement calendar (D31):** cutoff = Tehran midnight of the latest settlement weekday ≤ now (default Saturday, `ledger.settlementWeekday` in «تنظیمات»). Statement week = [cutoff − 7 days, cutoff). `due` = max(0, `available` − credits/adjustments counted at or after the cutoff). Payout limit = `available` (D31 «amount ≤ balance»). |
| R11 | **Jobs delivered before M4** are credited once: `seed:base` flags delivered/completed jobs that have no `ledgerAt`; the reconcile pass credits them (keys make it idempotent). |
| R12 | **Notifications (D19):** `payout_recorded` and `payout_voided` → the provider's inbox + web push (DB templates, editable). |
| R13 | **Idempotent admin requests:** payouts and manual adjustments carry a client `requestId` (UUID v4); a retried request returns the same entry, a different body under the same id is 409. |
| R14 | A provider with ledger entries cannot be deleted (deactivate instead). A delivery fee of 0 creates no delivery credit. |

## Global Constraints

- Contract first: `docs/api-contract.md` gets «v4.3 — ledger and settlement» in Task 1, before any code; every later task matches it (paths, bodies, permissions, error codes, Persian messages).
- Money mutations are atomic conditional updates — `findOneAndUpdate`/`updateOne` with a guard (`state`, `rev`, `ledgerPending`), keyed upserts with `$setOnInsert`, unique indexes; **never read-modify-write**. MongoDB here is standalone: no multi-document transactions; every two-step write is ordered so a crash leaves a recoverable, idempotent state.
- The ledger is append-only: no entry is deleted; a counted `amount` is never changed; corrections are new `adjustment` entries; a payout is undone only by a `payout_void` entry.
- **No change to customer pricing or the quote (D22):** never touch `computeQuote`, `quoteDraft`, `priceService`, `print-v4.ts`, the customer price-book snapshot or catalog customer prices. Provider-only combos are refused on the customer layer. For the same draft the quote is byte-identical to M3.
- Lean V1 (§66): no bonuses, penalties or automatic deductions; discounts, plans, coupons, rules, the urgent fee and the customer's pickup/delivery fees are Digital Sarv's share only.
- Integer toman everywhere (`|amount| ≤ 1,000,000,000`). ESM relative imports end in `.js`. RTL logical utilities only (`ms-/me-/ps-/pe-/start-/end-/text-start`). Persian numerals in the UI via `lib/format.ts` (`fa`, `money`, `toNum`, `jalali`, `when`); ASCII numbers in the API.
- Nothing hard-coded that admins edit: the settlement weekday lives in `Settings.ledger`; notification texts are `notificationTemplates` rows seeded by `seed:base`; non-print provider prices are price-book versions.
- Catalog invalidation on catalog-affecting writes (`CatalogService.invalidate()`); settings writes already invalidate. Ledger writes do not affect the catalog.
- Visibility (§26–27, §42, §64, D32): customer responses never contain ledger data; provider responses only the caller's own entries (provider id from `ProviderScopeGuard`, never from the URL/body; another provider's ids answer 404); margins and customer prices never reach providers.
- The M3 job rules stay: every job status change goes through `JobsService.move` / `giveBack` / `cas`; the ledger only adds bookkeeping flags (`ledgerPending`, `ledgerAt`) in the same `cas` or by a guarded `updateOne`. Ledger hooks never throw into the job flow.
- Follow `.claude/skills/digital-sarv-design/SKILL.md`; reuse the admin kit (`PageHeader`, `AdminCard`, `CardTitle`, `CardNote`, `QueryView`, `AdminTable`, `AdminRow`, `Pager`, `SearchInput`, `FormDialog`, `Field`, `NumInput`, `AdminSelect`, `Tag`, `AddButton`, `Chip`, `InfoBanner`) and the mobile kit (`AppShell`, `MobileHeader`, `ScreenBody`, `Panel`, `BottomTabs`, `ToneTag`, `LoadingBlock`, `ErrorState`, `EmptyState`). Toast after every mutation.
- Machine limits (disk is tight): no dev servers, no REPLs, no Docker image builds, no `playwright install`, no new npm dependencies. Browser checks only via `playwright-core` with `channel: 'msedge'` and the `localStorage['sarv-auth'] = {state:{token,user},version:0}` seeding from `CLAUDE.md`.
- Tests: unit tests use `test/fake-model.ts` (aggregate with `$match/$sort/$limit/$lookup`, `$expr`, `$size`; this plan adds `$group` and opt-in unique indexes). E2E runs against the project DB (Mongo `27018`, Redis `6380`, from the root `.env`) with `DISPATCH_SCHEDULER=0`.
- Staging is explicit-path only (`git add <paths>`); **never stage `23.png`, `Sarv.png` or `Digital Sarv- Update.txt`**, never `git add -A`. Commit after every task; message ends with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.

## Review Focus

1. **Two admins record a payout for the same provider at the same moment (or one admin double-clicks)** — at most one payout passes the balance check, the other gets 400 «مبلغ پرداخت از مانده حساب مجری بیشتر است» (or the same entry back for the same `requestId`); the balance never goes below what the first payout left (Task 11 tests `concurrent payouts never exceed the balance`, `a retried request returns the same payout`).
2. **The API dies (or the ledger insert fails) right after a job was marked delivered** — the job keeps `ledgerPending`, the next scheduler tick creates the credit exactly once, and a second hook run creates nothing (Task 8 tests `a failed ledger write is retried by the tick`, `the hook and the tick never credit twice`).
3. **The courier recounts a school order after the job was assigned (books changed, or a child dropped)** — the credit uses the provider's snapshot unit price × the final count, or becomes «نیازمند تعیین مبلغ» with a suggested amount when a child no longer matches; never a silently wrong amount (Task 6 tests `recount changes the book count`, `a dropped child makes the credit pending`).
4. **An admin voids a payout twice, or voids while another admin records a payout** — one void entry only (409 the second time), the balance is restored once, and the concurrent payout re-checks the restored balance through the slot (Task 11 tests `a payout is voided once`, `void and payout race through the slot`).
5. **A provider (or a stolen provider token) probes another provider's ledger or tries to change its bank account** — `/provider/ledger*` only ever returns the caller's own lines (no reasons, no bases), there is no provider write endpoint for the bank account, and every `/admin/ledger*` route refuses provider tokens and admins without the permission (Task 13 tests `provider isolation`, Task 9 `permissions per route`).

### Task 1: Contract v4.3 and plan status (docs)

**Files:**
- Modify: `docs/api-contract.md` (append the section below at the end), `docs/plan.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: every name, path, permission, shape, error code and Persian message below is binding for Tasks 2–19.

- [ ] **Step 1: Append to `docs/api-contract.md`**

````markdown
## v4.3 — ledger and settlement (Master Specification V4, Milestone 4)

Design: `docs/v4-design.md` (D8, D16, D20, D22–D33) and the rulings R1–R14 of
`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-17-v4-m4-ledger.md`. Everything is additive; v1–v4.2 behaviour is unchanged unless stated.
Customer prices, quotes and catalog customer data are unchanged (D22). All amounts are integer toman.

### Ledger entries (`ledgerEntries`, append-only)

| kind | sign of `amount` | written by | key (unique) |
|---|---|---|---|
| `credit` | ≥ 0 (null while pending) | system (job delivered + order paid) | `credit:<jobId>:<providerId>:<production\|delivery>` |
| `adjustment` | ± (compensation ≥ 0) | admin | `comp:<jobId>:<providerId>` (compensation) · `adj:<requestId>` (manual) |
| `payout` | < 0 | admin | `payout:<requestId>` |
| `payout_void` | > 0 (= −payout) | admin | `void:<payoutId>` |

- `state`: `counted` (in the balance) or `pending` («نیازمند تعیین مبلغ», only credits; `amount: null`). A pending credit is
  resolved once (`pending → counted` with the amount, compare-and-set on `state`); a counted amount is never changed.
  Corrections are new adjustments; a payout is undone only by a `payout_void`.
- **Balance** of a provider = Σ `amount` of its counted entries. **Settlement** (D31): `Settings.ledger.settlementWeekday`
  (JS `getDay()`, default `6` = Saturday). `cutoff` = Tehran 00:00 of the latest settlement day ≤ now; the statement week
  ending at a cutoff = [cutoff − 7 days, cutoff). `due` = max(0, balance − Σ credits/adjustments counted at or after the
  cutoff). A payout may not exceed the balance (`available`).
- **Admin-written entries take the provider's slot** (R1): `ledgerAccounts {_id: providerId, rev}`; the entry stores the
  unique `slot = "<providerId>:<rev>"` it was computed on, then `rev` is incremented. A concurrent writer on the same slot
  re-reads the balance and is refused when it no longer fits. A lost race three times in a row answers
  **409 «حساب مجری هم‌زمان تغییر کرد؛ دوباره تلاش کنید»**.

`LedgerEntryView` (admin):
```ts
{ id, kind, state, amount: number | null, title, providerId, providerName?, orderId?, orderCode?, jobId?,
  role?: 'production' | 'delivery', cause?: 'compensation' | 'manual', compCause?: 'cancelled' | 'reassigned' | 'unassigned',
  occurredAt, countedAt?, reason?, basis?: CreditBasis, payout?: { paidAt /* yyyy-mm-dd */, bankRef, note?, sheba? /* masked */, holder? },
  voidOf?, voided: boolean, createdBy?: { id, name }, byRole: 'system' | 'admin',
  resolution?: { by: { id, name } | null, at, reason }, createdAt }
CreditBasis = { payoutBasis: number | null, suggested: number | null, missing: string[], snapshotAt?: string, note?: string }
```
`title` (Persian): credit «سفارش ۱۰۴۲۱» / delivery credit «ارسال سفارش ۱۰۴۲۱»; compensation «جبران خسارت سفارش ۱۰۴۲۱»;
manual «اصلاحیه سفارش ۱۰۴۲۱» or «اصلاحیه»; «پرداخت تسویه»; «ابطال پرداخت».

### When a credit is earned (D26–D28)

- A job's move to `delivered` (provider/admin `move`, or the order-level follow-up `onOrderDelivered` by courier/admin/system)
  sets `Job.ledgerPending: true` **in the same compare-and-set**; right after the write the ledger creates the credits
  (never throws). The dispatch tick re-runs every job with `ledgerPending` until it is credited (`ledgerPending` unset,
  `ledgerAt` set). Credits wait while the order is not `paid` (COD is paid in the same update as `delivered`).
- **Production credit** (the job's `providerId`, role `production`) = `payoutFor(snapshot)`: Σ snapshot line subtotals,
  where school child lines are recomputed with the snapshot unit price × the order's final book counts (R5), + the
  provider's delivery fee when that provider delivered. Counted when the snapshot belongs to that provider and its
  `payoutBasis` is not null and the child lines still match; otherwise **pending** with
  `basis.suggested` (Σ priced lines + own delivery fee, or the recomputed amount) and `basis.missing` (the missing labels, or
  «قیمت مجری برای این سفارش ثبت نشده است» without a snapshot) and `basis.note` («تعداد کتاب‌ها پس از تخصیص تغییر کرده است»).
- **Delivery credit** (a separate delivery provider, `deliveryMethod = 'provider'`, role `delivery`) = the snapshot's
  `delivery.fee` when it names that provider (a fee of 0 creates nothing); unknown fee → pending.
- A job delivered without a provider creates nothing. `occurredAt` = `Job.deliveredAt`; `countedAt` = when counted.
- `seed:base` flags delivered/completed jobs without `ledgerAt` once (jobs delivered before v4.3 are credited once, R11).

### Compensation («جبران خسارت مجری», D30, R6)

Candidates (one per job + provider): a `cancelled` job whose `cancel.stage` is `in_production` / `after_production` (the
holder, `cause: 'cancelled'`); a provider that lost the job by `reassigned` (its `previousProviderId`) or `unassigned` (its
`providerId`) with `stage` `in_production` / `after_production`. Not listed once decided (`comp:` key exists) or when the
same provider delivered the job (production credit exists). `payoutBasis` = that provider's latest snapshot `payoutBasis`
(`Job.providerPricing` or `Job.pricingHistory`), null when unpriced. The decision is an adjustment
`cause: 'compensation'`, `0 ≤ amount ≤ payoutBasis` (any amount ≥ 0 when null), with a reason. A refund after delivery never
touches credits; an admin may add a negative manual adjustment.

### Admin endpoints (`/admin/ledger`, role admin)

| Method + path | Permission | Body / query → response |
|---|---|---|
| `GET /admin/ledger/balances` | `ledger_view` | `?page&limit&q` → `Paged<ProviderBalanceRow>` |
| `GET /admin/ledger/providers/:id` | `ledger_view` | → `ProviderLedgerAdmin` |
| `GET /admin/ledger/providers/:id/entries` | `ledger_view` | `?page&limit&kind&from&to` (yyyy-mm-dd, Tehran, by `createdAt`) → `Paged<LedgerEntryView>` (newest first) |
| `GET /admin/ledger/providers/:id/statement` | `ledger_view` | `?cutoff=yyyy-mm-dd` (default the current cutoff) → `WeeklyStatement` |
| `PUT /admin/ledger/providers/:id/bank` | `payout_record` | `{ sheba, holder, bankName? }` → `BankAccountView` |
| `GET /admin/ledger/pending` | `ledger_view` | `?page&limit` → `Paged<LedgerEntryView>` (pending credits, oldest first) |
| `POST /admin/ledger/entries/:id/resolve` | `ledger_adjust` | `{ amount ≥ 0, reason }` → `LedgerEntryView` |
| `GET /admin/ledger/compensation` | `ledger_view` | `?page&limit` → `Paged<CompensationItem>` |
| `POST /admin/ledger/compensation` | `ledger_adjust` | `{ jobId, providerId, amount ≥ 0, reason }` → `LedgerEntryView` (201) |
| `POST /admin/ledger/adjustments` | `ledger_adjust` | `{ providerId, amount ≠ 0, reason, orderCode?, requestId }` → `LedgerEntryView` (201) |
| `POST /admin/ledger/payouts` | `payout_record` | `{ providerId, amount ≥ 1, paidAt, bankRef, note?, requestId }` → `LedgerEntryView` (201) |
| `POST /admin/ledger/payouts/:id/void` | `payout_record` | `{ reason }` → `LedgerEntryView` (the void entry) |
| `GET /admin/ledger/wallet` | `ledger_view` | `?from&to` (yyyy-mm-dd inclusive, ≤ 366 days) → `WalletReport` |
| `GET /admin/ledger/wallet/export` | `ledger_view` | same query → `text/csv; charset=utf-8` (BOM), `Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="sarv-wallet-<from>-<to>.csv"` |

```ts
LedgerBalance       { available, due, earnedSinceCutoff, pendingCount }
SettlementInfo      { weekday, cutoff /* yyyy-mm-dd of the cutoff day */, next /* yyyy-mm-dd */ }
BankAccountView     { sheba, holder, bankName, updatedAt }
ProviderBalanceRow  { providerId, name, businessName, status, balance: LedgerBalance, lastPayoutAt: string | null, hasBank }
ProviderLedgerAdmin { provider: { id, name, businessName, status }, balance: LedgerBalance, bank: BankAccountView | null, settlement: SettlementInfo }
WeeklyStatement     { providerId, from, to /* yyyy-mm-dd, inclusive */, opening, earned, paid, closing, lines: LedgerEntryView[] }
CompensationItem    { jobId, orderId, orderCode, providerId, providerName, cause, stage: 'in_production' | 'after_production', at, payoutBasis: number | null }
WalletRow           { from, to, orders, charged, providerCost, courierTasks, courierCost, share }
WalletReport        { from, to, perTaskFee, rows: WalletRow[], total: WalletRow }
```
`WeeklyStatement`: `opening` = balance counted before `from`, `earned` = Σ credits/adjustments counted in the week,
`paid` = −Σ payouts/voids counted in the week, `closing` = opening + earned − paid; `lines` = the week's counted entries.
CSV columns: «از تاریخ, تا تاریخ, سفارش‌های تحویل‌شده, دریافتی از مشتریان, سهم مجریان, کارهای پیک, دستمزد پیک (برآورد), سهم دیجیتال سرو», one row per
week and «جمع»; ASCII digits and Gregorian dates.

**Wallet formula (D32, R9):** `charged` = Σ `chargedAmount` (fallback `quote.total`) of paid orders whose last `delivered`
timeline entry is in the row; `providerCost` = Σ counted credits + adjustments by `countedAt`; `courierTasks` = home
pickups (`picked_up` / `awaiting_approval` timeline) + courier deliveries (`delivery.method = 'courier'`, `delivered`
timeline) in the row; `courierCost` = `courierTasks × courier.perTaskFee` (current setting, an estimate); `share` =
`charged − providerCost − courierCost`. Rows are the settlement weeks intersecting [from, to] (first/last clipped).

### Provider endpoints (`/provider/ledger`, role provider, `ProviderScopeGuard`)

| Method + path | Response |
|---|---|
| `GET /provider/ledger` | `{ balance: { available, due, pendingCount }, settlement: { weekday, next }, bank: { sheba /* masked IR••…••0001 */, holder } \| null }` |
| `GET /provider/ledger/entries?page&limit` | `Paged<{ id, kind, state, title, orderCode?, at, amount: number \| null, bankRef?, voided? }>` (newest first) |

Only the caller's entries; never reasons, notes, bases, `createdBy`, other providers or customer prices (D32, R8).
There is no provider write endpoint for the bank account (R7).

### Bank account (D31, R7)

`Center.bankAccount { sheba, holder, bankName, updatedAt, updatedBy }`. Sheba: Persian/Arabic digits, spaces and dashes are
accepted and normalized to `IR` + 24 ASCII digits (24 digits without `IR` get the prefix); the ISO 7064 mod-97 check must
pass. Messages: «شماره شبا باید با IR شروع شود و ۲۴ رقم داشته باشد», «شماره شبا معتبر نیست؛ رقم‌های آن را بررسی کنید»,
holder 2–80 characters «نام صاحب حساب را وارد کنید». Provider admin/self JSON (v4.1) do not include it.

### Non-print provider prices (D29, R3)

New price-book combos, **provider layer only** (a customer-layer create/preview is 400 «این ترکیب فقط قیمت مجری دارد»):
`school|book`, `school|lined`, `docs|<bw|color|mixed>|<single|double>`, `docs|hardcover`,
`flyer|<A4|A5|A6>|<color|mono>|<glossy|plain>|<single|double>`, `flyer|design`, `cart|<laserBw|laserColor|inkjet>`,
`repair|<laser|inkjet|mfp|copier|other>`, `extra|<extraKey>`. `service` values of `GET /admin/price-book`,
`GET /provider/prices` and the bulk filter accept `school`, `docs`, `flyer`, `cart`, `repair`, `extra` (compare stays
print-only). Submission → approval exactly as in v4.1. Quantities at assignment: school = books, lined sheets
(books × lined count), extras (books per extra); docs = printed pages × copies + hardcover copies (spiral docs use the
order's `spiral|<rangeId>` lines); flyer = quantity (+ design once when «طراحی توسط ما»); cartridge = count; repair = 1
(`device` missing → `other`); print services' `extra|<key>` lines are priced like any combo; `spiral_color|…` lines are
customer-only and have no provider line. «قیمت مجری تنظیم نشده» + `confirmMissingPrice` remain for lines still unpriced
(the v3 «چاپ اسناد» kind, combos without a provider price). Snapshot lines of school children carry `childIndex` +
`childName`; the snapshot carries `childCount`.

### Permissions (D33)

`ledger_view` (balances, statements, entries, pending, compensation queue, wallet report and CSV), `ledger_adjust`
(resolve pending, compensation, manual adjustments), `payout_record` (record/void payouts, set the bank account).
Super-admins have all; shown in «کارکنان و دسترسی‌ها» as «مالی و تسویه».

### Settings («تنظیمات»)

`ledger: { settlementWeekday: 0–6 }` (default 6) in `GET/PUT /admin/settings` (per-field save, «روز هفته معتبر نیست (۰ یکشنبه … ۶ شنبه)»).

### Notifications (D19, R12)

Provider events `payout_recorded` («پرداخت تسویه برای شما ثبت شد.» + « — ۱٬۲۰۰٬۰۰۰ تومان») and `payout_voided`
(«یک پرداخت تسویه باطل شد.» + amount), inbox + web push, url `/provider/settlement`. Inbox rows of these events have no
order: `orderId: ''`, `orderCode: ''` in `UserNotification`.

### Errors

400 «مبلغ باید عدد صحیح (تومان) باشد» · «مبلغ پرداخت از مانده حساب مجری بیشتر است» · «ابتدا شماره شبای مجری را ثبت کنید» ·
«تاریخ پرداخت نمی‌تواند در آینده باشد» · «شماره پیگیری بانکی را وارد کنید» · «تاریخ باید به‌صورت yyyy-mm-dd باشد» · «مبلغ جبران خسارت نمی‌تواند از سهم قابل پرداخت به مجری بیشتر باشد» ·
«مبلغ اصلاحیه نمی‌تواند صفر باشد» · «دلیل را بنویسید» · «بازه گزارش حداکثر ۳۶۶ روز است» · «تاریخ پایان نباید قبل از تاریخ شروع باشد» ·
«این ترکیب فقط قیمت مجری دارد». 404 «مجری یافت نشد» · «ردیف حساب یافت نشد» · «پرداخت یافت نشد» · «مورد جبران خسارت یافت نشد» ·
«سفارش یافت نشد». 409 «این مبلغ قبلاً تعیین شده است» · «برای این مورد قبلاً تصمیم گرفته شده است» · «این پرداخت قبلاً باطل شده است» ·
«این درخواست قبلاً با مقادیر دیگری ثبت شده است» · «حساب مجری هم‌زمان تغییر کرد؛ دوباره تلاش کنید» ·
`DELETE /admin/providers/:id` «این مجری در حساب تسویه سابقه دارد؛ به‌جای حذف آن را غیرفعال کنید».

### Seed (dev)

Demo `seed`: «چاپ نگین» gets non-print provider prices (`school|book` 9,000, `school|lined` 300, `docs|bw|single` 600,
`docs|hardcover` 60,000, `cart|laserBw` 250,000, `flyer|A5|color|glossy|single` 120; `repair|laser` 400,000 pending
approval), the bank account `IR062960000000100324200001` «علی نگینی», two counted credits, one pending credit and one
payout. `seed:base` inserts the two payout templates and flags delivered jobs for crediting (R11).

### Admin menu and apps

Admin: «تسویه مجریان» (`/admin/ledger`, after «مدیریت مجریان», `ledger_view`; tabs «مانده مجریان», «نیازمند تعیین مبلغ»,
«جبران خسارت مجری», «کیف پول دیجیتال سرو»; a provider sheet with statement, entries, bank account, payout and
adjustment). «تنظیمات» gets «تسویه مجریان» (settlement weekday). Pricing center «قیمت مجریان» lists the new services.
Provider app: new «تسویه» tab (`/provider/settlement`); «قیمت‌های من» lists the new services.
````

```ts
describe('ledger keys, amounts and titles (D27)', () => {

  const SAT = 6;

    expect(nextSettlementYmd(new Date('2026-10-20T10:00:00Z'), SAT)).toBe('2026-10-24');

    const cutoff = new Date('2026-10-16T20:30:00.000Z');
    expect(weekEndingAt(cutoff)).toEqual({ from: new Date('2026-10-09T20:30:00.000Z'), to: cutoff });

});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `apps/api/src/modules/ledger/ledger-model.ts`**

```ts
/**
 * v4.3 provider ledger (D26–D33) — pure: entry kinds, idempotency keys, amount rule, Persian titles and the weekly
 * settlement calendar (Tehran). No Mongo here.
 */
import { addDaysYmd, tehranDayStart, tehranWeekday, tehranYmd } from '../../common/utils/dates.js';
import { faDigits } from '../../common/utils/fa.js';

export const LEDGER_KINDS = ['credit', 'adjustment', 'payout', 'payout_void'] as const;
export type LedgerKind = (typeof LEDGER_KINDS)[number];
export const LEDGER_STATES = ['counted', 'pending'] as const;
export type LedgerState = (typeof LEDGER_STATES)[number];
export const CREDIT_ROLES = ['production', 'delivery'] as const;
export type CreditRole = (typeof CREDIT_ROLES)[number];
export const ADJUSTMENT_CAUSES = ['compensation', 'manual'] as const;
export type AdjustmentCause = (typeof ADJUSTMENT_CAUSES)[number];
export const COMP_CAUSES = ['cancelled', 'reassigned', 'unassigned'] as const;
export type CompCause = (typeof COMP_CAUSES)[number];
/** D30: the stages at which a provider that lost the job may be compensated. */
export const COMP_STAGES = ['in_production', 'after_production'] as const;
export type CompStage = (typeof COMP_STAGES)[number];
/** Every ledger amount is an integer toman with |amount| ≤ this. */
export const MAX_AMOUNT = 1_000_000_000;

/** Contract v4.3 «Ledger entries»: one entry per key, ever. */
export const ledgerKey = {
  credit: (jobId: unknown, providerId: unknown, role: CreditRole) => `credit:${String(jobId)}:${String(providerId)}:${role}`,
  compensation: (jobId: unknown, providerId: unknown) => `comp:${String(jobId)}:${String(providerId)}`,
  adjustment: (requestId: string) => `adj:${requestId}`,
  payout: (requestId: string) => `payout:${requestId}`,
  void: (payoutId: unknown) => `void:${String(payoutId)}`,
};

/** R1: the provider version an admin entry was computed on (unique on the entry). */
export const slotOf = (providerId: string, rev: number) => `${providerId}:${rev}`;

export function isAmount(n: unknown, opts: { min: number }): n is number {
  return typeof n === 'number' && Number.isInteger(n) && n >= opts.min && Math.abs(n) <= MAX_AMOUNT;
}

export interface TitleSource { kind: LedgerKind; role?: CreditRole; cause?: AdjustmentCause; orderCode?: string }

/** The Persian line title shown to admins and providers (never the admin's reason). */
export function entryTitle(e: TitleSource): string {
  const code = e.orderCode ? ` ${faDigits(e.orderCode)}` : '';
  switch (e.kind) {
    case 'credit': return e.role === 'delivery' ? `ارسال سفارش${code}` : `سفارش${code}`;
    case 'adjustment': return e.cause === 'compensation' ? `جبران خسارت سفارش${code}` : code ? `اصلاحیه سفارش${code}` : 'اصلاحیه';
    case 'payout': return 'پرداخت تسویه';
    case 'payout_void': return 'ابطال پرداخت';
  }
}

/** Start (Tehran midnight) of the latest settlement day on or before `now` — the weekly cutoff (D31, R10). */
export function settlementCutoff(now: Date, weekday: number): Date {
  let ymd = tehranYmd(now);
  for (let i = 0; i < 7 && tehranWeekday(ymd) !== weekday; i++) ymd = addDaysYmd(ymd, -1);
  return tehranDayStart(ymd);
}

/** The next settlement day (`yyyy-mm-dd`, Tehran) — today when today is the settlement weekday. */
export function nextSettlementYmd(now: Date, weekday: number): string {
  let ymd = tehranYmd(now);
  for (let i = 0; i < 7 && tehranWeekday(ymd) !== weekday; i++) ymd = addDaysYmd(ymd, 1);
  return ymd;
}

/** The statement week that ends at `cutoff`: [cutoff − 7 days, cutoff) (Iran has no DST). */
export function weekEndingAt(cutoff: Date): { from: Date; to: Date } {
  return { from: new Date(cutoff.getTime() - 7 * 86_400_000), to: cutoff };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `apps/api/src/modules/ledger/sheba.ts`**

```ts
/** Iranian IBAN («شبا», D31/R7): `IR` + 24 digits, ISO 7064 mod-97 check. Pure. */
import { toAsciiDigits } from '../../common/utils/phone.js';

const SHAPE = /^IR\d{24}$/;

/** ASCII digits, no spaces / dashes / ZWNJ, upper case; 24 bare digits get the `IR` prefix. */
export function normalizeSheba(raw: string): string {
  const s = toAsciiDigits(String(raw ?? '')).replace(/[\s\-‌]/g, '').toUpperCase();
  return /^\d{24}$/.test(s) ? `IR${s}` : s;
}

export function isValidSheba(s: string): boolean {
  if (!SHAPE.test(s)) return false;
  // move «IR» + the check digits to the end; I = 18, R = 27
  const digits = `${s.slice(4)}1827${s.slice(2, 4)}`;
  let r = 0;
  for (const ch of digits) r = (r * 10 + Number(ch)) % 97;
  return r === 1;
}

export function shebaProblem(raw: string): string | null {
  const s = normalizeSheba(raw);
  if (!SHAPE.test(s)) return 'شماره شبا باید با IR شروع شود و ۲۴ رقم داشته باشد';
  return isValidSheba(s) ? null : 'شماره شبا معتبر نیست؛ رقم‌های آن را بررسی کنید';
}

/** `IR••••••••••••••••••••0001` — what a provider (and a payout record) shows. */
export function maskSheba(s: string): string {
  return s.length < 8 ? s : `${s.slice(0, 2)}${'•'.repeat(s.length - 6)}${s.slice(-4)}`;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Ledger schemas**

`apps/api/src/modules/ledger/ledger-entry.schema.ts`:
```ts
import { Prop, Schema, SchemaFactory } from '@nestjs/mongoose';
import { HydratedDocument, Schema as MongooseSchema, Types } from 'mongoose';
import {
  ADJUSTMENT_CAUSES, COMP_CAUSES, CREDIT_ROLES, LEDGER_KINDS, LEDGER_STATES,
  type AdjustmentCause, type CompCause, type CreditRole, type LedgerKind, type LedgerState,
} from './ledger-model.js';

/** What a credit was computed from (admin only): the snapshot basis, the suggested amount of a pending credit, missing labels. */
export interface CreditBasis { payoutBasis: number | null; suggested: number | null; missing: string[]; snapshotAt?: string; note?: string }
export interface PayoutInfo { paidAt: string; bankRef: string; note?: string; sheba?: string; holder?: string }
export interface Resolution { by: Types.ObjectId; at: Date; reason: string }

/**
 * v4.3 provider ledger entry (D26–D33). Append-only: an entry is never deleted and a counted `amount` never changes;
 * the only in-place change is a pending credit's one-time resolution (R2).
 */
@Schema({ collection: 'ledgerEntries', timestamps: { createdAt: true, updatedAt: false } })
export class LedgerEntry {
  /** Idempotency key (contract «Ledger entries»). */
  @Prop({ type: String, required: true }) key: string;
  @Prop({ type: MongooseSchema.Types.ObjectId, required: true }) providerId: Types.ObjectId;
  @Prop({ type: String, enum: LEDGER_KINDS, required: true }) kind: LedgerKind;
  @Prop({ type: String, enum: LEDGER_STATES, default: 'counted' }) state: LedgerState;
  /** Signed integer toman (credit ≥ 0, payout < 0, void > 0); null while pending. */
  @Prop({ type: Number, default: null }) amount: number | null;
  @Prop({ type: String, enum: CREDIT_ROLES }) role?: CreditRole;
  @Prop({ type: String, enum: ADJUSTMENT_CAUSES }) cause?: AdjustmentCause;
  @Prop({ type: String, enum: COMP_CAUSES }) compCause?: CompCause;
  @Prop({ type: MongooseSchema.Types.ObjectId }) jobId?: Types.ObjectId;
  @Prop({ type: MongooseSchema.Types.ObjectId }) orderId?: Types.ObjectId;
  @Prop({ type: String }) orderCode?: string;
  /** Business date: the delivery for a credit, the bank date for a payout, the creation otherwise. */
  @Prop({ type: Date, required: true }) occurredAt: Date;
  /** When the amount entered the balance (absent while pending) — statements and cutoffs use it. */
  @Prop({ type: Date }) countedAt?: Date;
  @Prop({ type: MongooseSchema.Types.Mixed }) basis?: CreditBasis;
  @Prop({ type: MongooseSchema.Types.Mixed }) payout?: PayoutInfo;
  @Prop({ type: MongooseSchema.Types.ObjectId }) voidOf?: Types.ObjectId;
  /** R1: the provider version (`ledgerAccounts.rev`) this admin entry was computed on. */
  @Prop({ type: String }) slot?: string;
  @Prop({ type: String }) reason?: string;
  @Prop({ type: MongooseSchema.Types.ObjectId }) createdBy?: Types.ObjectId;
  @Prop({ type: String, enum: ['system', 'admin'], required: true }) byRole: 'system' | 'admin';
  @Prop({ type: MongooseSchema.Types.Mixed }) resolution?: Resolution;

  createdAt: Date;
}

export type LedgerEntryDocument = HydratedDocument<LedgerEntry>;
export const LedgerEntrySchema = SchemaFactory.createForClass(LedgerEntry);
LedgerEntrySchema.index({ key: 1 }, { unique: true });
LedgerEntrySchema.index({ slot: 1 }, { unique: true, partialFilterExpression: { slot: { $type: 'string' } } });
LedgerEntrySchema.index({ providerId: 1, state: 1, countedAt: 1 });
LedgerEntrySchema.index({ providerId: 1, createdAt: -1 });
LedgerEntrySchema.index({ state: 1, createdAt: 1 });
LedgerEntrySchema.index({ countedAt: 1 });
LedgerEntrySchema.index({ voidOf: 1 }, { sparse: true });
LedgerEntrySchema.index({ jobId: 1 }, { sparse: true });
```

`apps/api/src/modules/ledger/ledger-account.schema.ts`:
```ts
import { Prop, Schema, SchemaFactory } from '@nestjs/mongoose';
import { HydratedDocument, Schema as MongooseSchema, Types } from 'mongoose';

/** R1: per-provider version of the admin-written ledger entries (`_id` = the provider id). Holds no money. */
@Schema({ collection: 'ledgerAccounts', timestamps: true })
export class LedgerAccount {
  @Prop({ type: MongooseSchema.Types.ObjectId }) _id: Types.ObjectId;
  @Prop({ type: Number, default: 0 }) rev: number;
}

export type LedgerAccountDocument = HydratedDocument<LedgerAccount>;
export const LedgerAccountSchema = SchemaFactory.createForClass(LedgerAccount);
```

  - `combo.ts`: `DOC_INKS`, `DocInk`, `FLYER_SIZES`, `FLYER_INKS`, `FLYER_PAPERS`, `CART_TYPE_KEYS`, `REPAIR_DEVICE_KEYS`, `COMBO_SERVICES` (10 values), `ComboService` (`'bw' | 'color' | 'spiral' | 'addon' | 'school' | 'docs' | 'flyer' | 'cart' | 'repair' | 'extra'`), `PROVIDER_ONLY_SERVICES`, `isProviderOnly(service): boolean`; `ComboOptions` gains the variants below.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** `apps/api/src/modules/price-book/provider-combos.spec.ts`

```ts
import { BadRequestException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { fakeModel } from '../../../test/fake-model.js';
import { comboWithinCapabilities } from '../centers/provider-model.js';
import { comboKey, isProviderOnly, parseCombo } from './combo.js';
import { comboLabel } from './labels.js';
import { PriceBookService } from './price-book.service.js';

const KEYS = [
  'school|book', 'school|lined', 'docs|bw|single', 'docs|mixed|double', 'docs|hardcover',
  'flyer|A5|color|glossy|single', 'flyer|A4|mono|plain|double', 'flyer|design',
  'cart|laserBw', 'cart|inkjet', 'repair|laser', 'repair|other', 'extra|trim',
];

describe('D29 provider-only combos', () => {
  it('round-trip and are provider-only; print combos are not', () => {
    for (const k of KEYS) {
      const o = parseCombo(k);
      expect(o, k).not.toBeNull();
      expect(comboKey(o!)).toBe(k);
      expect(isProviderOnly(o!.service)).toBe(true);
    }
    for (const s of ['bw', 'color', 'spiral', 'addon'] as const) expect(isProviderOnly(s)).toBe(false);
  });
  it('refuses unknown parts', () => {
    for (const k of ['school|cover', 'docs|bw', 'docs|gold|single', 'flyer|A3|color|glossy|single', 'flyer|A5|color|matte|single', 'cart|toner', 'repair|fax', 'extra|bad key', 'extra|', 'spiral_color|blue']) {
      expect(parseCombo(k), k).toBeNull();
    }
  });
  it('labels every variant in Persian', () => {
    const names = { papers: new Map(), ranges: new Map(), extras: new Map([['trim', 'برش']]) };
    const label = (k: string) => comboLabel(parseCombo(k)!, names);
    expect(label('school|book')).toBe('فنری کتاب مدرسه · هر کتاب');
    expect(label('school|lined')).toBe('فنری کتاب مدرسه · هر برگ خط‌دار');
    expect(label('docs|color|double')).toBe('پایان‌نامه · چاپ رنگی · دورو · هر صفحه');
    expect(label('docs|hardcover')).toBe('پایان‌نامه · صحافی گالینگور · هر نسخه');
    expect(label('flyer|A5|color|glossy|single')).toBe('تراکت · A5 · تمام‌رنگی · گلاسه · یک‌رو · هر برگ');
    expect(label('flyer|design')).toBe('تراکت · طراحی');
    expect(label('cart|laserColor')).toBe('شارژ کارتریج · لیزری رنگی');
    expect(label('repair|mfp')).toBe('تعمیر پرینتر · چندکاره');
    expect(label('extra|trim')).toBe('خدمات اضافی · برش');
    expect(comboLabel(parseCombo('extra|gone')!, { papers: new Map(), ranges: new Map() })).toBe('خدمات اضافی · gone');
  });
  it('capability hint: each service needs its own capability; extras need a print or school capability', () => {
    const cap = (service: string) => ({ service, sizes: [], colorTypes: [], papers: [], sides: [], addons: [] }) as never;
    expect(comboWithinCapabilities(parseCombo('school|book')!, [cap('school')])).toBe(true);
    expect(comboWithinCapabilities(parseCombo('docs|hardcover')!, [cap('school')])).toBe(false);
    expect(comboWithinCapabilities(parseCombo('flyer|design')!, [cap('flyer')])).toBe(true);
    expect(comboWithinCapabilities(parseCombo('cart|inkjet')!, [cap('cart')])).toBe(true);
    expect(comboWithinCapabilities(parseCombo('repair|other')!, [cap('repair')])).toBe(true);
    expect(comboWithinCapabilities(parseCombo('extra|trim')!, [cap('school')])).toBe(true);
    expect(comboWithinCapabilities(parseCombo('extra|trim')!, [cap('cart')])).toBe(false);
  });
});

function setup() {
  const A = new Types.ObjectId();
  const admin = new Types.ObjectId();
  const versions = fakeModel([]);
  const users = fakeModel([{ _id: admin, name: 'مدیر', role: 'admin' }]);
  const centers = fakeModel([{ _id: A, name: 'مجری الف', status: 'active', capabilities: [{ service: 'cart', sizes: [], colorTypes: [], papers: [], sides: [], addons: [] }] }]);
  const extras = fakeModel([{ _id: new Types.ObjectId(), key: 'trim', label: 'برش', price: 5000, on: true, sort: 1 }]);
  const catalog = { invalidate: vi.fn(async () => undefined) };
  const svc = new PriceBookService(versions as never, fakeModel([]) as never, fakeModel([]) as never, fakeModel([]) as never, users as never, catalog as never, centers as never, extras as never);
  return { svc, versions, by: String(admin), A: String(A) };
}

describe('price book with D29 combos', () => {
  it('a customer-layer version of a provider-only combo is refused (D22: customer prices unchanged)', async () => {
    const { svc, by, versions } = setup();
    const body = { layer: 'customer' as const, comboKey: 'school|book', mode: 'manual' as const, price: 9000, publish: true, reason: 'x' };
    await expect(svc.create(body, by)).rejects.toThrow(new BadRequestException('این ترکیب فقط قیمت مجری دارد'));
    await expect(svc.preview({ comboKey: 'cart|laserBw', mode: 'manual', price: 1, layer: 'customer' })).resolves.toEqual({ unitPrice: null, error: 'این ترکیب فقط قیمت مجری دارد' });
    expect(versions.docs).toHaveLength(0);
  });
  it('the customer matrix never lists them; the provider matrix lists every key of a service (extras from the catalog)', async () => {
    const { svc, A } = setup();
    const customerKeys = (await svc.matrix('customer')).map((r) => r.comboKey);
    expect(customerKeys.some((k) => /^(school|docs|flyer|cart|repair|extra)\|/.test(k))).toBe(false);
    expect((await svc.matrix('provider', 'school', new Date(), A)).map((r) => r.comboKey)).toEqual(['school|book', 'school|lined']);
    expect((await svc.matrix('provider', 'docs', new Date(), A)).map((r) => r.comboKey)).toEqual([
      'docs|bw|single', 'docs|bw|double', 'docs|color|single', 'docs|color|double', 'docs|mixed|single', 'docs|mixed|double', 'docs|hardcover',
    ]);
    expect(await svc.matrix('provider', 'flyer', new Date(), A)).toHaveLength(25);
    expect((await svc.matrix('provider', 'cart', new Date(), A)).map((r) => r.comboKey)).toEqual(['cart|laserBw', 'cart|laserColor', 'cart|inkjet']);
    expect((await svc.matrix('provider', 'repair', new Date(), A)).map((r) => r.comboKey)).toEqual(['repair|laser', 'repair|inkjet', 'repair|mfp', 'repair|copier', 'repair|other']);
    expect((await svc.matrix('provider', 'extra', new Date(), A)).map((r) => [r.comboKey, r.label])).toEqual([['extra|trim', 'خدمات اضافی · برش']]);
  });
  it('a provider submits a cartridge price; approval publishes it on the provider layer only', async () => {
    const { svc, A, by, versions } = setup();
    const v = await svc.submit(A, { comboKey: 'cart|laserBw', price: 250000, reason: 'قیمت جدید' }, by);
    expect(v.status).toBe('pending_approval');
    await svc.approve(v.id, 'تأیید', by);
    const row = (await svc.providerPrices(A, 'cart', by)).find((r) => r.comboKey === 'cart|laserBw')!;
    expect(row).toMatchObject({ capable: true, current: { price: 250000, status: 'active' } });
    expect(versions.docs.every((d) => d.layer === 'provider')).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

```ts
  | { service: 'color'; size: PrintSize; colorType: ColorType; paper: string; side: Side }
  | { service: 'spiral'; rangeId: string }

  | { service: 'cart'; cartType: CartTypeKey }
  | { service: 'repair'; device: RepairDeviceKey }

export type ComboService = ComboOptions['service'];

export const PROVIDER_ONLY_SERVICES: readonly ComboService[] = ['school', 'docs', 'flyer', 'cart', 'repair', 'extra'];
export const isProviderOnly = (s: ComboService): boolean => PROVIDER_ONLY_SERVICES.includes(s);
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `labels.ts`** — whole file:

```ts
import { faDigits } from '../../common/utils/fa.js';
import type { ComboOptions } from './combo.js';

const SIDE = { single: 'یک‌رو', double: 'دورو' } as const;
const COLOR = { text: 'متن رنگی', full: 'تمام‌رنگی' } as const;
const ADDON = { glue: 'صحافی ته‌چسب', hardcover: 'صحافی گالینگور', staple: 'منگنه', lam_cover: 'لمینت جلد', lam_sheet: 'لمینت هر برگ' } as const;
const DOC_INK = { bw: 'سیاه‌وسفید', color: 'رنگی', mixed: 'ترکیبی' } as const;
const FLYER_INK = { color: 'تمام‌رنگی', mono: 'سیاه‌وسفید' } as const;
const FLYER_PAPER = { glossy: 'گلاسه', plain: 'تحریر' } as const;
const CART = { laserBw: 'لیزری سیاه', laserColor: 'لیزری رنگی', inkjet: 'جوهرافشان' } as const;
const DEVICE = { laser: 'لیزری', inkjet: 'جوهرافشان', mfp: 'چندکاره', copier: 'فتوکپی', other: 'سایر' } as const;
const fa = (n: number) => faDigits(n);

export interface LabelNames {
  papers: Map<string, string>;
  ranges: Map<string, { minPages: number; maxPages: number }>;
  /** v4.3: catalog extras by key (`extra|<key>`). */
  extras?: Map<string, string>;
}

/** Persian admin label of a combination, e.g. «چاپ رنگی · A4 · تمام‌رنگی · مقوا ۱۶۰ گرم · دورو». */
export function comboLabel(o: ComboOptions, names: LabelNames): string {
  switch (o.service) {
    case 'bw': return `چاپ سیاه‌وسفید · ${o.size} · ${SIDE[o.side]}`;
    case 'color': return `چاپ رنگی · ${o.size} · ${COLOR[o.colorType]} · ${names.papers.get(o.paper) ?? o.paper} · ${SIDE[o.side]}`;
    case 'spiral': {
      const r = names.ranges.get(o.rangeId);
      return r ? `فنری ${fa(r.minPages)} تا ${fa(r.maxPages)} صفحه` : 'فنری (بازه حذف‌شده)';
    }
    case 'addon': return o.addon === 'lam_sheet' ? `${ADDON.lam_sheet} · ${o.size}` : ADDON[o.addon];
    case 'school': return o.item === 'book' ? 'فنری کتاب مدرسه · هر کتاب' : 'فنری کتاب مدرسه · هر برگ خط‌دار';
    case 'docs': return 'item' in o ? 'پایان‌نامه · صحافی گالینگور · هر نسخه' : `پایان‌نامه · چاپ ${DOC_INK[o.ink]} · ${SIDE[o.side]} · هر صفحه`;
    case 'flyer': return 'item' in o ? 'تراکت · طراحی' : `تراکت · ${o.size} · ${FLYER_INK[o.ink]} · ${FLYER_PAPER[o.paper]} · ${SIDE[o.side]} · هر برگ`;
    case 'cart': return `شارژ کارتریج · ${CART[o.cartType]}`;
    case 'repair': return `تعمیر پرینتر · ${DEVICE[o.device]}`;
    case 'extra': return `خدمات اضافی · ${names.extras?.get(o.key) ?? o.key}`;
  }
}
```

```ts
  /** Catalog extras (`extra|<key>` provider combos) — only the provider layer has them. */
  private async extraList(layer: PriceLayer): Promise<{ key: string; label: string }[]> {
    if (layer !== 'provider' || !this.extras) return [];
    return (await this.extras.find({}, { key: 1, label: 1 }).sort({ sort: 1, _id: 1 }).lean()) as { key: string; label: string }[];
  }
```
- `names()` becomes:
```ts
  private names(papers: { key: string; name: string }[], ranges: { _id: unknown; minPages: number; maxPages: number }[], extras: { key: string; label: string }[] = []): LabelNames {
    return {
      papers: new Map(papers.map((p) => [p.key, p.name])),
      ranges: new Map(ranges.map((r) => [String(r._id), { minPages: r.minPages, maxPages: r.maxPages }])),
      extras: new Map(extras.map((x) => [x.key, x.label])),
    };
  }
```
- `matrix()`: the `Promise.all` loads a fourth value `this.extraList(layer)` (`extras`); `const names = this.names(papers, ranges, extras);`; the loop iterates `this.knownKeys(papers, ranges, rows, layer, extras)`.
- `pendingList()`: add `this.extraList('provider')` to its `Promise.all` (`extras`) and build `this.names(papers, ranges, extras)`.

- `knownKeys()` becomes:
```ts
  /** Every known combination of the layer (+ any combo with versions). Provider-only combos exist on the provider layer only. */
  private knownKeys(papers: { key: string; colorPrint?: boolean }[], ranges: { _id: unknown }[], rows: { comboKey: string }[], layer: PriceLayer, extras: { key: string }[] = []): string[] {
    const keys: string[] = [];
    for (const size of PRINT_SIZES) for (const side of SIDES) keys.push(comboKey({ service: 'bw', size, side }));
    for (const size of PRINT_SIZES) for (const colorType of COLOR_TYPES) for (const p of papers.filter((x) => x.colorPrint)) for (const side of SIDES) {
      keys.push(comboKey({ service: 'color', size, colorType, paper: p.key, side }));
    }
    for (const r of ranges) keys.push(comboKey({ service: 'spiral', rangeId: String(r._id) }));
    for (const addon of PRINT_ADDONS) {
      if (addon === 'lam_sheet') for (const size of PRINT_SIZES) keys.push(comboKey({ service: 'addon', addon, size }));
      else keys.push(comboKey({ service: 'addon', addon }));
    }
    if (layer === 'provider') {
      keys.push('school|book', 'school|lined');
      for (const ink of DOC_INKS) for (const side of SIDES) keys.push(comboKey({ service: 'docs', ink, side }));
      keys.push('docs|hardcover');
      for (const size of FLYER_SIZES) for (const ink of FLYER_INKS) for (const paper of FLYER_PAPERS) for (const side of SIDES) keys.push(comboKey({ service: 'flyer', size, ink, paper, side }));
      keys.push('flyer|design');
      for (const cartType of CART_TYPE_KEYS) keys.push(comboKey({ service: 'cart', cartType }));
      for (const device of REPAIR_DEVICE_KEYS) keys.push(comboKey({ service: 'repair', device }));
      for (const x of extras) keys.push(comboKey({ service: 'extra', key: x.key }));
    }
    for (const r of rows) {
      const o = parseCombo(r.comboKey);
      if (!keys.includes(r.comboKey) && o && (layer === 'provider' || !isProviderOnly(o.service))) keys.push(r.comboKey);
    }
    return keys;
  }
```

```ts
/**
 * v4.2/v4.3 provider price of a job (spec §40, §52–53, §66; D29) — pure. Every service maps to provider combos
 * (print lines as quoted, school per book / lined sheet / extra, theses per page + binding, flyers, cartridges, repairs);
 * what has no provider price is «قیمت مجری تنظیم نشده» (missing), never 0 and never another provider's price.
 */
import { parseCombo } from '../price-book/combo.js';
import { comboLabel, type LabelNames } from '../price-book/labels.js';
import type { ProviderEntry } from '../price-book/snapshot.js';
import { normalizeCart, normalizeDocs, normalizeFlyer, normalizeRepair } from '../pricing/pricing.js';
import type { ProviderPricingLine, ProviderPricingMissing, ProviderPricingSnapshot, ProviderQuote } from './job-model.js';

/** Customer-only surcharges of the print services: no provider line (R3). */
const CUSTOMER_ONLY = /^spiral_color\|/;

export interface PricedChild { name?: string; books?: number; lined?: boolean; linedCount?: number; extras?: string[] }
export interface PricedService { kind: string; label?: string; spec?: unknown; pricing?: { lines: { comboKey: string; quantity: number }[] } }
export interface PricedOrder { children?: PricedChild[]; services?: PricedService[] }
export interface NeededLine { comboKey: string; quantity: number }

/** Persian label of a snapshot line key (price-book combos via `comboLabel`). */
export function providerLineLabel(key: string, names: LabelNames): string {
  const o = parseCombo(key);
  if (o) return comboLabel(o, names);
  if (key.startsWith('extra|')) return 'خدمات اضافی';
  if (key.startsWith('spiral_color|')) return 'هزینه رنگ فنری';
  if (key === 'docs_print') return 'چاپ صفحات';
  return key;
}

/** What a provider does for one school child (D29): binding per book, lined sheets, the child's extras per book. */
export function childProviderLines(c: PricedChild): NeededLine[] {
  const books = c.books ?? 0;
  const out: NeededLine[] = [{ comboKey: 'school|book', quantity: books }];
  if (c.lined) out.push({ comboKey: 'school|lined', quantity: books * (c.linedCount ?? 10) });
  for (const k of c.extras ?? []) out.push({ comboKey: `extra|${k}`, quantity: books });
  return out;
}

/** The provider lines of one service line; null = the service has no provider combos (v3 «چاپ اسناد», unknown kinds). */
export function serviceProviderLines(s: PricedService): NeededLine[] | null {
  switch (s.kind) {
    case 'black_white_printing':
    case 'color_printing': {
      const lines = (s.pricing?.lines ?? []).filter((l) => !CUSTOMER_ONLY.test(l.comboKey));
      return lines.length ? lines.map((l) => ({ comboKey: l.comboKey, quantity: l.quantity })) : null;
    }
    case 'docs': {
      const d = normalizeDocs(s.spec as never);
      const out: NeededLine[] = [{ comboKey: `docs|${d.ink}|${d.sides}`, quantity: d.pagesN * d.copies }];
      if (d.binding === 'spiral') {
        const spiral = (s.pricing?.lines ?? []).filter((l) => l.comboKey.startsWith('spiral|'));
        if (!spiral.length) return null;
        out.push(...spiral.map((l) => ({ comboKey: l.comboKey, quantity: l.quantity })));
      } else out.push({ comboKey: 'docs|hardcover', quantity: d.copies });
      return out;
    }
    case 'flyer': {
      const f = normalizeFlyer(s.spec as never);
      const out: NeededLine[] = [{ comboKey: `flyer|${f.size}|${f.ink}|${f.paper}|${f.sides ?? 'single'}`, quantity: f.qty }];
      if (f.mode === 'need') out.push({ comboKey: 'flyer|design', quantity: 1 });
      return out;
    }
    case 'cart': {
      const c = normalizeCart(s.spec as never);
      return [{ comboKey: `cart|${c.cartType ?? 'laserBw'}`, quantity: c.count }];
    }
    case 'repair': {
      const r = normalizeRepair(s.spec as never);
      return [{ comboKey: `repair|${r.device ?? 'other'}`, quantity: 1 }];
    }
    default:
      return null;
  }
}

/** The provider's price of every line of the order from that provider's own effective entries. */
export function providerQuote(order: PricedOrder, entries: Record<string, ProviderEntry>, labelOf: (comboKey: string) => string): ProviderQuote {
  const lines: ProviderPricingLine[] = [];
  const missing: ProviderPricingMissing[] = [];
  const price = (need: NeededLine, at: { serviceIndex?: number; childIndex?: number }, childName: string | undefined, prefix: string) => {
    const usable = parseCombo(need.comboKey) ? entries[need.comboKey] : undefined;
    // a provider price that is not a finite number > 0 is no price at all (never a 0-cost line)
    const e = usable && Number.isFinite(usable.price) && usable.price > 0 ? usable : undefined;
    const qtyOk = Number.isInteger(need.quantity) && need.quantity >= 1;
    if (!e || !qtyOk) {
      missing.push({ ...at, comboKey: need.comboKey, label: `${prefix}: ${labelOf(need.comboKey)}${!qtyOk ? ' · تعداد نامعتبر' : ''}` });
      return;
    }
    lines.push({
      ...at, ...(childName !== undefined ? { childName } : {}),
      comboKey: need.comboKey, label: labelOf(need.comboKey), versionId: e.versionId, version: e.version,
      unitPrice: e.price, quantity: need.quantity, subtotal: e.price * need.quantity,
    });
  };
  (order.children ?? []).forEach((c, i) => {
    for (const need of childProviderLines(c)) price(need, { childIndex: i }, c.name ?? '', `فنری کتاب مدرسه — ${c.name || 'فرزند'}`);
  });
  (order.services ?? []).forEach((s, i) => {
    const needs = serviceProviderLines(s);
    if (!needs) {
      missing.push({ serviceIndex: i, comboKey: null, label: s.label || 'سرویس' });
      return;
    }
    for (const need of needs) price(need, { serviceIndex: i }, undefined, s.label || 'سرویس');
  });
  // an order with no lines at all cannot be priced: null, never 0
  if (!lines.length && !missing.length) missing.push({ comboKey: null, label: 'سفارش ردیف قابل قیمت‌گذاری ندارد' });
  return { lines, missing, subtotal: missing.length ? null : lines.reduce((t, l) => t + l.subtotal, 0), childCount: order.children?.length ?? 0 };
}

/** The assignment snapshot (contract v4.2 `ProviderPricingSnapshot` + v4.3 `childCount`). */
export function pricingSnapshot(input: {
  providerId: string; quote: ProviderQuote; at: Date; delivery: { providerId: string; fee: number } | null; confirmedMissing: boolean;
}): ProviderPricingSnapshot {
  const { providerId, quote, at, delivery, confirmedMissing } = input;
  const ownDelivery = delivery && delivery.providerId === providerId ? delivery.fee : 0;
  return {
    providerId, snapshotAt: at.toISOString(), currency: 'toman', lines: quote.lines, missing: quote.missing,
    providerSubtotal: quote.subtotal, delivery, payoutBasis: quote.subtotal === null ? null : quote.subtotal + ownDelivery, confirmedMissing,
    ...(quote.childCount !== undefined ? { childCount: quote.childCount } : {}),
  };
}
```
(The first existing test expects `q.lines[0]` to equal a service line without `childIndex`/`childName` — `price` adds those only for children.)

```ts
  async labelOf(): Promise<{ labelOf: (k: string) => string; paperNames: Map<string, string> }> {
    const [papers, ranges, extras] = await Promise.all([
      this.papers.find().lean(), this.ranges.find().lean(),
      this.extras ? this.extras.find({}, { key: 1, label: 1 }).lean() : Promise.resolve([] as { key: string; label: string }[]),
    ]);
    const paperNames = new Map(papers.map((p) => [p.key, p.name]));
    const names = {
      papers: paperNames, ranges: new Map(ranges.map((r) => [String(r._id), { minPages: r.minPages, maxPages: r.maxPages }])),
      extras: new Map(extras.map((x) => [x.key, x.label])),
    };
    return { labelOf: (k) => providerLineLabel(k, names), paperNames };
  }
```
Dispatch eligibility and assignment need no other change: `evaluate` / `eligibility` / `give` already call `providerQuote`, so a provider with D29 prices is priced and `confirmMissingPrice` is required only for what is still missing (D23 → D29).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `apps/api/src/modules/ledger/credits.ts`**

```ts
/**
 * v4.3 credit drafts of a delivered job (D26–D28, R5, R14) — pure. The production provider earns its assignment
 * snapshot's payout basis (school lines recomputed with the final book counts); a separate delivery provider earns its
 * fee. What the snapshot cannot price is a pending credit («نیازمند تعیین مبلغ»), never a guess.
 */
import type { ProviderPricingSnapshot } from '../dispatch/job-model.js';
import { childProviderLines, type PricedChild } from '../dispatch/provider-pricing.js';
import type { CreditBasis } from './ledger-entry.schema.js';
import { ledgerKey, type CreditRole } from './ledger-model.js';

export interface CreditJob {
  _id: unknown; orderId: unknown; orderCode: string; providerId?: unknown; deliveryProviderId?: unknown;
  deliveryMethod?: string; providerPricing?: ProviderPricingSnapshot | null; deliveredAt?: Date;
}
export interface CreditOrder { children?: PricedChild[] }
export interface CreditDraft { key: string; providerId: string; role: CreditRole; state: 'counted' | 'pending'; amount: number | null; basis: CreditBasis }

export const STALE_NOTE = 'تعداد کتاب‌ها پس از تخصیص تغییر کرده است';
export const NO_SNAPSHOT = 'قیمت مجری برای این سفارش ثبت نشده است';
export const NO_DELIVERY_FEE = 'هزینه ارسال مجری ثبت نشده است';

/** The final quantity of a school snapshot line for the order's child; null when the line no longer applies. */
export function childLineQuantity(comboKey: string, child: PricedChild | undefined): number | null {
  if (!child) return null;
  const need = childProviderLines(child).find((l) => l.comboKey === comboKey);
  return need && Number.isInteger(need.quantity) && need.quantity >= 1 ? need.quantity : null;
}

/** The payout basis at delivery: snapshot lines (child lines × the final counts) + the provider's own delivery fee. */
export function payoutFor(snap: ProviderPricingSnapshot, order: CreditOrder): { amount: number; stale: boolean } {
  const children = order.children ?? [];
  let stale = snap.childCount !== undefined && snap.childCount !== children.length;
  let total = 0;
  for (const l of snap.lines) {
    if (l.childIndex === undefined) {
      total += l.subtotal;
      continue;
    }
    const c = children[l.childIndex];
    const sameChild = !!c && (l.childName === undefined || (c.name ?? '') === l.childName);
    const q = sameChild ? childLineQuantity(l.comboKey, c) : null;
    if (q === null) {
      stale = true;
      total += l.subtotal;
      continue;
    }
    total += l.unitPrice * q;
  }
  // a line the child gained after assignment (lined sheets, an extra) has no provider price in the snapshot
  children.forEach((c, i) => {
    for (const need of childProviderLines(c)) if (!snap.lines.some((l) => l.childIndex === i && l.comboKey === need.comboKey)) stale = true;
  });
  const own = snap.delivery && snap.delivery.providerId === snap.providerId ? snap.delivery.fee : 0;
  return { amount: total + own, stale };
}

/** D27: the credits of a delivered job — one per (job, provider, role). */
export function creditDrafts(job: CreditJob, order: CreditOrder): CreditDraft[] {
  if (!job.providerId) return [];
  const pid = String(job.providerId);
  const snap = job.providerPricing && job.providerPricing.providerId === pid ? job.providerPricing : null;
  const production = { key: ledgerKey.credit(job._id, pid, 'production'), providerId: pid, role: 'production' as const };
  const out: CreditDraft[] = [];
  if (!snap) {
    out.push({ ...production, state: 'pending', amount: null, basis: { payoutBasis: null, suggested: null, missing: [NO_SNAPSHOT] } });
  } else {
    const basis = { payoutBasis: snap.payoutBasis, missing: snap.missing.map((m) => m.label), snapshotAt: snap.snapshotAt };
    if (snap.payoutBasis === null) {
      const own = snap.delivery && snap.delivery.providerId === pid ? snap.delivery.fee : 0;
      out.push({ ...production, state: 'pending', amount: null, basis: { ...basis, suggested: snap.lines.reduce((t, l) => t + l.subtotal, 0) + own } });
    } else {
      const { amount, stale } = payoutFor(snap, order);
      out.push(stale
        ? { ...production, state: 'pending', amount: null, basis: { ...basis, suggested: amount, note: STALE_NOTE } }
        : { ...production, state: 'counted', amount, basis: { ...basis, suggested: null } });
    }
  }
  const dp = job.deliveryProviderId ? String(job.deliveryProviderId) : null;
  if (job.deliveryMethod === 'provider' && dp && dp !== pid) {
    const delivery = { key: ledgerKey.credit(job._id, dp, 'delivery'), providerId: dp, role: 'delivery' as const };
    const fee = snap?.delivery && snap.delivery.providerId === dp ? snap.delivery.fee : null;
    if (fee === null) out.push({ ...delivery, state: 'pending', amount: null, basis: { payoutBasis: null, suggested: null, missing: [NO_DELIVERY_FEE] } });
    else if (fee > 0) out.push({ ...delivery, state: 'counted', amount: fee, basis: { payoutBasis: fee, suggested: null, missing: [] } });
  }
  return out;
}
```

```ts
  it('$group sums and maxes by a field (or null) and counts with $sum: 1', async () => {
    const m = fakeModel([{ p: 'a', n: 2, at: 1 }, { p: 'a', n: 3, at: 5 }, { p: 'b', n: 4, at: 2 }]);

    ]);
    expect(await m.aggregate([{ $match: { p: 'a' } }, { $group: { _id: null, total: { $sum: '$n' } } }])).toEqual([{ _id: null, total: 5 }]);

    await m.create({ key: 'c' });
    await expect(m.create({ key: 'a' })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 11000, keyPattern: { key: 1 } });
    await expect(m.updateOne({ key: 'd' }, { $setOnInsert: { slot: 's1' } }, { upsert: true })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 11000, keyPattern: { slot: 1 } });
    expect(await m.updateOne({ key: 'a' }, { $setOnInsert: { slot: 's9' } }, { upsert: true })).toMatchObject({ matchedCount: 1, upsertedCount: 0 });
    expect(m.docs).toHaveLength(3);
```

```ts
import { BadRequestException, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { fakeModel, type Doc } from '../../../test/fake-model.js';
import { LedgerService } from './ledger.service.js';

const P = new Types.ObjectId();
const NOW = new Date('2026-10-20T10:00:00Z'); // a Tuesday; the cutoff is Saturday 2026-10-17 00:00 Tehran
const CUTOFF = new Date('2026-10-16T20:30:00.000Z');

function setup(jobOver: Doc = {}, orderOver: Doc = {}) {
  const order = { _id: new Types.ObjectId(), code: '10421', status: 'delivered', paid: true, children: [], ...orderOver };
  const job = {
    _id: new Types.ObjectId(), orderId: order._id, orderCode: '10421', status: 'delivered', providerId: P, deliveryMethod: 'courier',
    deliveredAt: new Date('2026-10-19T09:00:00Z'), ledgerPending: true,
    providerPricing: {
      providerId: String(P), snapshotAt: '2026-10-18T08:00:00.000Z', currency: 'toman', missing: [], providerSubtotal: 60000, delivery: null,
      lines: [{ serviceIndex: 0, comboKey: 'bw|A4|single', label: 'x', versionId: 'v', version: 1, unitPrice: 3000, quantity: 20, subtotal: 60000 }],
      payoutBasis: 60000, confirmedMissing: false, childCount: 0,
    },
    ...jobOver,
  };
  const entries = fakeModel([], { unique: [['key'], ['slot']] });
  const accounts = fakeModel([]);
  const jobs = fakeModel([job]);
  const orders = fakeModel([order]);
  const catalog = { getSettings: vi.fn(async () => ({ ledger: { settlementWeekday: 6 } })) };
  const svc = new LedgerService(entries as never, accounts as never, jobs as never, orders as never, catalog as never);
  return { svc, entries, accounts, jobs, orders, job, order };
}
const counted = (amount: number, countedAt: Date, kind = 'credit', o: Doc = {}) => ({
  _id: new Types.ObjectId(), key: `k-${new Types.ObjectId()}`, providerId: P, kind, state: 'counted', amount, countedAt, occurredAt: countedAt, byRole: 'system', ...o,
});

describe('LedgerService.creditJob (D27)', () => {
  it('credits a delivered, paid job once, clears the flag and stamps ledgerAt', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    expect(await s.svc.creditJob(s.job._id)).toBe(1);
    expect(s.entries.docs[0]).toMatchObject({
      key: `credit:${String(s.job._id)}:${String(P)}:production`, kind: 'credit', state: 'counted', amount: 60000, role: 'production', orderCode: '10421', byRole: 'system',
    });
    expect(s.entries.docs[0].occurredAt).toEqual(s.job.deliveredAt);
    expect(s.entries.docs[0].countedAt).toBeInstanceOf(Date);
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0].ledgerPending).toBeUndefined();
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0].ledgerAt).toBeInstanceOf(Date);
    s.jobs.docs[0].ledgerPending = true; // a second run never credits twice
    expect(await s.svc.creditJob(s.job._id)).toBe(0);
    expect(s.entries.docs).toHaveLength(1);
  });
  it('concurrent runs (the hook and the tick) insert one entry', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const [a, b] = await Promise.all([s.svc.creditJob(s.job._id), s.svc.creditJob(s.job._id)]);
    expect(a + b).toBe(1);
    expect(s.entries.docs).toHaveLength(1);
  });
  it('an unpaid order waits with the flag kept; a job that is not flagged is skipped', async () => {
    const s = setup({}, { paid: false });
    expect(await s.svc.creditJob(s.job._id)).toBe(0);
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0].ledgerPending).toBe(true);
    s.orders.docs[0].paid = true;
    expect(await s.svc.reconcile()).toBe(1);
    const t = setup({ ledgerPending: undefined });
    expect(await t.svc.creditJob(t.job._id)).toBe(0);
    expect(t.entries.docs).toHaveLength(0);
  });
  it('a snapshot-less (migrated) job gets a pending credit that does not count (D28)', async () => {
    const s = setup({ providerPricing: undefined });
    await s.svc.creditJob(s.job._id);
    expect(s.entries.docs[0]).toMatchObject({ state: 'pending', amount: null });
    expect(s.entries.docs[0].countedAt).toBeUndefined();
    expect(await s.svc.balanceOf(String(P), CUTOFF)).toEqual({ available: 0, earnedSinceCutoff: 0, due: 0, pendingCount: 1 });
  });
  it('never throws: a failing write is logged, the flag stays and reconcile credits it', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    vi.spyOn(Logger.prototype, 'error').mockImplementation(() => undefined);
    s.entries.updateOne.mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('mongo down'));
    expect(await s.svc.creditJob(s.job._id)).toBe(0);
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0].ledgerPending).toBe(true);
    expect(await s.svc.reconcile()).toBe(1);
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0].ledgerPending).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

describe('LedgerService balances (D31, R10)', () => {
  it('available = the counted sum; due leaves out what was earned since the cutoff; payouts count whenever made', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    s.entries.docs.push(
      counted(100000, new Date('2026-10-14T10:00:00Z')),
      counted(40000, new Date('2026-10-18T10:00:00Z')), // after the cutoff: not due this week
      counted(-30000, new Date('2026-10-19T10:00:00Z'), 'payout'),
      counted(-5000, new Date('2026-10-15T10:00:00Z'), 'adjustment'),
      { _id: new Types.ObjectId(), key: 'p', providerId: P, kind: 'credit', state: 'pending', amount: null, occurredAt: NOW, byRole: 'system' },
    );
    expect(await s.svc.balanceOf(String(P), CUTOFF)).toEqual({ available: 105000, earnedSinceCutoff: 40000, due: 65000, pendingCount: 1 });
    expect((await s.svc.balances(undefined, CUTOFF)).get(String(P))?.available).toBe(105000);
    expect(await s.svc.balanceOf(String(new Types.ObjectId()), CUTOFF)).toEqual({ available: 0, earnedSinceCutoff: 0, due: 0, pendingCount: 0 });
  });
});

describe('LedgerService.withSlot (R1)', () => {
  const take = (amount: number) => (b: { available: number }) => {
    if (amount > b.available) throw new BadRequestException('مبلغ پرداخت از مانده حساب مجری بیشتر است');
    return { providerId: P, kind: 'payout' as const, state: 'counted' as const, amount: -amount, occurredAt: NOW, countedAt: NOW, byRole: 'admin' as const };
  };
  it('two concurrent writers on one balance: one wins, the other re-checks the fresh balance and is refused', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    s.entries.docs.push(counted(100000, new Date('2026-10-14T10:00:00Z')));
    const res = await Promise.allSettled([s.svc.withSlot(String(P), 'payout:a', take(80000)), s.svc.withSlot(String(P), 'payout:b', take(80000))]);
    expect(res.map((r) => r.status).sort()).toEqual(['fulfilled', 'rejected']);
    expect((res.find((r) => r.status === 'rejected') as PromiseRejectedResult).reason.message).toBe('مبلغ پرداخت از مانده حساب مجری بیشتر است');
    expect((await s.svc.balanceOf(String(P), CUTOFF)).available).toBe(20000);
    expect(s.accounts.docs[0].rev).toBe(1);
  });
  it('a slot left behind by a crash (entry written, rev not advanced) is repaired by the next write', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    s.entries.docs.push(counted(100000, new Date('2026-10-14T10:00:00Z'), 'adjustment', { slot: `${String(P)}:0` }));
    s.accounts.docs.push({ _id: P, rev: 0 });
    const r = await s.svc.withSlot(String(P), 'payout:c', take(50000));
    expect(r.created).toBe(true);
    expect(r.entry.slot).toBe(`${String(P)}:1`);
    expect(s.accounts.docs[0].rev).toBe(2);
  });
  it('the same key twice returns the existing entry (an idempotent request)', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    s.entries.docs.push(counted(100000, new Date('2026-10-14T10:00:00Z')));
    const build = vi.fn(() => ({ providerId: P, kind: 'payout' as const, state: 'counted' as const, amount: -1000, occurredAt: NOW, countedAt: NOW, byRole: 'admin' as const }));
    const a = await s.svc.withSlot(String(P), 'payout:req-1', build);
    const b = await s.svc.withSlot(String(P), 'payout:req-1', build);
    expect(build).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1); // the retry finds its key before re-checking the balance
    expect([a.created, b.created]).toEqual([true, false]);
    expect(String(b.entry._id)).toBe(String(a.entry._id));
    expect(s.entries.docs.filter((d) => d.kind === 'payout')).toHaveLength(1);
  });
});
```

Add the `$group` stage — in `runPipeline`, before the final `else throw`:
```ts
    else if (op === '$group') rows = groupStage(rows, arg);
```
and above `runPipeline`:
```ts
/** `$group` with `_id: '$field' | null` and `$sum` (`'$field'` or a constant) / `$max` accumulators. */
function groupStage(rows: Doc[], spec: Doc): Doc[] {
  const { _id: idSpec, ...acc } = spec;
  const pathOf = (e: unknown) => (typeof e === 'string' && e.startsWith('$') ? e.slice(1) : null);
  const groups = new Map<string, { id: unknown; rows: Doc[] }>();
  for (const r of rows) {
    const p = pathOf(idSpec);
    const id = p ? (get(r, p) ?? null) : (idSpec ?? null);
    const k = String(norm(id));
    if (!groups.has(k)) groups.set(k, { id, rows: [] });
    groups.get(k)!.rows.push(r);
  }
  return [...groups.values()].map(({ id, rows: rs }) => {
    const out: Doc = { _id: id };
    for (const [name, a] of Object.entries(acc as Record<string, Doc>)) {
      const [fn, expr] = Object.entries(a)[0];
      const p = pathOf(expr);
      const val = (r: Doc) => (p ? get(r, p) : expr);
      if (fn === '$sum') out[name] = rs.reduce((t, r) => t + (typeof val(r) === 'number' ? (val(r) as number) : 0), 0);
      else if (fn === '$max') out[name] = rs.map(val).filter((v) => v != null).reduce<unknown>((m, v) => (m == null || (norm(v) as number) > (norm(m) as number) ? v : m), null);
      else throw new Error(`fake-model: unsupported accumulator ${fn}`);
    }
    return out;
  });
}
```

Opt-in unique indexes — change the signature and add the check:
```ts
export interface FakeOptions {
  /** Unique indexes as field lists; a document missing a field is not indexed (like a partial index). */
  unique?: string[][];
}

export function fakeModel(docs: Doc[] = [], opts: FakeOptions = {}) {
  const joins: Record<string, { docs: Doc[] }> = {};
  const assertUnique = (d: Doc) => {
    const fields = (opts.unique ?? []).find((fs) => fs.every((f) => get(d, f) != null) && docs.some((x) => x !== d && fs.every((f) => eq(get(x, f), get(d, f)))));
    if (fields) throw Object.assign(new Error(`E11000 duplicate key error (${fields.join(', ')})`), { code: 11000, keyPattern: Object.fromEntries(fields.map((f) => [f, 1])) });
  };
```
`insertFromUpsert` builds the document, applies the update, checks, **then** pushes:
```ts
  const insertFromUpsert = (filter: Doc, update: Doc) => {
    const now = new Date();
    const d: Doc = { _id: new Types.ObjectId(), createdAt: now, updatedAt: now, ...clone(equalityFields(filter)) };
    applyUpdate(d, { ...update, $set: { ...((update.$setOnInsert as Doc) ?? {}), ...((update.$set as Doc) ?? {}) } });
    assertUnique(d);
    docs.push(d);
    return d;
  };
```
and `create`:
```ts
  const create = async (doc: Doc) => {
    const now = new Date();
    const d = { _id: new Types.ObjectId(), createdAt: now, updatedAt: now, ...clone(doc) };
    assertUnique(d);
    docs.push(d);
    return out(d);
  };
```
