- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `apps/api/src/modules/ledger/ledger.service.ts`**

```ts
import { ConflictException, Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
import { InjectModel } from '@nestjs/mongoose';
import { Model, Types } from 'mongoose';
import { CatalogService } from '../catalog/catalog.service.js';
import { Job } from '../dispatch/job.schema.js';
import { Order } from '../orders/order.schema.js';
import { creditDrafts, type CreditJob, type CreditOrder } from './credits.js';
import { LedgerAccount } from './ledger-account.schema.js';
import { LedgerEntry, type LedgerEntryDocument } from './ledger-entry.schema.js';
import { settlementCutoff, slotOf } from './ledger-model.js';

export interface Balance { available: number; earnedSinceCutoff: number; due: number; pendingCount: number }
export const EMPTY_BALANCE: Balance = { available: 0, earnedSinceCutoff: 0, due: 0, pendingCount: 0 };
/** A ledger entry to insert (`slot` is added by `withSlot`). */
export type NewEntry = Omit<LedgerEntry, 'createdAt' | 'slot'>;
export const ledgerRaced = () => new ConflictException('حساب مجری هم‌زمان تغییر کرد؛ دوباره تلاش کنید');

const CREDITABLE = ['delivered', 'completed'];
const dupOn = (err: unknown, field: string) =>
  (err as { code?: number }).code === 11000 && !!(err as { keyPattern?: Record<string, unknown> }).keyPattern?.[field];

/**
 * v4.3 provider ledger — the single write path (D26–D33). Every entry is a keyed upsert (`$setOnInsert` on the unique
 * `key`): repeating a write never adds money. Admin entries take the provider's slot (R1) so concurrent writers re-check
 * the balance. Balances are derived from the entries, never stored. No multi-document transactions are needed.
 */
@Injectable()
export class LedgerService {
  private readonly logger = new Logger('Ledger');

  constructor(
    @InjectModel(LedgerEntry.name) private readonly entries: Model<LedgerEntry>,
    @InjectModel(LedgerAccount.name) private readonly accounts: Model<LedgerAccount>,
    @InjectModel(Job.name) private readonly jobs: Model<Job>,
    @InjectModel(Order.name) private readonly orders: Model<Order>,
    private readonly catalog: CatalogService,
  ) {}

  async weekday(): Promise<number> {
    return (await this.catalog.getSettings()).ledger.settlementWeekday;
  }

  /** D31: the current weekly cutoff (Tehran midnight of the latest settlement day). */
  async cutoff(now = new Date()): Promise<Date> {
    return settlementCutoff(now, await this.weekday());
  }

  /** Keyed insert: the new entry, or the existing one with the same key (`created: false`). A slot conflict is rethrown. */
  async insertOnce(doc: NewEntry & { slot?: string }): Promise<{ entry: LedgerEntryDocument; created: boolean }> {
    const { key, ...rest } = doc;
    try {
      const res = await this.entries.updateOne({ key }, { $setOnInsert: rest }, { upsert: true });
      return { entry: (await this.entries.findOne({ key }))!, created: (res.upsertedCount ?? 0) > 0 };
    } catch (err) {
      if (!dupOn(err, 'key')) throw err;
      return { entry: (await this.entries.findOne({ key }))!, created: false };
    }
  }

  /**
   * R1: an admin entry computed on the provider's current version. A request whose key already exists returns that entry
   * (`created: false`) before anything is re-checked, so a retried or double-submitted request is idempotent. Otherwise
   * the entry stores the unique slot `<provider>:<rev>` and `rev` advances after it. The loser of a slot advances `rev`
   * itself (which also repairs a slot left by a crash) and re-runs `build` on the fresh balance; `build` throws to refuse
   * (e.g. a payout above the balance).
   */
  async withSlot(providerId: string, key: string, build: (b: Balance) => Omit<NewEntry, 'key'>): Promise<{ entry: LedgerEntryDocument; created: boolean }> {
    const pid = new Types.ObjectId(providerId);
    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt++) {
      const existing = await this.entries.findOne({ key });
      if (existing) return { entry: existing, created: false };
      const acc = await this.accounts.findOneAndUpdate({ _id: pid }, { $setOnInsert: { rev: 0 } }, { upsert: true, returnDocument: 'after' });
      const rev = acc?.rev ?? 0;
      // the balance is read after the version: every entry of an older slot is already in it
      const doc = build(await this.balanceOf(providerId));
      try {
        const res = await this.insertOnce({ ...doc, key, slot: slotOf(providerId, rev) });
        if (res.created) await this.accounts.updateOne({ _id: pid, rev }, { $inc: { rev: 1 } });
        return res;
      } catch (err) {
        if (!dupOn(err, 'slot')) throw err;
        await this.accounts.updateOne({ _id: pid, rev }, { $inc: { rev: 1 } });
      }
    }
    throw ledgerRaced();
  }

  async balanceOf(providerId: string, cutoff?: Date): Promise<Balance> {
    return (await this.balances([providerId], cutoff)).get(providerId) ?? { ...EMPTY_BALANCE };
  }

  /** Balances of the given providers (all when omitted): three grouped reads over the entries. */
  async balances(providerIds?: string[], cutoff?: Date): Promise<Map<string, Balance>> {
    const at = cutoff ?? (await this.cutoff());
    const scope = providerIds ? { providerId: { $in: providerIds.map((p) => new Types.ObjectId(p)) } } : {};
    const sum = (match: Record<string, unknown>) =>
      this.entries.aggregate<{ _id: Types.ObjectId; v: number }>([{ $match: { ...scope, ...match } }, { $group: { _id: '$providerId', v: { $sum: '$amount' } } }]);
    const [all, recent, pending] = await Promise.all([
      sum({ state: 'counted' }),
      sum({ state: 'counted', kind: { $in: ['credit', 'adjustment'] }, countedAt: { $gte: at } }),
      this.entries.aggregate<{ _id: Types.ObjectId; v: number }>([{ $match: { ...scope, state: 'pending' } }, { $group: { _id: '$providerId', v: { $sum: 1 } } }]),
    ]);
    const out = new Map<string, Balance>();
    const row = (id: unknown) => {
      const k = String(id);
      if (!out.has(k)) out.set(k, { ...EMPTY_BALANCE });
      return out.get(k)!;
    };
    for (const r of all) row(r._id).available = r.v;
    for (const r of recent) row(r._id).earnedSinceCutoff = r.v;
    for (const r of pending) row(r._id).pendingCount = r.v;
    for (const b of out.values()) b.due = Math.max(0, b.available - b.earnedSinceCutoff);
    return out;
  }

  /**
   * D27: the credits of a delivered job (production + a separate delivery provider), once the order is paid. Idempotent
   * by key; clears `Job.ledgerPending` and stamps `ledgerAt` (a bookkeeping write, not a status change). Never throws:
   * the job is already delivered and the tick retries a flagged job.
   */
  async creditJob(jobId: unknown): Promise<number> {
    try {
      const job = await this.jobs.findById(jobId).lean();
      if (!job?.ledgerPending) return 0;
      const now = new Date();
      let created = 0;
      if (CREDITABLE.includes(job.status)) {
        const order = await this.orders.findById(job.orderId, { paid: 1, children: 1 }).lean();
        if (!order?.paid) return 0; // COD is marked paid in the delivered update itself; anything else waits
        for (const d of creditDrafts(job as unknown as CreditJob, order as unknown as CreditOrder)) {
          const r = await this.insertOnce({
            key: d.key, providerId: new Types.ObjectId(d.providerId), kind: 'credit', state: d.state, amount: d.amount, role: d.role,
            jobId: job._id, orderId: job.orderId, orderCode: job.orderCode, occurredAt: job.deliveredAt ?? now,
            ...(d.state === 'counted' ? { countedAt: now } : {}), basis: d.basis, byRole: 'system',
          });
          if (r.created) created++;
        }
      }
      await this.jobs.updateOne({ _id: job._id, ledgerPending: true }, { $unset: { ledgerPending: 1 }, $set: { ledgerAt: now } });
      return created;
    } catch (err) {
      this.logger.error(`ledger credit of job ${String(jobId)} failed (the tick retries): ${(err as Error).message}`);
      return 0;
    }
  }

  /** Safety net (dispatch tick): every job still flagged `ledgerPending`, oldest first, one bounded page. Never throws. */
  async reconcile(limit = 200): Promise<number> {
    try {
      const rows = await this.jobs.find({ ledgerPending: true }, { _id: 1 }).sort({ _id: 1 }).limit(limit).lean();
      let n = 0;
      for (const j of rows) n += await this.creditJob(j._id);
      return n;
    } catch (err) {
      this.logger.warn(`ledger reconcile failed: ${(err as Error).message}`);
      return 0;
    }
  }
}
```

```ts
describe('v4.3 ledger hook (D27, Review Focus 2)', () => {
  const snapshot = {
    providerId: String(P), snapshotAt: '2026-10-16T08:00:00.000Z', currency: 'toman', missing: [], providerSubtotal: 60000,
    lines: [{ serviceIndex: 0, comboKey: 'bw|A4|single', label: 'x', versionId: 'v', version: 1, unitPrice: 3000, quantity: 20, subtotal: 60000 }],
    delivery: { providerId: String(P), fee: 30000 }, payoutBasis: 90000, confirmedMissing: false, childCount: 0,
  };
  function withLedger(orderOver: Doc = {}) {
    const s = setup(orderOver);
    const entries = fakeModel([], { unique: [['key'], ['slot']] });
    const catalog = { getSettings: vi.fn(async () => ({ dispatch: { ...DEFAULT_DISPATCH }, ledger: { settlementWeekday: 6 } })) };
    const ledger = new LedgerService(entries as never, fakeModel([]) as never, s.jobs as never, s.orders as never, catalog as never);
    const svc = new JobsService(s.jobs as never, s.orders as never, catalog as never, s.notifier as never, undefined, ledger);
    return { ...s, svc, ledger, entries };
  }
  async function providerDelivers(s: ReturnType<typeof withLedger>) {
    const id = await heldBy(s, 'out_for_delivery');
    Object.assign(s.jobs.docs[0], { deliveryMethod: 'provider', deliveryProviderId: P, providerPricing: snapshot });
    await s.svc.move(id, 'delivered', { actor: 'delivery', byRole: 'provider', providerId: String(P) });
    return id;
  }

  it('provider delivery credits the producer from its snapshot exactly once, the COD payment included in the same delivery', async () => {
    const s = withLedger({ status: 'packing' });
    const id = await providerDelivers(s);
    expect(s.orders.docs[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'delivered', paid: true });
    expect(s.entries.docs).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(s.entries.docs[0]).toMatchObject({ key: `credit:${id}:${String(P)}:production`, kind: 'credit', state: 'counted', amount: 90000, orderCode: '10400' });
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0].ledgerPending).toBeUndefined();
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0].ledgerAt).toBeInstanceOf(Date);
    s.jobs.docs[0].ledgerPending = true; // the hook and the tick never credit twice
    expect(await s.ledger.reconcile()).toBe(0);
    expect(s.entries.docs).toHaveLength(1);
  });
  it('a failed ledger write is retried by the tick: the flag was set by the delivery write itself', async () => {
    const s = withLedger({ status: 'packing' });
    vi.spyOn(Logger.prototype, 'error').mockImplementation(() => undefined);
    s.entries.updateOne.mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('mongo down'));
    await providerDelivers(s); // never throws into the job flow
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'delivered', ledgerPending: true });
    expect(s.entries.docs).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(await s.ledger.reconcile()).toBe(1);
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0].ledgerPending).toBeUndefined();
  });
  it('the order-level delivery (courier / admin) flags the job in the same write and credits it', async () => {
    const s = withLedger({ status: 'out_for_delivery', paid: true });
    await heldBy(s, 'out_for_delivery');
    Object.assign(s.jobs.docs[0], { providerPricing: { ...snapshot, delivery: null, payoutBasis: 60000 } });
    expect(await s.svc.onOrderDelivered(s.order._id, 'admin')).toBe(true);
    const update = s.jobs.findOneAndUpdate.mock.calls.at(-1)![1] as { $set: Record<string, unknown> };
    expect(update.$set).toMatchObject({ status: 'delivered', ledgerPending: true });
    expect(s.entries.docs[0]).toMatchObject({ amount: 60000, state: 'counted' });
  });
  it('without a ledger (older wiring / tests) delivery still works and leaves the flag for the tick', async () => {
    const s = setup({ status: 'out_for_delivery', paid: true });
    await heldBy(s, 'out_for_delivery');
    expect(await s.svc.onOrderDelivered(s.order._id, 'admin')).toBe(true);
    expect(s.jobs.docs[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'delivered', ledgerPending: true });
  });
});
```

```ts
import { NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { fakeModel, type Doc } from '../../../test/fake-model.js';
import { LedgerReadsService } from './ledger-reads.service.js';
import { LedgerService } from './ledger.service.js';

const A = new Types.ObjectId();
const B = new Types.ObjectId();
const ADMIN = new Types.ObjectId();
const NOW = new Date('2026-10-20T10:00:00Z'); // Tuesday — cutoff Saturday 2026-10-17, next 2026-10-24
const at = (iso: string) => new Date(iso);
const entry = (providerId: Types.ObjectId, kind: string, amount: number | null, countedAt: string | null, o: Doc = {}) => ({
  _id: new Types.ObjectId(), key: `k-${new Types.ObjectId()}`, providerId, kind, state: countedAt ? 'counted' : 'pending', amount,
  ...(countedAt ? { countedAt: at(countedAt) } : {}), occurredAt: at(countedAt ?? '2026-10-19T10:00:00Z'), createdAt: at(countedAt ?? '2026-10-19T10:00:00Z'),
  byRole: kind === 'credit' ? 'system' : 'admin', ...o,
});

function setup(docs: Doc[] = []) {
  const entries = fakeModel(docs, { unique: [['key'], ['slot']] });
  const centers = fakeModel([
    { _id: A, name: 'چاپ نگین', businessName: 'چاپ و تکثیر نگین', status: 'active', bankAccount: { sheba: 'IR062960000000100324200001', holder: 'علی نگینی', bankName: 'نمونه', updatedAt: at('2026-10-01T08:00:00Z') } },
    { _id: B, name: 'دیجیتال آرت', status: 'busy' },
  ]);
  const users = fakeModel([{ _id: ADMIN, name: 'مدیر مالی', role: 'admin' }]);
  const catalog = { getSettings: vi.fn(async () => ({ ledger: { settlementWeekday: 6 } })) };
  const ledger = new LedgerService(entries as never, fakeModel([]) as never, fakeModel([]) as never, fakeModel([]) as never, catalog as never);
  const svc = new LedgerReadsService(entries as never, centers as never, users as never, ledger);
  return { svc, entries };
}

describe('LedgerReadsService (admin)', () => {
  beforeEach(() => vi.useFakeTimers({ now: NOW, toFake: ['Date'] }));
  afterEach(() => vi.useRealTimers());

  it('balances: every provider with its balance, highest due first; bank flag, last payout and a search', async () => {
    const pay = entry(A, 'payout', -30000, '2026-10-19T10:00:00Z', { payout: { paidAt: '2026-10-19', bankRef: 'T1' } });
    const { svc } = setup([
      entry(A, 'credit', 100000, '2026-10-14T10:00:00Z'), entry(A, 'credit', 40000, '2026-10-18T10:00:00Z'), pay,
      entry(B, 'credit', 200000, '2026-10-15T10:00:00Z'), entry(B, 'credit', null, null),
    ]);
    const res = await svc.balances({});
    expect(res.total).toBe(2);
    expect(res.items.map((r) => [r.name, r.balance.available, r.balance.due, r.balance.pendingCount, r.hasBank])).toEqual([
      ['دیجیتال آرت', 200000, 200000, 1, false], ['چاپ نگین', 110000, 70000, 0, true],
    ]);
    expect(res.items[1].lastPayoutAt).toBe(pay.occurredAt.toISOString());
    expect((await svc.balances({ q: 'نگین' })).items.map((r) => r.name)).toEqual(['چاپ نگین']);
  });
  it('provider: balance, full bank account and the settlement calendar; an unknown provider is 404', async () => {
    const { svc } = setup([entry(A, 'credit', 100000, '2026-10-14T10:00:00Z')]);
    expect(await svc.provider(String(A))).toEqual({
      provider: { id: String(A), name: 'چاپ نگین', businessName: 'چاپ و تکثیر نگین', status: 'active' },
      balance: { available: 100000, earnedSinceCutoff: 0, due: 100000, pendingCount: 0 },
      bank: { sheba: 'IR062960000000100324200001', holder: 'علی نگینی', bankName: 'نمونه', updatedAt: '2026-10-01T08:00:00.000Z' },
      settlement: { weekday: 6, cutoff: '2026-10-17', next: '2026-10-24' },
    });
    await expect(svc.provider(String(new Types.ObjectId()))).rejects.toThrow(new NotFoundException('مجری یافت نشد'));
    await expect(svc.provider('bad')).rejects.toThrow(NotFoundException);
  });
  it('entries: newest first, kind and date filters, void flags and author names', async () => {
    const pay = entry(A, 'payout', -30000, '2026-10-19T10:00:00Z', { createdBy: ADMIN, payout: { paidAt: '2026-10-19', bankRef: 'T1' } });
    const { svc } = setup([
      entry(A, 'credit', 100000, '2026-10-14T10:00:00Z', { orderCode: '10421', role: 'production' }), pay,
      entry(A, 'payout_void', 30000, '2026-10-19T12:00:00Z', { voidOf: pay._id, createdBy: ADMIN }), entry(B, 'credit', 5, '2026-10-14T10:00:00Z'),
    ]);
    const all = await svc.entries(String(A), {});
    expect(all.items.map((e) => [e.kind, e.title, e.voided])).toEqual([['payout_void', 'ابطال پرداخت', false], ['payout', 'پرداخت تسویه', true], ['credit', 'سفارش ۱۰۴۲۱', false]]);
    expect(all.items[1].createdBy).toEqual({ id: String(ADMIN), name: 'مدیر مالی' });
    expect((await svc.entries(String(A), { kind: 'credit' })).items).toHaveLength(1);
    expect((await svc.entries(String(A), { from: '2026-10-19', to: '2026-10-19' })).items.map((e) => e.kind)).toEqual(['payout_void', 'payout']);
  });
  it('weekly statement: opening, the week’s counted lines, earned, paid and closing (D31)', async () => {
    const { svc } = setup([
      entry(A, 'credit', 100000, '2026-10-08T10:00:00Z'), // before the week
      entry(A, 'credit', 50000, '2026-10-12T10:00:00Z'),
      entry(A, 'adjustment', -5000, '2026-10-13T10:00:00Z'),
      entry(A, 'payout', -80000, '2026-10-15T10:00:00Z'),
      entry(A, 'credit', 40000, '2026-10-18T10:00:00Z'), // the current week
      entry(A, 'credit', null, null),
    ]);
    const s = await svc.statement(String(A));
    expect(s).toMatchObject({ providerId: String(A), from: '2026-10-10', to: '2026-10-16', opening: 100000, earned: 45000, paid: 80000, closing: 65000 });
    expect(s.lines.map((l) => l.amount)).toEqual([50000, -5000, -80000]);
    expect(await svc.statement(String(A), '2026-10-24')).toMatchObject({ from: '2026-10-17', to: '2026-10-23', opening: 65000, earned: 40000, paid: 0, closing: 105000 });
  });
  it('pending: only pending credits, oldest first, with the provider name', async () => {
    const { svc } = setup([
      entry(B, 'credit', null, null, { createdAt: at('2026-10-19T09:00:00Z'), basis: { payoutBasis: null, suggested: 1000, missing: ['چاپ اسناد'] } }),
      entry(A, 'credit', null, null, { createdAt: at('2026-10-18T09:00:00Z') }),
      entry(A, 'credit', 1, '2026-10-18T09:00:00Z'),
    ]);
    const res = await svc.pending({});
    expect(res.items.map((e) => [e.providerName, e.state])).toEqual([['چاپ نگین', 'pending'], ['دیجیتال آرت', 'pending']]);
    expect(res.items[1].basis).toEqual({ payoutBasis: null, suggested: 1000, missing: ['چاپ اسناد'] });
  });
});
```

```ts
import { PERMISSIONS_KEY, ROLES_KEY } from '../../common/auth/decorators.js';
import { PermissionsGuard } from '../../common/auth/permissions.guard.js';
import { AdminLedgerController } from './ledger.controller.js';

/** D33: every admin ledger route names its permission (grows with Tasks 10–12). */
export const ADMIN_LEDGER_PERMISSIONS: Record<string, string[]> = {
  balances: ['ledger_view'], provider: ['ledger_view'], entries: ['ledger_view'], statement: ['ledger_view'], pending: ['ledger_view'],
};

describe('AdminLedgerController (D33)', () => {
  it('is admin-only behind the PermissionsGuard, and every route names its permission', () => {
    expect(Reflect.getMetadata(ROLES_KEY, AdminLedgerController)).toEqual(['admin']);
    expect(Reflect.getMetadata('__guards__', AdminLedgerController)).toContain(PermissionsGuard); // Nest's GUARDS_METADATA
    const proto = AdminLedgerController.prototype as unknown as Record<string, object>;
    for (const [m, p] of Object.entries(ADMIN_LEDGER_PERMISSIONS)) expect(Reflect.getMetadata(PERMISSIONS_KEY, proto[m]), m).toEqual(p);
    const routes = Object.getOwnPropertyNames(AdminLedgerController.prototype).filter((m) => m !== 'constructor');
    expect(routes.sort()).toEqual(Object.keys(ADMIN_LEDGER_PERMISSIONS).sort()); // no route without a permission
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `apps/api/src/modules/ledger/ledger-json.ts`**

```ts
import type { Types } from 'mongoose';
import type { CreditBasis, LedgerEntry, PayoutInfo } from './ledger-entry.schema.js';
import { entryTitle, type AdjustmentCause, type CompCause, type CreditRole, type LedgerKind, type LedgerState } from './ledger-model.js';

export type EntryRow = LedgerEntry & { _id: Types.ObjectId };

/** Contract v4.3 `LedgerEntryView` (admin only). */
export interface LedgerEntryView {
  id: string; kind: LedgerKind; state: LedgerState; amount: number | null; title: string; providerId: string; providerName?: string;
  orderId?: string; orderCode?: string; jobId?: string; role?: CreditRole; cause?: AdjustmentCause; compCause?: CompCause;
  occurredAt: string; countedAt?: string; reason?: string; basis?: CreditBasis; payout?: PayoutInfo; voidOf?: string; voided: boolean;
  createdBy?: { id: string; name: string }; byRole: 'system' | 'admin';
  resolution?: { by: { id: string; name: string } | null; at: string; reason: string }; createdAt: string;
}
export interface ViewCtx { voided: Set<string>; users: Map<string, string>; providers?: Map<string, string> }

const iso = (d: Date | string) => new Date(d).toISOString();
const who = (id: unknown, users: Map<string, string>) => ({ id: String(id), name: users.get(String(id)) ?? '' });

export function entryView(e: EntryRow, ctx: ViewCtx): LedgerEntryView {
  const name = ctx.providers?.get(String(e.providerId));
  return {
    id: String(e._id), kind: e.kind, state: e.state, amount: e.amount ?? null, title: entryTitle(e), providerId: String(e.providerId),
    ...(name ? { providerName: name } : {}),
    ...(e.orderId ? { orderId: String(e.orderId) } : {}), ...(e.orderCode ? { orderCode: e.orderCode } : {}), ...(e.jobId ? { jobId: String(e.jobId) } : {}),
    ...(e.role ? { role: e.role } : {}), ...(e.cause ? { cause: e.cause } : {}), ...(e.compCause ? { compCause: e.compCause } : {}),
    occurredAt: iso(e.occurredAt), ...(e.countedAt ? { countedAt: iso(e.countedAt) } : {}), ...(e.reason ? { reason: e.reason } : {}),
    ...(e.basis ? { basis: e.basis } : {}), ...(e.payout ? { payout: e.payout } : {}), ...(e.voidOf ? { voidOf: String(e.voidOf) } : {}),
    voided: ctx.voided.has(String(e._id)),
    ...(e.createdBy ? { createdBy: who(e.createdBy, ctx.users) } : {}), byRole: e.byRole,
    ...(e.resolution ? { resolution: { by: e.resolution.by ? who(e.resolution.by, ctx.users) : null, at: iso(e.resolution.at), reason: e.resolution.reason } } : {}),
    createdAt: iso(e.createdAt),
  };
}

/** The provider's own statement line (D32, R8): never reasons, notes, bases, authors or other providers. */
export interface ProviderLedgerLine { id: string; kind: LedgerKind; state: LedgerState; title: string; orderCode?: string; at: string; amount: number | null; bankRef?: string; voided?: boolean }

export function providerLine(e: EntryRow, voided: Set<string>): ProviderLedgerLine {
  return {
    id: String(e._id), kind: e.kind, state: e.state, title: entryTitle(e), ...(e.orderCode ? { orderCode: e.orderCode } : {}),
    at: iso(e.occurredAt), amount: e.amount ?? null,
    ...(e.kind === 'payout' ? { voided: voided.has(String(e._id)), ...(e.payout?.bankRef ? { bankRef: e.payout.bankRef } : {}) } : {}),
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `apps/api/src/modules/ledger/dto/ledger.dto.ts`** (all ledger request bodies; later tasks only import them)

```ts
import { Transform, Type } from 'class-transformer';
import { IsIn, IsInt, IsMongoId, IsOptional, IsString, IsUUID, Matches, Max, MaxLength, Min, MinLength } from 'class-validator';
import { PageQueryDto } from '../../../common/utils/pagination.js';
import { LEDGER_KINDS, MAX_AMOUNT, type LedgerKind } from '../ledger-model.js';

const YMD = /^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])-(0[1-9]|[12]\d|3[01])$/;
const ymdMsg = { message: 'تاریخ باید به‌صورت yyyy-mm-dd باشد' };
const amountMsg = { message: 'مبلغ باید عدد صحیح (تومان) باشد' };
const reasonMsg = { message: 'دلیل را بنویسید' };
const requestMsg = { message: 'شناسه درخواست معتبر نیست' };
const holderMsg = { message: 'نام صاحب حساب را وارد کنید' };
const bankRefMsg = { message: 'شماره پیگیری بانکی را وارد کنید' };
const trim = ({ value }: { value: unknown }) => (typeof value === 'string' ? value.trim() : value);

export class LedgerEntriesQueryDto extends PageQueryDto {
  @IsOptional() @IsIn(LEDGER_KINDS) kind?: LedgerKind;
  @IsOptional() @Matches(YMD, ymdMsg) from?: string;
  @IsOptional() @Matches(YMD, ymdMsg) to?: string;
}
export class StatementQueryDto {
  @IsOptional() @Matches(YMD, ymdMsg) cutoff?: string;
}
export class ResolvePendingDto {
  @Type(() => Number) @IsInt(amountMsg) @Min(0, amountMsg) @Max(MAX_AMOUNT, amountMsg) amount: number;
  @Transform(trim) @IsString(reasonMsg) @MinLength(1, reasonMsg) @MaxLength(300) reason: string;
}
export class CompensationDto {
  @IsMongoId({ message: 'مورد جبران خسارت یافت نشد' }) jobId: string;
  @IsMongoId({ message: 'مجری یافت نشد' }) providerId: string;
  @Type(() => Number) @IsInt(amountMsg) @Min(0, amountMsg) @Max(MAX_AMOUNT, amountMsg) amount: number;
  @Transform(trim) @IsString(reasonMsg) @MinLength(1, reasonMsg) @MaxLength(300) reason: string;
}
export class AdjustmentDto {
  @IsMongoId({ message: 'مجری یافت نشد' }) providerId: string;
  @Type(() => Number) @IsInt(amountMsg) @Min(-MAX_AMOUNT, amountMsg) @Max(MAX_AMOUNT, amountMsg) amount: number;
  @Transform(trim) @IsString(reasonMsg) @MinLength(1, reasonMsg) @MaxLength(300) reason: string;
  @IsOptional() @Transform(trim) @IsString() @MaxLength(20) orderCode?: string;
  @IsUUID('4', requestMsg) requestId: string;
}
export class PayoutDto {
  @IsMongoId({ message: 'مجری یافت نشد' }) providerId: string;
  @Type(() => Number) @IsInt(amountMsg) @Min(1, amountMsg) @Max(MAX_AMOUNT, amountMsg) amount: number;
  @Matches(YMD, ymdMsg) paidAt: string;
  @Transform(trim) @IsString(bankRefMsg) @MinLength(1, bankRefMsg) @MaxLength(60) bankRef: string;
  @IsOptional() @Transform(trim) @IsString() @MaxLength(300) note?: string;
  @IsUUID('4', requestMsg) requestId: string;
}
export class VoidPayoutDto {
  @Transform(trim) @IsString(reasonMsg) @MinLength(1, reasonMsg) @MaxLength(300) reason: string;
}
export class BankAccountDto {
  @IsString({ message: 'شماره شبا باید با IR شروع شود و ۲۴ رقم داشته باشد' }) @MaxLength(40) sheba: string;
  @Transform(trim) @IsString(holderMsg) @MinLength(2, holderMsg) @MaxLength(80, holderMsg) holder: string;
  @IsOptional() @Transform(trim) @IsString() @MaxLength(60) bankName?: string;
}
export class WalletQueryDto {
  @Matches(YMD, ymdMsg) from: string;
  @Matches(YMD, ymdMsg) to: string;
}
```

```ts
import { Injectable, NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { InjectModel } from '@nestjs/mongoose';
import { Model, Types } from 'mongoose';
import { addDaysYmd, tehranDayStart, tehranYmd } from '../../common/utils/dates.js';
import { assertObjectId } from '../../common/utils/object-id.js';
import { escapeRegex, pageParams, type Paged } from '../../common/utils/pagination.js';
import { Center, type BankAccount } from '../centers/center.schema.js';
import { User } from '../users/user.schema.js';
import type { LedgerEntriesQueryDto } from './dto/ledger.dto.js';
import { entryView, type EntryRow, type LedgerEntryView } from './ledger-json.js';
import { LedgerEntry } from './ledger-entry.schema.js';
import { nextSettlementYmd, settlementCutoff, weekEndingAt } from './ledger-model.js';
import { EMPTY_BALANCE, LedgerService, type Balance } from './ledger.service.js';

export interface SettlementInfo { weekday: number; cutoff: string; next: string }
export interface BankAccountView { sheba: string; holder: string; bankName: string; updatedAt: string }
export interface ProviderBalanceRow { providerId: string; name: string; businessName: string; status: string; balance: Balance; lastPayoutAt: string | null; hasBank: boolean }
export interface ProviderLedgerAdmin { provider: { id: string; name: string; businessName: string; status: string }; balance: Balance; bank: BankAccountView | null; settlement: SettlementInfo }
export interface WeeklyStatement { providerId: string; from: string; to: string; opening: number; earned: number; paid: number; closing: number; lines: LedgerEntryView[] }
export type ProviderRow = { _id: Types.ObjectId; name: string; businessName?: string; status?: string; bankAccount?: BankAccount; userId?: Types.ObjectId };

const oid = (id: string) => new Types.ObjectId(id);
const PROVIDER_FIELDS = { name: 1, businessName: 1, status: 1, bankAccount: 1, userId: 1 };

export function bankView(b?: BankAccount | null): BankAccountView | null {
  return b?.sheba ? { sheba: b.sheba, holder: b.holder, bankName: b.bankName ?? '', updatedAt: new Date(b.updatedAt).toISOString() } : null;
}

/** «تسویه مجریان» reads (contract v4.3): balances, a provider's account, entries, weekly statements, pending amounts. */
@Injectable()
export class LedgerReadsService {
  constructor(
    @InjectModel(LedgerEntry.name) private readonly entries: Model<LedgerEntry>,
    @InjectModel(Center.name) private readonly centers: Model<Center>,
    @InjectModel(User.name) private readonly users: Model<User>,
    private readonly ledger: LedgerService,
  ) {}

  async settlement(now = new Date()): Promise<SettlementInfo> {
    const weekday = await this.ledger.weekday();
    return { weekday, cutoff: tehranYmd(settlementCutoff(now, weekday)), next: nextSettlementYmd(now, weekday) };
  }

  async findProvider(id: string): Promise<ProviderRow> {
    assertObjectId(id, 'مجری');
    const p = (await this.centers.findById(id, PROVIDER_FIELDS).lean()) as ProviderRow | null;
    if (!p) throw new NotFoundException('مجری یافت نشد');
    return p;
  }

  /** Every provider (or a name search) with its balance — highest due first; paged in memory (providers are few). */
  async balances(q: { page?: number; limit?: number; q?: string }): Promise<Paged<ProviderBalanceRow>> {
    const { page, limit, skip } = pageParams(q);
    const term = q.q?.trim();
    const rx = term ? new RegExp(escapeRegex(term), 'i') : null;
    const providers = (await this.centers.find(rx ? { $or: [{ name: rx }, { businessName: rx }] } : {}, PROVIDER_FIELDS).lean()) as ProviderRow[];
    const ids = providers.map((p) => String(p._id));
    const [balances, last] = await Promise.all([this.ledger.balances(ids), this.lastPayouts(ids)]);
    const rows = providers
      .map((p) => {
        const id = String(p._id);
        return {
          providerId: id, name: p.name, businessName: p.businessName ?? '', status: p.status ?? 'active',
          balance: balances.get(id) ?? { ...EMPTY_BALANCE }, lastPayoutAt: last.get(id) ?? null, hasBank: !!p.bankAccount?.sheba,
        };
      })
      .sort((a, b) => b.balance.due - a.balance.due || b.balance.available - a.balance.available || a.name.localeCompare(b.name, 'fa'));
    return { items: rows.slice(skip, skip + limit), total: rows.length, page, limit };
  }

  private async lastPayouts(ids: string[]): Promise<Map<string, string>> {
    if (!ids.length) return new Map();
    const rows = await this.entries.aggregate<{ _id: Types.ObjectId; at: Date }>([
      { $match: { providerId: { $in: ids.map(oid) }, kind: 'payout' } },
      { $group: { _id: '$providerId', at: { $max: '$occurredAt' } } },
    ]);
    return new Map(rows.map((r) => [String(r._id), new Date(r.at).toISOString()]));
  }

  async provider(id: string): Promise<ProviderLedgerAdmin> {
    const p = await this.findProvider(id);
    const [balance, settlement] = await Promise.all([this.ledger.balanceOf(id), this.settlement()]);
    return { provider: { id, name: p.name, businessName: p.businessName ?? '', status: p.status ?? 'active' }, balance, bank: bankView(p.bankAccount), settlement };
  }

  async entries(id: string, q: Partial<LedgerEntriesQueryDto>): Promise<Paged<LedgerEntryView>> {
    await this.findProvider(id);
    const { page, limit, skip } = pageParams(q);
    const created: Record<string, Date> = {};
    if (q.from) created.$gte = tehranDayStart(q.from);
    if (q.to) created.$lt = tehranDayStart(addDaysYmd(q.to, 1));
    const filter = { providerId: oid(id), ...(q.kind ? { kind: q.kind } : {}), ...(Object.keys(created).length ? { createdAt: created } : {}) };
    const [rows, total] = await Promise.all([
      this.entries.find(filter).sort({ createdAt: -1, _id: -1 }).skip(skip).limit(limit).lean() as Promise<EntryRow[]>,
      this.entries.countDocuments(filter),
    ]);
    return { items: await this.views(rows), total, page, limit };
  }

  /** D31: the statement week ending at the cutoff of `cutoffYmd`'s week (default: the current cutoff). */
  async statement(id: string, cutoffYmd?: string): Promise<WeeklyStatement> {
    await this.findProvider(id);
    const weekday = await this.ledger.weekday();
    const ref = cutoffYmd ? new Date(tehranDayStart(cutoffYmd).getTime() + 12 * 3_600_000) : new Date();
    const { from, to } = weekEndingAt(settlementCutoff(ref, weekday));
    const pid = oid(id);
    const [before, rows] = await Promise.all([
      this.entries.aggregate<{ v: number }>([{ $match: { providerId: pid, state: 'counted', countedAt: { $lt: from } } }, { $group: { _id: null, v: { $sum: '$amount' } } }]),
      this.entries.find({ providerId: pid, state: 'counted', countedAt: { $gte: from, $lt: to } }).sort({ countedAt: 1, _id: 1 }).lean() as Promise<EntryRow[]>,
    ]);
    const opening = before[0]?.v ?? 0;
    const sum = (kinds: string[]) => rows.filter((r) => kinds.includes(r.kind)).reduce((t, r) => t + (r.amount ?? 0), 0);
    const earned = sum(['credit', 'adjustment']);
    const paid = -sum(['payout', 'payout_void']);
    return { providerId: id, from: tehranYmd(from), to: addDaysYmd(tehranYmd(to), -1), opening, earned, paid, closing: opening + earned - paid, lines: await this.views(rows) };
  }

  /** «نیازمند تعیین مبلغ» (D28): pending credits of every provider, oldest first. */
  async pending(q: { page?: number; limit?: number }): Promise<Paged<LedgerEntryView>> {
    const { page, limit, skip } = pageParams(q);
    const filter = { state: 'pending' };
    const [rows, total] = await Promise.all([
      this.entries.find(filter).sort({ createdAt: 1, _id: 1 }).skip(skip).limit(limit).lean() as Promise<EntryRow[]>,
      this.entries.countDocuments(filter),
    ]);
    return { items: await this.views(rows, true), total, page, limit };
  }

  /** Admin views: void flags of payouts, author / resolver names and (optionally) provider names. */
  async views(rows: EntryRow[], withProviders = false): Promise<LedgerEntryView[]> {
    const payoutIds = rows.filter((r) => r.kind === 'payout').map((r) => r._id);
    const userIds = [...new Set(rows.flatMap((r) => [r.createdBy, r.resolution?.by]).filter(Boolean).map(String))];
    const providerIds = withProviders ? [...new Set(rows.map((r) => String(r.providerId)))] : [];
    const [voids, users, providers] = await Promise.all([
      payoutIds.length ? this.entries.find({ kind: 'payout_void', voidOf: { $in: payoutIds } }, { voidOf: 1 }).lean() : Promise.resolve([]),
      userIds.length ? this.users.find({ _id: { $in: userIds.map(oid) } }, { name: 1 }).lean() : Promise.resolve([]),
      providerIds.length ? this.centers.find({ _id: { $in: providerIds.map(oid) } }, { name: 1 }).lean() : Promise.resolve([]),
    ]);
    const ctx = {
      voided: new Set((voids as { voidOf?: unknown }[]).map((v) => String(v.voidOf))),
      users: new Map((users as { _id: unknown; name: string }[]).map((u) => [String(u._id), u.name])),
      providers: new Map((providers as { _id: unknown; name: string }[]).map((p) => [String(p._id), p.name])),
    };
    return rows.map((r) => entryView(r, ctx));
  }
}
```

```ts
import { Controller, Get, Param, Query, UseGuards } from '@nestjs/common';
import { RequirePermissions, Roles } from '../../common/auth/decorators.js';
import { JwtAuthGuard, RolesGuard } from '../../common/auth/guards.js';
import { PermissionsGuard } from '../../common/auth/permissions.guard.js';
import { PageQueryDto } from '../../common/utils/pagination.js';
import { LedgerEntriesQueryDto, StatementQueryDto } from './dto/ledger.dto.js';
import { LedgerReadsService } from './ledger-reads.service.js';

/** «تسویه مجریان» (contract v4.3 «Admin endpoints»; D33 permissions). */
@Controller('admin/ledger')
@UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard, RolesGuard, PermissionsGuard)
@Roles('admin')
export class AdminLedgerController {
  constructor(private readonly reads: LedgerReadsService) {}

  @Get('balances') @RequirePermissions('ledger_view')
  balances(@Query() q: PageQueryDto) { return this.reads.balances(q); }

  @Get('providers/:id') @RequirePermissions('ledger_view')
  provider(@Param('id') id: string) { return this.reads.provider(id); }

  @Get('providers/:id/entries') @RequirePermissions('ledger_view')
  entries(@Param('id') id: string, @Query() q: LedgerEntriesQueryDto) { return this.reads.entries(id, q); }

  @Get('providers/:id/statement') @RequirePermissions('ledger_view')
  statement(@Param('id') id: string, @Query() q: StatementQueryDto) { return this.reads.statement(id, q.cutoff); }

  @Get('pending') @RequirePermissions('ledger_view')
  pending(@Query() q: PageQueryDto) { return this.reads.pending(q); }
}
```

```ts
import { basisOf, compensationCandidates } from './compensation.js';

const at = (h: number) => new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 18, h));
const base = { _id: 'j1', orderId: 'o1', orderCode: '10421', status: 'in_production', history: [] as never[] };
const snap = (providerId: string, payoutBasis: number | null) => ({ providerId, payoutBasis });

describe('compensation candidates (D30, R6)', () => {
  it('a job cancelled during or after production lists its holder with the snapshot basis', () => {
    for (const stage of ['in_production', 'after_production'] as const) {
      const job = { ...base, status: 'cancelled', cancel: { at: at(10), stage, providerId: 'P' }, providerPricing: snap('P', 90000) };
      expect(compensationCandidates(job)).toEqual([{ providerId: 'P', cause: 'cancelled', stage, at: at(10), payoutBasis: 90000, orderId: 'o1', orderCode: '10421' }]);
    }
    expect(compensationCandidates({ ...base, status: 'cancelled', cancel: { at: at(10), stage: 'before_production', providerId: 'P' } })).toEqual([]);
  });
  it('a provider taken off mid-work by reassign / unassign is listed with its own last snapshot; a failed provider is not', () => {
    const job = {
      ...base,
      providerPricing: snap('Q', 50000),
      pricingHistory: [snap('P', 70000), snap('Q', 50000)],
      history: [
        { at: at(9), action: 'reassigned', stage: 'in_production', providerId: 'Q', previousProviderId: 'P' },
        { at: at(11), action: 'unassigned', stage: 'after_production', providerId: 'Q' },
        { at: at(12), action: 'failed', stage: 'in_production', providerId: 'R' },
        { at: at(13), action: 'reassigned', stage: 'before_production', providerId: 'S', previousProviderId: 'T' },
      ],
    };
    expect(compensationCandidates(job as never).map((c) => [c.providerId, c.cause, c.stage, c.payoutBasis])).toEqual([
      ['P', 'reassigned', 'in_production', 70000], ['Q', 'unassigned', 'after_production', 50000],
    ]);
  });
  it('one candidate per provider (the latest event); an unpriced provider has a null basis', () => {
    const job = {
      ...base, status: 'cancelled', cancel: { at: at(14), stage: 'in_production', providerId: 'P' },
      history: [{ at: at(9), action: 'unassigned', stage: 'in_production', providerId: 'P' }],
    };
    expect(compensationCandidates(job as never)).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ providerId: 'P', cause: 'cancelled', at: at(14), payoutBasis: null })]);
    expect(basisOf({ ...base, pricingHistory: [snap('P', 1), snap('P', 2)] } as never, 'P')).toBe(2);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { BadRequestException, ConflictException, NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { fakeModel, type Doc } from '../../../test/fake-model.js';
import { LedgerActionsService } from './ledger-actions.service.js';
import { LedgerReadsService } from './ledger-reads.service.js';
import { LedgerService } from './ledger.service.js';

const P = new Types.ObjectId();
const ADMIN = String(new Types.ObjectId());
const REQ = '3f2a9c1e-8b7d-4e6f-9a1b-2c3d4e5f6a7b';

function setup({ jobs = [] as Doc[], entries = [] as Doc[] } = {}) {
  const e = fakeModel(entries, { unique: [['key'], ['slot']] });
  const j = fakeModel(jobs);
  const orders = fakeModel([{ _id: new Types.ObjectId(), code: '10421' }]);
  const centers = fakeModel([{ _id: P, name: 'چاپ نگین', status: 'active' }]);
  const catalog = { getSettings: vi.fn(async () => ({ ledger: { settlementWeekday: 6 } })) };
  const ledger = new LedgerService(e as never, fakeModel([]) as never, j as never, orders as never, catalog as never);
  const reads = new LedgerReadsService(e as never, centers as never, fakeModel([]) as never, ledger);
  const svc = new LedgerActionsService(e as never, j as never, orders as never, ledger, reads);
  return { svc, entries: e, ledger, orders };
}
const pending = () => ({ _id: new Types.ObjectId(), key: 'credit:j:p:production', providerId: P, kind: 'credit', state: 'pending', amount: null, occurredAt: new Date(), byRole: 'system', createdAt: new Date() });
const cancelledJob = (o: Doc = {}) => ({
  _id: new Types.ObjectId(), orderId: new Types.ObjectId(), orderCode: '10421', status: 'cancelled', history: [], updatedAt: new Date(),
  cancel: { at: new Date('2026-10-18T10:00:00Z'), stage: 'in_production', providerId: P }, providerPricing: { providerId: String(P), payoutBasis: 90000 }, ...o,
});

describe('resolve a pending credit (D28, R2)', () => {
  it('sets the amount once with a reason; the balance counts it; a second resolve is 409', async () => {
    const row = pending();
    const s = setup({ entries: [row] });
    const v = await s.svc.resolve(String(row._id), { amount: 75000, reason: 'قیمت توافقی' }, ADMIN);
    expect(v).toMatchObject({ state: 'counted', amount: 75000, resolution: { by: { id: ADMIN }, reason: 'قیمت توافقی' } });
    expect((await s.ledger.balanceOf(String(P))).available).toBe(75000);
    await expect(s.svc.resolve(String(row._id), { amount: 1, reason: 'x' }, ADMIN)).rejects.toThrow(new ConflictException('این مبلغ قبلاً تعیین شده است'));
  });
  it('refuses a fractional or negative amount, an empty reason and an entry that is not a credit', async () => {
    const row = pending();
    const s = setup({ entries: [row, { ...pending(), _id: new Types.ObjectId(), key: 'payout:x', kind: 'payout' }] });
    await expect(s.svc.resolve(String(row._id), { amount: 1.5, reason: 'x' }, ADMIN)).rejects.toThrow(new BadRequestException('مبلغ باید عدد صحیح (تومان) باشد'));
    await expect(s.svc.resolve(String(row._id), { amount: -1, reason: 'x' }, ADMIN)).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
    await expect(s.svc.resolve(String(row._id), { amount: 1, reason: '  ' }, ADMIN)).rejects.toThrow(new BadRequestException('دلیل را بنویسید'));
    await expect(s.svc.resolve(String(s.entries.docs[1]._id), { amount: 1, reason: 'x' }, ADMIN)).rejects.toThrow(new NotFoundException('ردیف حساب یافت نشد'));
  });
});

describe('«جبران خسارت مجری» (D30, R6)', () => {
  it('the queue lists open cases; a decision (even 0) records an adjustment and closes the case', async () => {
    const job = cancelledJob();
    const s = setup({ jobs: [job] });
    const q = await s.svc.compensationQueue({});
    expect(q.items).toEqual([{ jobId: String(job._id), orderId: String(job.orderId), orderCode: '10421', providerId: String(P), providerName: 'چاپ نگین', cause: 'cancelled', stage: 'in_production', at: '2026-10-18T10:00:00.000Z', payoutBasis: 90000 }]);
    const v = await s.svc.decideCompensation({ jobId: String(job._id), providerId: String(P), amount: 0, reason: 'کار آغاز نشده بود' }, ADMIN);
    expect(v).toMatchObject({ kind: 'adjustment', cause: 'compensation', compCause: 'cancelled', amount: 0, title: 'جبران خسارت سفارش ۱۰۴۲۱' });
    expect((await s.svc.compensationQueue({})).total).toBe(0);
    await expect(s.svc.decideCompensation({ jobId: String(job._id), providerId: String(P), amount: 1, reason: 'x' }, ADMIN)).rejects.toThrow(new ConflictException('برای این مورد قبلاً تصمیم گرفته شده است'));
  });
  it('the amount is capped by the payout basis; an unpriced case takes any amount; an unknown case is 404', async () => {
    const s = setup({ jobs: [cancelledJob(), cancelledJob({ providerPricing: undefined })] });
    const [a, b] = (await s.svc.compensationQueue({})).items.sort((x, y) => Number(y.payoutBasis !== null) - Number(x.payoutBasis !== null)); // a priced, b unpriced
    await expect(s.svc.decideCompensation({ jobId: a.jobId, providerId: a.providerId, amount: 90001, reason: 'x' }, ADMIN))
      .rejects.toThrow(new BadRequestException('مبلغ جبران خسارت نمی‌تواند از سهم قابل پرداخت به مجری بیشتر باشد'));
    await expect(s.svc.decideCompensation({ jobId: b.jobId, providerId: b.providerId, amount: 500000, reason: 'برآورد کارگاه' }, ADMIN)).resolves.toMatchObject({ amount: 500000 });
    await expect(s.svc.decideCompensation({ jobId: String(new Types.ObjectId()), providerId: String(P), amount: 1, reason: 'x' }, ADMIN))
      .rejects.toThrow(new NotFoundException('مورد جبران خسارت یافت نشد'));
  });
  it('a provider that later delivered the same job is not listed', async () => {
    const job = cancelledJob({ status: 'delivered', cancel: undefined, history: [{ at: new Date(), action: 'unassigned', stage: 'in_production', providerId: P }] });
    const s = setup({ jobs: [job], entries: [{ _id: new Types.ObjectId(), key: `credit:${String(job._id)}:${String(P)}:production`, providerId: P, kind: 'credit', state: 'counted', amount: 1, occurredAt: new Date(), countedAt: new Date(), byRole: 'system' }] });
    expect((await s.svc.compensationQueue({})).total).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe('manual adjustments (D30, R13)', () => {
  it('a negative adjustment with a reason and an order code; a retried request returns the same entry', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const body = { providerId: String(P), amount: -20000, reason: 'مرجوعی پس از تحویل', orderCode: '۱۰۴۲۱', requestId: REQ };
    const a = await s.svc.adjust(body, ADMIN);
    expect(a).toMatchObject({ kind: 'adjustment', cause: 'manual', amount: -20000, orderCode: '10421', title: 'اصلاحیه سفارش ۱۰۴۲۱' });
    expect((await s.svc.adjust(body, ADMIN)).id).toBe(a.id);
    await expect(s.svc.adjust({ ...body, amount: -1 }, ADMIN)).rejects.toThrow(new ConflictException('این درخواست قبلاً با مقادیر دیگری ثبت شده است'));
    expect((await s.ledger.balanceOf(String(P))).available).toBe(-20000);
  });
  it('refuses 0, an unknown order code and an unknown provider', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    await expect(s.svc.adjust({ providerId: String(P), amount: 0, reason: 'x', requestId: REQ }, ADMIN)).rejects.toThrow(new BadRequestException('مبلغ اصلاحیه نمی‌تواند صفر باشد'));
    await expect(s.svc.adjust({ providerId: String(P), amount: 5, reason: 'x', orderCode: '99999', requestId: REQ }, ADMIN)).rejects.toThrow(new NotFoundException('سفارش یافت نشد'));
    await expect(s.svc.adjust({ providerId: String(new Types.ObjectId()), amount: 5, reason: 'x', requestId: REQ }, ADMIN)).rejects.toThrow(new NotFoundException('مجری یافت نشد'));
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `apps/api/src/modules/ledger/compensation.ts`**

```ts
/**
 * v4.3 «جبران خسارت مجری» candidates of a job (D30, R6) — pure. A provider that worked on the job and lost it at the
 * production / after-production stage (cancellation, or an admin's reassign / unassign) may be compensated once.
 */
import { COMP_STAGES, type CompCause, type CompStage } from './ledger-model.js';

export interface CompJob {
  _id: unknown; orderId: unknown; orderCode: string; status: string;
  cancel?: { at: Date; stage: string; providerId?: unknown };
  history?: { at: Date; action: string; stage?: string; providerId?: unknown; previousProviderId?: unknown }[];
  providerPricing?: { providerId: string; payoutBasis: number | null } | null;
  pricingHistory?: { providerId: string; payoutBasis: number | null }[];
}
export interface CompCandidate { providerId: string; cause: CompCause; stage: CompStage; at: Date; payoutBasis: number | null; orderId: string; orderCode: string }

const isStage = (s?: string): s is CompStage => (COMP_STAGES as readonly string[]).includes(s ?? '');

/** That provider's latest snapshot payout basis (current snapshot first, then the history newest first); null = unpriced. */
export function basisOf(job: CompJob, providerId: string): number | null {
  const snaps = [...(job.pricingHistory ?? [])].reverse();
  if (job.providerPricing) snaps.unshift(job.providerPricing);
  return snaps.find((s) => s.providerId === providerId)?.payoutBasis ?? null;
}

export function compensationCandidates(job: CompJob): CompCandidate[] {
  const out = new Map<string, CompCandidate>();
  const add = (pid: unknown, cause: CompCause, stage: CompStage, at: Date) => {
    const providerId = String(pid);
    out.set(providerId, { providerId, cause, stage, at, payoutBasis: basisOf(job, providerId), orderId: String(job.orderId), orderCode: job.orderCode });
  };
  for (const h of job.history ?? []) {
    if (!isStage(h.stage)) continue;
    if (h.action === 'reassigned' && h.previousProviderId) add(h.previousProviderId, 'reassigned', h.stage, h.at);
    else if (h.action === 'unassigned' && h.providerId) add(h.providerId, 'unassigned', h.stage, h.at);
  }
  if (job.status === 'cancelled' && job.cancel?.providerId && isStage(job.cancel.stage)) add(job.cancel.providerId, 'cancelled', job.cancel.stage, job.cancel.at);
  return [...out.values()];
}
```

```ts
import { BadRequestException, ConflictException, Injectable, NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { InjectModel } from '@nestjs/mongoose';
import { Model, Types } from 'mongoose';
import { assertObjectId } from '../../common/utils/object-id.js';
import { pageParams, type Paged } from '../../common/utils/pagination.js';
import { toAsciiDigits } from '../../common/utils/phone.js';
import { Job } from '../dispatch/job.schema.js';
import { Order } from '../orders/order.schema.js';
import { compensationCandidates, type CompJob } from './compensation.js';
import type { EntryRow, LedgerEntryView } from './ledger-json.js';
import { LedgerEntry } from './ledger-entry.schema.js';
import { COMP_STAGES, isAmount, ledgerKey, MAX_AMOUNT, type CompCause, type CompStage } from './ledger-model.js';
import { LedgerReadsService } from './ledger-reads.service.js';
import { LedgerService } from './ledger.service.js';

export interface CompensationItem {
  jobId: string; orderId: string; orderCode: string; providerId: string; providerName: string;
  cause: CompCause; stage: CompStage; at: string; payoutBasis: number | null;
}

export const AMOUNT_MSG = 'مبلغ باید عدد صحیح (تومان) باشد';
const DECIDED = 'برای این مورد قبلاً تصمیم گرفته شده است';
const SAME_REQUEST = 'این درخواست قبلاً با مقادیر دیگری ثبت شده است';
const oid = (id: string) => new Types.ObjectId(id);
export function needReason(reason: string | undefined): string {
  const r = reason?.trim();
  if (!r) throw new BadRequestException('دلیل را بنویسید');
  return r;
}

/** Admin money decisions besides payouts (D28, D30): pending amounts, compensation, manual adjustments. */
@Injectable()
export class LedgerActionsService {
  constructor(
    @InjectModel(LedgerEntry.name) private readonly entries: Model<LedgerEntry>,
    @InjectModel(Job.name) private readonly jobs: Model<Job>,
    @InjectModel(Order.name) private readonly orders: Model<Order>,
    private readonly ledger: LedgerService,
    private readonly reads: LedgerReadsService,
  ) {}

  /** R2: a pending credit gets its amount once (compare-and-set on `state`). */
  async resolve(entryId: string, body: { amount: number; reason: string }, by: string): Promise<LedgerEntryView> {
    assertObjectId(entryId, 'ردیف حساب');
    if (!isAmount(body.amount, { min: 0 })) throw new BadRequestException(AMOUNT_MSG);
    const reason = needReason(body.reason);
    const row = await this.entries.findById(entryId).lean();
    if (!row || row.kind !== 'credit') throw new NotFoundException('ردیف حساب یافت نشد');
    const now = new Date();
    const updated = await this.entries.findOneAndUpdate(
      { _id: row._id, kind: 'credit', state: 'pending' },
      { $set: { state: 'counted', amount: body.amount, countedAt: now, resolution: { by: oid(by), at: now, reason } } },
      { returnDocument: 'after' },
    );
    if (!updated) throw new ConflictException('این مبلغ قبلاً تعیین شده است');
    return (await this.reads.views([updated as unknown as EntryRow]))[0];
  }

  async compensationQueue(q: { page?: number; limit?: number }): Promise<Paged<CompensationItem>> {
    const items = await this.openCompensation();
    const { page, limit, skip } = pageParams(q);
    return { items: items.slice(skip, skip + limit), total: items.length, page, limit };
  }

  /** Open cases (newest first): candidates without a decision and without a production credit of the same provider. */
  private async openCompensation(jobId?: Types.ObjectId): Promise<CompensationItem[]> {
    const stages = [...COMP_STAGES];
    const filter = {
      ...(jobId ? { _id: jobId } : {}),
      $or: [
        { status: 'cancelled', 'cancel.stage': { $in: stages } },
        { history: { $elemMatch: { action: { $in: ['reassigned', 'unassigned'] }, stage: { $in: stages } } } },
      ],
    };
    // bounded: the filter is selective (work lost mid-production); M5 adds paging by the filter if it ever grows
    const jobs = (await this.jobs.find(filter).sort({ updatedAt: -1 }).limit(1000).lean()) as unknown as CompJob[];
    const candidates = jobs.flatMap((j) => compensationCandidates(j).map((c) => ({ ...c, jobId: String(j._id) })));
    if (!candidates.length) return [];
    const keys = candidates.flatMap((c) => [ledgerKey.compensation(c.jobId, c.providerId), ledgerKey.credit(c.jobId, c.providerId, 'production')]);
    const done = new Set(((await this.entries.find({ key: { $in: keys } }, { key: 1 }).lean()) as { key: string }[]).map((e) => e.key));
    const open = candidates.filter((c) => !done.has(ledgerKey.compensation(c.jobId, c.providerId)) && !done.has(ledgerKey.credit(c.jobId, c.providerId, 'production')));
    const names = await this.reads.providerNames(open.map((c) => c.providerId));
    return open
      .map((c) => ({
        jobId: c.jobId, orderId: c.orderId, orderCode: c.orderCode, providerId: c.providerId, providerName: names.get(c.providerId) ?? '',
        cause: c.cause, stage: c.stage, at: new Date(c.at).toISOString(), payoutBasis: c.payoutBasis,
      }))
      .sort((a, b) => b.at.localeCompare(a.at));
  }

  /** D30: 0 ≤ amount ≤ the provider's payout basis (any amount ≥ 0 when unpriced), once per job + provider. */
  async decideCompensation(body: { jobId: string; providerId: string; amount: number; reason: string }, by: string): Promise<LedgerEntryView> {
    assertObjectId(body.jobId, 'مورد جبران خسارت');
    assertObjectId(body.providerId, 'مجری');
    if (!isAmount(body.amount, { min: 0 })) throw new BadRequestException(AMOUNT_MSG);
    const reason = needReason(body.reason);
    const key = ledgerKey.compensation(body.jobId, body.providerId);
    const c = (await this.openCompensation(oid(body.jobId))).find((x) => x.providerId === body.providerId);
    if (!c) {
      if (await this.entries.exists({ key })) throw new ConflictException(DECIDED);
      throw new NotFoundException('مورد جبران خسارت یافت نشد');
    }
    if (c.payoutBasis !== null && body.amount > c.payoutBasis) throw new BadRequestException('مبلغ جبران خسارت نمی‌تواند از سهم قابل پرداخت به مجری بیشتر باشد');
    const now = new Date();
    const { entry, created } = await this.ledger.withSlot(body.providerId, key, () => ({
      providerId: oid(body.providerId), kind: 'adjustment', cause: 'compensation', compCause: c.cause, state: 'counted', amount: body.amount,
      jobId: oid(body.jobId), orderId: oid(c.orderId), orderCode: c.orderCode, occurredAt: now, countedAt: now, reason,
      basis: { payoutBasis: c.payoutBasis, suggested: null, missing: [] }, createdBy: oid(by), byRole: 'admin',
    }));
    if (!created) throw new ConflictException(DECIDED);
    return (await this.reads.views([entry as unknown as EntryRow]))[0];
  }

  /** A signed correction with a reason (e.g. a refund after delivery); idempotent per `requestId` (R13). */
  async adjust(body: { providerId: string; amount: number; reason: string; orderCode?: string; requestId: string }, by: string): Promise<LedgerEntryView> {
    await this.reads.findProvider(body.providerId);
    if (!isAmount(body.amount, { min: -MAX_AMOUNT })) throw new BadRequestException(AMOUNT_MSG);
    if (body.amount === 0) throw new BadRequestException('مبلغ اصلاحیه نمی‌تواند صفر باشد');
    const reason = needReason(body.reason);
    let order: { _id: Types.ObjectId; code: string } | null = null;
    const code = body.orderCode?.trim() ? toAsciiDigits(body.orderCode.trim()) : '';
    if (code) {
      order = (await this.orders.findOne({ code }, { code: 1 }).lean()) as { _id: Types.ObjectId; code: string } | null;
      if (!order) throw new NotFoundException('سفارش یافت نشد');
    }
    const now = new Date();
    const { entry, created } = await this.ledger.withSlot(body.providerId, ledgerKey.adjustment(body.requestId), () => ({
      providerId: oid(body.providerId), kind: 'adjustment', cause: 'manual', state: 'counted', amount: body.amount,
      ...(order ? { orderId: order._id, orderCode: order.code } : {}), occurredAt: now, countedAt: now, reason, createdBy: oid(by), byRole: 'admin',
    }));
    if (!created && (String(entry.providerId) !== body.providerId || entry.amount !== body.amount || entry.kind !== 'adjustment')) throw new ConflictException(SAME_REQUEST);
    return (await this.reads.views([entry as unknown as EntryRow]))[0];
  }
}
```

```ts
import { BadRequestException, ConflictException, NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { fakeModel, type Doc } from '../../../test/fake-model.js';
import { LedgerReadsService } from './ledger-reads.service.js';
import { LedgerService } from './ledger.service.js';
import { PayoutsService } from './payouts.service.js';

const P = new Types.ObjectId();
const ADMIN = String(new Types.ObjectId());
const SHEBA = 'IR062960000000100324200001';
const uuid = (n: number) => `3f2a9c1e-8b7d-4e6f-9a1b-${String(n).padStart(12, '0')}`;
const credit = (amount: number) => ({ _id: new Types.ObjectId(), key: `credit:${new Types.ObjectId()}`, providerId: P, kind: 'credit', state: 'counted', amount, occurredAt: new Date('2026-10-14T10:00:00Z'), countedAt: new Date('2026-10-14T10:00:00Z'), byRole: 'system', createdAt: new Date() });

function setup({ bank = true, entries = [credit(100000)] as Doc[] } = {}) {
  const e = fakeModel(entries, { unique: [['key'], ['slot']] });
  const centers = fakeModel([{ _id: P, name: 'چاپ نگین', status: 'active', userId: new Types.ObjectId(), ...(bank ? { bankAccount: { sheba: SHEBA, holder: 'علی نگینی', bankName: 'نمونه', updatedAt: new Date() } } : {}) }]);
  const catalog = { getSettings: vi.fn(async () => ({ ledger: { settlementWeekday: 6 } })) };
  const ledger = new LedgerService(e as never, fakeModel([]) as never, fakeModel([]) as never, fakeModel([]) as never, catalog as never);
  const reads = new LedgerReadsService(e as never, centers as never, fakeModel([]) as never, ledger);
  const notifier = { payout: vi.fn(async () => undefined) };
  const svc = new PayoutsService(e as never, centers as never, ledger, reads, notifier as never);
  return { svc, entries: e, centers, ledger, notifier };
}
const body = (amount: number, n = 1, o: object = {}) => ({ providerId: String(P), amount, paidAt: '2026-10-17', bankRef: 'TRX-1', requestId: uuid(n), ...o });

describe('payouts (D31, R1)', () => {
  it('records a payout up to the balance with the bank reference and the masked Sheba, and tells the provider', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const v = await s.svc.record(body(60000, 1, { note: 'تسویه هفته اول' }), ADMIN);
    expect(v).toMatchObject({ kind: 'payout', amount: -60000, title: 'پرداخت تسویه', payout: { paidAt: '2026-10-17', bankRef: 'TRX-1', note: 'تسویه هفته اول', sheba: `IR${'•'.repeat(20)}0001`, holder: 'علی نگینی' } });
    expect((await s.ledger.balanceOf(String(P))).available).toBe(40000);
    expect(s.notifier.payout).toHaveBeenCalledWith('payout_recorded', String(P), 60000, expect.anything());
  });
  it('refuses more than the balance, a future date, a missing bank account and a fractional amount', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    await expect(s.svc.record(body(100001), ADMIN)).rejects.toThrow(new BadRequestException('مبلغ پرداخت از مانده حساب مجری بیشتر است'));
    await expect(s.svc.record(body(1, 2, { paidAt: '2999-01-01' }), ADMIN)).rejects.toThrow(new BadRequestException('تاریخ پرداخت نمی‌تواند در آینده باشد'));
    await expect(s.svc.record(body(1.5, 3), ADMIN)).rejects.toThrow(new BadRequestException('مبلغ باید عدد صحیح (تومان) باشد'));
    await expect(setup({ bank: false }).svc.record(body(1), ADMIN)).rejects.toThrow(new BadRequestException('ابتدا شماره شبای مجری را ثبت کنید'));
    await expect(s.svc.record(body(1, 4, { providerId: String(new Types.ObjectId()) }), ADMIN)).rejects.toThrow(new NotFoundException('مجری یافت نشد'));
  });
  it('concurrent payouts never exceed the balance (Review Focus 1)', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const res = await Promise.allSettled([s.svc.record(body(80000, 1), ADMIN), s.svc.record(body(80000, 2), ADMIN), s.svc.record(body(80000, 3), ADMIN)]);
    expect(res.filter((r) => r.status === 'fulfilled')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect((await s.ledger.balanceOf(String(P))).available).toBe(20000);
  });
  it('a retried request returns the same payout (also concurrently); different values under the same id are 409 (R13)', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const [a, b] = await Promise.all([s.svc.record(body(60000, 7), ADMIN), s.svc.record(body(60000, 7), ADMIN)]);
    expect(a.id).toBe(b.id);
    expect((await s.svc.record(body(60000, 7), ADMIN)).id).toBe(a.id);
    expect(s.entries.docs.filter((d) => d.kind === 'payout')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(s.notifier.payout).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    await expect(s.svc.record(body(1000, 7), ADMIN)).rejects.toThrow(new ConflictException('این درخواست قبلاً با مقادیر دیگری ثبت شده است'));
  });
});

describe('payout voids (D31, Review Focus 4)', () => {
  it('a payout is voided once: the balance comes back, the provider is told, a second void is 409', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const p = await s.svc.record(body(60000), ADMIN);
    const v = await s.svc.void(p.id, 'شماره پیگیری اشتباه', ADMIN);
    expect(v).toMatchObject({ kind: 'payout_void', amount: 60000, voidOf: p.id, title: 'ابطال پرداخت' });
    expect((await s.ledger.balanceOf(String(P))).available).toBe(100000);
    expect(s.notifier.payout).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith('payout_voided', String(P), 60000, expect.anything());
    await expect(s.svc.void(p.id, 'دوباره', ADMIN)).rejects.toThrow(new ConflictException('این پرداخت قبلاً باطل شده است'));
    await expect(Promise.all([s.svc.void(p.id, 'x', ADMIN), s.svc.void(p.id, 'y', ADMIN)])).rejects.toThrow(ConflictException);
    expect(s.entries.docs.filter((d) => d.kind === 'payout_void')).toHaveLength(1);
  });
  it('void and a new payout race through the slot: the balance never goes below zero', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const p = await s.svc.record(body(80000, 1), ADMIN);
    const [voided, paid] = await Promise.allSettled([s.svc.void(p.id, 'اشتباه', ADMIN), s.svc.record(body(50000, 2), ADMIN)]);
    expect(voided.status).toBe('fulfilled');
    const available = (await s.ledger.balanceOf(String(P))).available;
    expect(available).toBe(paid.status === 'fulfilled' ? 50000 : 100000);
    expect(available).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
  });
  it('only payouts can be voided; an unknown id is 404; the reason is required', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    await expect(s.svc.void(String(s.entries.docs[0]._id), 'x', ADMIN)).rejects.toThrow(new NotFoundException('پرداخت یافت نشد'));
    await expect(s.svc.void(String(new Types.ObjectId()), 'x', ADMIN)).rejects.toThrow(NotFoundException);
    const p = await s.svc.record(body(1), ADMIN);
    await expect(s.svc.void(p.id, ' ', ADMIN)).rejects.toThrow(new BadRequestException('دلیل را بنویسید'));
  });
});

describe('bank account (D31, R7)', () => {
  it('normalizes and validates the Sheba; an invalid one changes nothing', async () => {
    const s = setup({ bank: false });
    const v = await s.svc.setBank(String(P), { sheba: '۰۶۲۹۶۰۰۰۰۰۰۰۱۰۰۳۲۴۲۰۰۰۰۱', holder: ' علی نگینی ', bankName: 'نمونه' }, ADMIN);
    expect(v).toMatchObject({ sheba: SHEBA, holder: 'علی نگینی', bankName: 'نمونه' });
    expect(s.centers.docs[0].bankAccount).toMatchObject({ sheba: SHEBA, holder: 'علی نگینی', updatedBy: expect.anything() });
    await expect(s.svc.setBank(String(P), { sheba: 'IR062960000000100324200002', holder: 'x y' }, ADMIN)).rejects.toThrow(new BadRequestException('شماره شبا معتبر نیست؛ رقم‌های آن را بررسی کنید'));
    await expect(s.svc.setBank(String(P), { sheba: SHEBA, holder: 'x' }, ADMIN)).rejects.toThrow(new BadRequestException('نام صاحب حساب را وارد کنید'));
    expect(s.centers.docs[0].bankAccount.sheba).toBe(SHEBA);
    await expect(s.svc.setBank(String(new Types.ObjectId()), { sheba: SHEBA, holder: 'علی نگینی' }, ADMIN)).rejects.toThrow(new NotFoundException('مجری یافت نشد'));
  });
});
```

```ts
import { Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
import { InjectModel } from '@nestjs/mongoose';
import { Model } from 'mongoose';
import type { LedgerEvent } from '../../common/constants.js';
import { fa } from '../../common/utils/fa.js';
import { Center } from '../centers/center.schema.js';
import { NotificationsService } from '../notifications/notifications.service.js';

/** D19 / R12: payout events to the provider's linked login — «سرو مجری» inbox + web push. Never throws. */
@Injectable()
export class LedgerNotifier {
  private readonly logger = new Logger('LedgerNotifier');

  constructor(
    private readonly notifications: NotificationsService,
    @InjectModel(Center.name) private readonly centers: Model<Center>,
  ) {}

  async payout(event: LedgerEvent, providerId: string, amount: number, entryId: unknown): Promise<void> {
    try {
      const c = await this.centers.findById(providerId, { userId: 1 }).lean();
      if (!c?.userId) return;
      await this.notifications.notify(event, [c.userId], { url: '/provider/settlement', tag: `payout-${String(entryId)}` }, ` — ${fa(amount)} تومان`);
    } catch (err) {
      this.logger.warn(`${event} for provider ${providerId} failed: ${(err as Error).message}`);
    }
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `apps/api/src/modules/ledger/payouts.service.ts`**

```ts
import { BadRequestException, ConflictException, Injectable, NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { InjectModel } from '@nestjs/mongoose';
import { Model, Types } from 'mongoose';
import { isYmd, tehranYmd } from '../../common/utils/dates.js';
import { assertObjectId } from '../../common/utils/object-id.js';
import { Center, type BankAccount } from '../centers/center.schema.js';
import { AMOUNT_MSG, needReason } from './ledger-actions.service.js';
import type { EntryRow, LedgerEntryView } from './ledger-json.js';
import { LedgerEntry } from './ledger-entry.schema.js';
import { isAmount, ledgerKey } from './ledger-model.js';
import { LedgerNotifier } from './ledger-notifier.js';
import { bankView, LedgerReadsService, type BankAccountView } from './ledger-reads.service.js';
import { LedgerService } from './ledger.service.js';
import { maskSheba, normalizeSheba, shebaProblem } from './sheba.js';

const oid = (id: string) => new Types.ObjectId(id);

/** D31: weekly payouts recorded by an admin (never above the balance, R1), their voids, and the payout account (R7). */
@Injectable()
export class PayoutsService {
  constructor(
    @InjectModel(LedgerEntry.name) private readonly entries: Model<LedgerEntry>,
    @InjectModel(Center.name) private readonly centers: Model<Center>,
    private readonly ledger: LedgerService,
    private readonly reads: LedgerReadsService,
    private readonly notifier: LedgerNotifier,
  ) {}

  async record(body: { providerId: string; amount: number; paidAt: string; bankRef: string; note?: string; requestId: string }, by: string): Promise<LedgerEntryView> {
    const p = await this.reads.findProvider(body.providerId);
    if (!isAmount(body.amount, { min: 1 })) throw new BadRequestException(AMOUNT_MSG);
    if (!isYmd(body.paidAt)) throw new BadRequestException('تاریخ باید به‌صورت yyyy-mm-dd باشد');
    if (body.paidAt > tehranYmd()) throw new BadRequestException('تاریخ پرداخت نمی‌تواند در آینده باشد');
    const bankRef = body.bankRef?.trim();
    if (!bankRef) throw new BadRequestException('شماره پیگیری بانکی را وارد کنید');
    const bank = p.bankAccount;
    if (!bank?.sheba) throw new BadRequestException('ابتدا شماره شبای مجری را ثبت کنید');
    const note = body.note?.trim();
    const now = new Date();
    const { entry, created } = await this.ledger.withSlot(body.providerId, ledgerKey.payout(body.requestId), (b) => {
      if (body.amount > b.available) throw new BadRequestException('مبلغ پرداخت از مانده حساب مجری بیشتر است');
      return {
        providerId: oid(body.providerId), kind: 'payout', state: 'counted', amount: -body.amount,
        occurredAt: new Date(`${body.paidAt}T12:00:00+03:30`), countedAt: now,
        payout: { paidAt: body.paidAt, bankRef, ...(note ? { note } : {}), sheba: maskSheba(bank.sheba), holder: bank.holder },
        ...(note ? { reason: note } : {}), createdBy: oid(by), byRole: 'admin',
      };
    });
    if (!created) {
      const same = entry.kind === 'payout' && String(entry.providerId) === body.providerId && entry.amount === -body.amount;
      if (!same) throw new ConflictException('این درخواست قبلاً با مقادیر دیگری ثبت شده است');
    } else {
      await this.notifier.payout('payout_recorded', body.providerId, body.amount, entry._id);
    }
    return (await this.reads.views([entry as unknown as EntryRow]))[0];
  }

  /** A payout is undone only by a reversing entry, once (key `void:<payoutId>`). */
  async void(payoutId: string, reason: string, by: string): Promise<LedgerEntryView> {
    assertObjectId(payoutId, 'پرداخت');
    const why = needReason(reason);
    const payout = await this.entries.findById(payoutId).lean();
    if (!payout || payout.kind !== 'payout') throw new NotFoundException('پرداخت یافت نشد');
    const providerId = String(payout.providerId);
    const amount = -(payout.amount ?? 0);
    const now = new Date();
    const { entry, created } = await this.ledger.withSlot(providerId, ledgerKey.void(payout._id), () => ({
      providerId: payout.providerId, kind: 'payout_void', state: 'counted', amount, voidOf: payout._id,
      occurredAt: now, countedAt: now, reason: why, createdBy: oid(by), byRole: 'admin',
    }));
    if (!created) throw new ConflictException('این پرداخت قبلاً باطل شده است');
    await this.notifier.payout('payout_voided', providerId, amount, entry._id);
    return (await this.reads.views([entry as unknown as EntryRow]))[0];
  }

  /** R7: admins with `payout_record` only; the Sheba is normalized and checksum-validated. */
  async setBank(providerId: string, body: { sheba: string; holder: string; bankName?: string }, by: string): Promise<BankAccountView> {
    assertObjectId(providerId, 'مجری');
    const problem = shebaProblem(body.sheba);
    if (problem) throw new BadRequestException(problem);
    const holder = body.holder?.trim() ?? '';
    if (holder.length < 2 || holder.length > 80) throw new BadRequestException('نام صاحب حساب را وارد کنید');
    const bankAccount: BankAccount = { sheba: normalizeSheba(body.sheba), holder, bankName: body.bankName?.trim() ?? '', updatedAt: new Date(), updatedBy: oid(by) };
    const doc = await this.centers.findByIdAndUpdate(providerId, { $set: { bankAccount } }, { returnDocument: 'after' });
    if (!doc) throw new NotFoundException('مجری یافت نشد');
    return bankView(bankAccount)!;
  }
}
```

```ts
import { walletCsv, walletReport } from './wallet-report.js';

const at = (iso: string) => new Date(iso);
const orders = [
  // home pickup + courier delivery, paid by gateway: 2 courier tasks in week 1
  { paid: true, chargedAmount: 500000, delivery: { method: 'courier', homePickup: true }, timeline: [{ status: 'picked_up', at: at('2026-10-13T07:00:00Z') }, { status: 'delivered', at: at('2026-10-14T07:00:00Z') }] },
  // delivered by the provider (COD paid on delivery): no courier task
  { paid: true, chargedAmount: 300000, delivery: { method: 'provider', homePickup: false }, timeline: [{ status: 'delivered', at: at('2026-10-18T07:00:00Z') }] },
  // picked up, then cancelled: the courier still did the pickup; nothing charged
  { paid: false, delivery: { method: 'courier', homePickup: true }, timeline: [{ status: 'picked_up', at: at('2026-10-20T07:00:00Z') }, { status: 'cancelled', at: at('2026-10-21T07:00:00Z') }] },
  // outside the range
  { paid: true, quote: { total: 999 }, timeline: [{ status: 'delivered', at: at('2026-10-30T07:00:00Z') }] },
];
const entries = [
  { kind: 'credit', amount: 60000, countedAt: at('2026-10-15T10:00:00Z') },
  { kind: 'adjustment', amount: -5000, countedAt: at('2026-10-19T10:00:00Z') },
  { kind: 'payout', amount: -50000, countedAt: at('2026-10-19T10:00:00Z') }, // a payout is a settlement, not a cost
];
const report = () => walletReport({ from: '2026-10-12', to: '2026-10-25', weekday: 6, perTaskFee: 75000, orders, entries });

describe('«کیف پول دیجیتال سرو» (D32, R9)', () => {
  it('one row per settlement week (first and last clipped): charged − provider credits − courier pay = the share', () => {
    const r = report();
    expect(r.rows).toEqual([
      { from: '2026-10-12', to: '2026-10-16', orders: 1, charged: 500000, providerCost: 60000, courierTasks: 2, courierCost: 150000, share: 290000 },
      { from: '2026-10-17', to: '2026-10-23', orders: 1, charged: 300000, providerCost: -5000, courierTasks: 1, courierCost: 75000, share: 230000 },
      { from: '2026-10-24', to: '2026-10-25', orders: 0, charged: 0, providerCost: 0, courierTasks: 0, courierCost: 0, share: 0 },
    ]);
    expect(r.total).toEqual({ from: '2026-10-12', to: '2026-10-25', orders: 2, charged: 800000, providerCost: 55000, courierTasks: 3, courierCost: 225000, share: 520000 });
    expect(r.perTaskFee).toBe(75000);
  });
  it('a one-day range on a settlement day is one row; an order without `delivery` reads as courier + home pickup', () => {
    const legacy = [{ paid: true, chargedAmount: 10000, timeline: [{ status: 'picked_up', at: at('2026-10-17T06:00:00Z') }, { status: 'delivered', at: at('2026-10-17T09:00:00Z') }] }];
    const r = walletReport({ from: '2026-10-17', to: '2026-10-17', weekday: 6, perTaskFee: 1000, orders: legacy, entries: [] });
    expect(r.rows).toEqual([{ from: '2026-10-17', to: '2026-10-17', orders: 1, charged: 10000, providerCost: 0, courierTasks: 2, courierCost: 2000, share: 8000 }]);
  });
  it('CSV: UTF-8 BOM, Persian headers, ASCII numbers, one line per week and «جمع», CRLF', () => {
    const csv = walletCsv(report());
    expect(csv.startsWith('﻿')).toBe(true);
    const lines = csv.slice(1).split('\r\n');
    expect(lines[0]).toBe('از تاریخ,تا تاریخ,سفارش‌های تحویل‌شده,دریافتی از مشتریان,سهم مجریان,کارهای پیک,دستمزد پیک (برآورد),سهم دیجیتال سرو');
    expect(lines[1]).toBe('2026-10-12,2026-10-16,1,500000,60000,2,150000,290000');
    expect(lines[4]).toBe('جمع,,2,800000,55000,3,225000,520000');
    expect(lines).toHaveLength(6); // trailing CRLF
  });
});
```
