- [ ] **Step 3: `apps/api/src/modules/ledger/wallet-report.ts`**

```ts
/**
 * v4.3 «کیف پول دیجیتال سرو» (D32, R9) — pure. Per settlement week: what customers paid for delivered orders, minus the
 * provider credits/adjustments counted that week, minus the courier pay estimate (tasks × the current per-task fee).
 */
import { addDaysYmd, tehranDayStart, tehranYmd } from '../../common/utils/dates.js';
import { deliveryOf } from '../orders/delivery-rules.js';
import { settlementCutoff } from './ledger-model.js';

export interface WalletOrder {
  paid?: boolean; chargedAmount?: number; quote?: { total?: number };
  timeline?: { status: string; at: Date }[]; delivery?: { method?: string; homePickup?: boolean } | null;
}
export interface WalletEntry { kind: string; amount: number | null; countedAt?: Date }
export interface WalletRow { from: string; to: string; orders: number; charged: number; providerCost: number; courierTasks: number; courierCost: number; share: number }
export interface WalletReport { from: string; to: string; perTaskFee: number; rows: WalletRow[]; total: WalletRow }

const WEEK = 7 * 86_400_000;
const PICKUP_EVENTS = ['picked_up', 'awaiting_approval'];

function eventAt(o: WalletOrder, statuses: string[], which: 'first' | 'last'): Date | null {
  const list = (o.timeline ?? []).filter((e) => statuses.includes(e.status));
  const e = which === 'first' ? list[0] : list[list.length - 1];
  return e ? new Date(e.at) : null;
}

export function walletReport(input: { from: string; to: string; weekday: number; perTaskFee: number; orders: WalletOrder[]; entries: WalletEntry[] }): WalletReport {
  const start = tehranDayStart(input.from);
  const end = tehranDayStart(addDaysYmd(input.to, 1));
  const bounds: Date[] = [start];
  for (let b = settlementCutoff(start, input.weekday).getTime() + WEEK; b < end.getTime(); b += WEEK) bounds.push(new Date(b));
  bounds.push(end);
  const zero = (from: string, to: string): WalletRow => ({ from, to, orders: 0, charged: 0, providerCost: 0, courierTasks: 0, courierCost: 0, share: 0 });
  const rows = bounds.slice(0, -1).map((b, i) => zero(tehranYmd(b), addDaysYmd(tehranYmd(bounds[i + 1]), -1)));
  const rowOf = (t: Date | null): WalletRow | null => {
    if (!t || t < start || t >= end) return null;
    let i = 0;
    while (i < rows.length - 1 && t >= bounds[i + 1]) i++;
    return rows[i];
  };
  for (const o of input.orders) {
    const d = deliveryOf(o as never);
    const delivered = rowOf(eventAt(o, ['delivered'], 'last'));
    if (delivered && o.paid) {
      delivered.orders++;
      delivered.charged += o.chargedAmount ?? o.quote?.total ?? 0;
    }
    // the courier is paid per task (as in «سرو پیک»): a home pickup, and a delivery when the courier delivered
    if (delivered && d.method === 'courier') delivered.courierTasks++;
    const pickup = d.homePickup ? rowOf(eventAt(o, PICKUP_EVENTS, 'first')) : null;
    if (pickup) pickup.courierTasks++;
  }
  for (const e of input.entries) {
    if (e.kind !== 'credit' && e.kind !== 'adjustment') continue;
    const r = rowOf(e.countedAt ? new Date(e.countedAt) : null);
    if (r) r.providerCost += e.amount ?? 0;
  }
  const total = zero(input.from, input.to);
  for (const r of rows) {
    r.courierCost = r.courierTasks * input.perTaskFee;
    r.share = r.charged - r.providerCost - r.courierCost;
    total.orders += r.orders;
    total.charged += r.charged;
    total.providerCost += r.providerCost;
    total.courierTasks += r.courierTasks;
    total.courierCost += r.courierCost;
    total.share += r.share;
  }
  return { from: input.from, to: input.to, perTaskFee: input.perTaskFee, rows, total };
}

const HEAD = ['از تاریخ', 'تا تاریخ', 'سفارش‌های تحویل‌شده', 'دریافتی از مشتریان', 'سهم مجریان', 'کارهای پیک', 'دستمزد پیک (برآورد)', 'سهم دیجیتال سرو'];

/** Excel-friendly CSV: UTF-8 BOM, CRLF, ASCII digits and Gregorian dates (no user text, so no CSV injection surface). */
export function walletCsv(r: WalletReport): string {
  const nums = (x: WalletRow) => [x.orders, x.charged, x.providerCost, x.courierTasks, x.courierCost, x.share];
  const lines = [HEAD.join(','), ...r.rows.map((x) => [x.from, x.to, ...nums(x)].join(',')), ['جمع', '', ...nums(r.total)].join(',')];
  return `﻿${lines.join('\r\n')}\r\n`;
}
```

```ts
import { BadRequestException, Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { InjectModel } from '@nestjs/mongoose';
import { Model } from 'mongoose';
import { addDaysYmd, isYmd, tehranDayStart } from '../../common/utils/dates.js';
import { CatalogService } from '../catalog/catalog.service.js';
import { Order } from '../orders/order.schema.js';
import { LedgerEntry } from './ledger-entry.schema.js';
import { walletCsv, walletReport, type WalletEntry, type WalletOrder, type WalletReport } from './wallet-report.js';

@Injectable()
export class WalletService {
  constructor(
    @InjectModel(Order.name) private readonly orders: Model<Order>,
    @InjectModel(LedgerEntry.name) private readonly entries: Model<LedgerEntry>,
    private readonly catalog: CatalogService,
  ) {}

  async report(q: { from: string; to: string }): Promise<WalletReport> {
    if (!isYmd(q.from) || !isYmd(q.to)) throw new BadRequestException('تاریخ باید به‌صورت yyyy-mm-dd باشد');
    if (q.to < q.from) throw new BadRequestException('تاریخ پایان نباید قبل از تاریخ شروع باشد');
    const start = tehranDayStart(q.from);
    const end = tehranDayStart(addDaysYmd(q.to, 1));
    if ((end.getTime() - start.getTime()) / 86_400_000 > 366) throw new BadRequestException('بازه گزارش حداکثر ۳۶۶ روز است');
    const { ledger, courier } = await this.catalog.getSettings();
    const [orders, entries] = await Promise.all([
      this.orders.find(
        { timeline: { $elemMatch: { status: { $in: ['delivered', 'picked_up', 'awaiting_approval'] }, at: { $gte: start, $lt: end } } } },
        { paid: 1, chargedAmount: 1, 'quote.total': 1, timeline: 1, delivery: 1 },
      ).lean(),
      this.entries.find({ state: 'counted', kind: { $in: ['credit', 'adjustment'] }, countedAt: { $gte: start, $lt: end } }, { kind: 1, amount: 1, countedAt: 1 }).lean(),
    ]);
    return walletReport({
      from: q.from, to: q.to, weekday: ledger.settlementWeekday, perTaskFee: courier.perTaskFee,
      orders: orders as unknown as WalletOrder[], entries: entries as unknown as WalletEntry[],
    });
  }

  async csv(q: { from: string; to: string }): Promise<{ filename: string; body: string }> {
    return { filename: `sarv-wallet-${q.from}-${q.to}.csv`, body: walletCsv(await this.report(q)) };
  }
}
```

```ts

  walletReport(@Query() q: WalletQueryDto) { return this.wallet.report(q); }

  @Get('wallet/export') @RequirePermissions('ledger_view')
  async walletExport(@Query() q: WalletQueryDto, @Res({ passthrough: true }) res: Response): Promise<string> {
    const { filename, body } = await this.wallet.csv(q);
    res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'text/csv; charset=utf-8');
    res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', `attachment; filename="${filename}"`);
    return body;
  }
```

```ts
  /** «سرو مجری» → «تسویه»: the caller's own balance, the next settlement day and its masked bank account (R7, R8). */
  async providerSummary(providerId: string): Promise<ProviderLedgerSelf> {
    const p = await this.findProvider(providerId);
    const [b, s] = await Promise.all([this.ledger.balanceOf(providerId), this.settlement()]);
    return {
      balance: { available: b.available, due: b.due, pendingCount: b.pendingCount },
      settlement: { weekday: s.weekday, next: s.next },
      bank: p.bankAccount?.sheba ? { sheba: maskSheba(p.bankAccount.sheba), holder: p.bankAccount.holder } : null,
    };
  }

  /** The caller's own lines, newest first (D32): titles, dates, own amounts and payout bank references only. */
  async providerEntries(providerId: string, q: { page?: number; limit?: number }): Promise<Paged<ProviderLedgerLine>> {
    const { page, limit, skip } = pageParams(q);
    const filter = { providerId: oid(providerId) };
    const [rows, total] = await Promise.all([
      this.entries.find(filter).sort({ createdAt: -1, _id: -1 }).skip(skip).limit(limit).lean() as Promise<EntryRow[]>,
      this.entries.countDocuments(filter),
    ]);
    const payoutIds = rows.filter((r) => r.kind === 'payout').map((r) => r._id);
    const voids = payoutIds.length ? ((await this.entries.find({ kind: 'payout_void', voidOf: { $in: payoutIds } }, { voidOf: 1 }).lean()) as { voidOf?: unknown }[]) : [];
    const voided = new Set(voids.map((v) => String(v.voidOf)));
    return { items: rows.map((r) => providerLine(r, voided)), total, page, limit };
  }
```

```ts
/** «سرو مجری» → «تسویه» (contract v4.3 «Provider endpoints»): read-only, always the caller's own provider (§64). */
@Controller('provider/ledger')
@UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard, RolesGuard, ProviderScopeGuard)
@Roles('provider')
export class ProviderLedgerController {
  constructor(private readonly reads: LedgerReadsService) {}

  @Get() summary(@CurrentProvider() providerId: string) { return this.reads.providerSummary(providerId); }

  @Get('entries') entries(@CurrentProvider() providerId: string, @Query() q: PageQueryDto) { return this.reads.providerEntries(providerId, q); }
}
```

```ts
// ── v4.3 ledger and settlement (contract «v4.3») ──────────────────────────
export type LedgerKind = 'credit' | 'adjustment' | 'payout' | 'payout_void'
export type LedgerState = 'counted' | 'pending'
export type CreditRole = 'production' | 'delivery'
export type CompCause = 'cancelled' | 'reassigned' | 'unassigned'
export interface LedgerSettings { settlementWeekday: number }
export interface LedgerBalance { available: number; due: number; earnedSinceCutoff: number; pendingCount: number }
export interface SettlementInfo { weekday: number; cutoff: string; next: string }
export interface BankAccountView { sheba: string; holder: string; bankName: string; updatedAt: string }
export interface CreditBasis { payoutBasis: number | null; suggested: number | null; missing: string[]; snapshotAt?: string; note?: string }
export interface LedgerEntryView {
  id: string; kind: LedgerKind; state: LedgerState; amount: number | null; title: string; providerId: string; providerName?: string
  orderId?: string; orderCode?: string; jobId?: string; role?: CreditRole; cause?: 'compensation' | 'manual'; compCause?: CompCause
  occurredAt: string; countedAt?: string; reason?: string; basis?: CreditBasis
  payout?: { paidAt: string; bankRef: string; note?: string; sheba?: string; holder?: string }
  voidOf?: string; voided: boolean; createdBy?: { id: string; name: string }; byRole: 'system' | 'admin'
  resolution?: { by: { id: string; name: string } | null; at: string; reason: string }; createdAt: string
}
export interface ProviderBalanceRow { providerId: string; name: string; businessName: string; status: ProviderStatus; balance: LedgerBalance; lastPayoutAt: string | null; hasBank: boolean }
export interface ProviderLedgerAdmin { provider: { id: string; name: string; businessName: string; status: ProviderStatus }; balance: LedgerBalance; bank: BankAccountView | null; settlement: SettlementInfo }
export interface WeeklyStatement { providerId: string; from: string; to: string; opening: number; earned: number; paid: number; closing: number; lines: LedgerEntryView[] }
export interface CompensationItem {
  jobId: string; orderId: string; orderCode: string; providerId: string; providerName: string
  cause: CompCause; stage: 'in_production' | 'after_production'; at: string; payoutBasis: number | null
}
export interface WalletRow { from: string; to: string; orders: number; charged: number; providerCost: number; courierTasks: number; courierCost: number; share: number }
export interface WalletReport { from: string; to: string; perTaskFee: number; rows: WalletRow[]; total: WalletRow }
export interface ProviderLedgerSelf {
  balance: { available: number; due: number; pendingCount: number }
  settlement: { weekday: number; next: string }
  bank: { sheba: string; holder: string } | null
}
export interface ProviderLedgerLine { id: string; kind: LedgerKind; state: LedgerState; title: string; orderCode?: string; at: string; amount: number | null; bankRef?: string; voided?: boolean }
```

```ts
import { jalali, money } from '@/lib/format'
import type { CompCause, LedgerKind } from '@/lib/types'

export const LEDGER_KIND_LABEL: Record<LedgerKind, string> = { credit: 'اعتبار سفارش', adjustment: 'اصلاحیه', payout: 'پرداخت تسویه', payout_void: 'ابطال پرداخت' }
export const LEDGER_KIND_TONE: Record<LedgerKind, BrandTone> = { credit: 'green', adjustment: 'violet', payout: 'blue', payout_void: 'amber' }
export const COMP_CAUSE_LABEL: Record<CompCause, string> = { cancelled: 'لغو سفارش', reassigned: 'تغییر مجری', unassigned: 'لغو تخصیص' }
export const COMP_STAGE_LABEL = { in_production: 'حین تولید', after_production: 'پس از تولید' } as const
export const PENDING_AMOUNT = 'نیازمند تعیین مبلغ'

/** «+۱۲٬۰۰۰ تومان» / «−۱۲٬۰۰۰ تومان». */
export function signedMoney(n: number): string {
  if (n < 0) return `−${money(-n)}`
  return n > 0 ? `+${money(n)}` : money(0)
}

/** A day in Tehran as `yyyy-mm-dd` (the API's date format). */
export function tehranYmd(d: Date = new Date()): string {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone: 'Asia/Tehran', year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit' }).format(d)
}
export function addDaysYmd(ymd: string, days: number): string {
  return tehranYmd(new Date(new Date(`${ymd}T12:00:00+03:30`).getTime() + days * 86_400_000))
}
/** A `yyyy-mm-dd` shown as a Jalali date. */
export const jalaliYmd = (ymd: string) => jalali(`${ymd}T12:00:00+03:30`)
/** One id per opened payout / adjustment form: a double submit returns the same entry (R13). */
export const newRequestId = () => crypto.randomUUID()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `lib/sheba.ts`** (mirror of the API rule — the API stays authoritative)

```ts
import { toEnDigits } from '@/lib/format'

const SHAPE = /^IR\d{24}$/

export function normalizeSheba(raw: string): string {
  const s = toEnDigits(raw).replace(/[\s\-‌]/g, '').toUpperCase()
  return /^\d{24}$/.test(s) ? `IR${s}` : s
}

export function shebaProblem(raw: string): string | null {
  const s = normalizeSheba(raw)
  if (!SHAPE.test(s)) return 'شماره شبا باید با IR شروع شود و ۲۴ رقم داشته باشد'
  let r = 0
  for (const ch of `${s.slice(4)}1827${s.slice(2, 4)}`) r = (r * 10 + Number(ch)) % 97
  return r === 1 ? null : 'شماره شبا معتبر نیست؛ رقم‌های آن را بررسی کنید'
}

/** `IR06 2960 0000 …` — always render inside `dir="ltr"`. */
export const groupSheba = (s: string) => s.replace(/(.{4})(?=.)/g, '$1 ')
```

- [ ] **Step 6: `features/admin/ledger-api.ts`**

```ts
import { keepPreviousData, useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { api } from '@/lib/api'
import type {
  BankAccountView, CompensationItem, LedgerEntryView, LedgerKind, Paged, ProviderBalanceRow, ProviderLedgerAdmin, WalletReport, WeeklyStatement,
} from '@/lib/types'

export interface EntriesParams { page: number; limit?: number; kind?: LedgerKind; from?: string; to?: string }
export interface PayoutBody { providerId: string; amount: number; paidAt: string; bankRef: string; note?: string; requestId: string }
export interface AdjustBody { providerId: string; amount: number; reason: string; orderCode?: string; requestId: string }
export interface CompensationBody { jobId: string; providerId: string; amount: number; reason: string }
export interface BankBody { providerId: string; sheba: string; holder: string; bankName?: string }

/** Admin ledger query keys — everything under `['admin', 'ledger']` so one invalidation refreshes every view. */
export const lk = {
  all: ['admin', 'ledger'] as const,
  balances: (page: number, q?: string) => ['admin', 'ledger', 'balances', page, q ?? ''] as const,
  provider: (id: string) => ['admin', 'ledger', 'provider', id] as const,
  entries: (id: string, p: EntriesParams) => ['admin', 'ledger', 'entries', id, p] as const,
  statement: (id: string, cutoff: string) => ['admin', 'ledger', 'statement', id, cutoff] as const,
  pending: (page: number) => ['admin', 'ledger', 'pending', page] as const,
  compensation: (page: number) => ['admin', 'ledger', 'compensation', page] as const,
  wallet: (from: string, to: string) => ['admin', 'ledger', 'wallet', from, to] as const,
}

export const useLedgerBalances = (page: number, q?: string) =>
  useQuery({ queryKey: lk.balances(page, q), placeholderData: keepPreviousData, queryFn: () => api.get<Paged<ProviderBalanceRow>>('/admin/ledger/balances', { page, limit: 20, q }) })
export const useProviderLedger = (id: string | undefined) =>
  useQuery({ queryKey: lk.provider(id ?? ''), enabled: !!id, queryFn: () => api.get<ProviderLedgerAdmin>(`/admin/ledger/providers/${id}`) })
export const useLedgerEntries = (id: string, p: EntriesParams) =>
  useQuery({ queryKey: lk.entries(id, p), placeholderData: keepPreviousData, queryFn: () => api.get<Paged<LedgerEntryView>>(`/admin/ledger/providers/${id}/entries`, { ...p, limit: p.limit ?? 10 }) })
export const useStatement = (id: string, cutoff: string) =>
  useQuery({ queryKey: lk.statement(id, cutoff), placeholderData: keepPreviousData, queryFn: () => api.get<WeeklyStatement>(`/admin/ledger/providers/${id}/statement`, { cutoff }) })
export const usePendingCredits = (page: number, enabled = true) =>
  useQuery({ queryKey: lk.pending(page), enabled, placeholderData: keepPreviousData, queryFn: () => api.get<Paged<LedgerEntryView>>('/admin/ledger/pending', { page, limit: 20 }) })
export const useCompensation = (page: number, enabled = true) =>
  useQuery({ queryKey: lk.compensation(page), enabled, placeholderData: keepPreviousData, queryFn: () => api.get<Paged<CompensationItem>>('/admin/ledger/compensation', { page, limit: 20 }) })
export const useWallet = (from: string, to: string, enabled = true) =>
  useQuery({ queryKey: lk.wallet(from, to), enabled, placeholderData: keepPreviousData, queryFn: () => api.get<WalletReport>('/admin/ledger/wallet', { from, to }) })

/** Every ledger write refreshes every ledger view (balances, statements, queues, the wallet report). */
export function useLedgerMutations() {
  const qc = useQueryClient()
  const refresh = () => qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: lk.all })
  return {
    payout: useMutation({ mutationFn: (b: PayoutBody) => api.post<LedgerEntryView>('/admin/ledger/payouts', b), onSuccess: refresh }),
    voidPayout: useMutation({ mutationFn: ({ id, reason }: { id: string; reason: string }) => api.post<LedgerEntryView>(`/admin/ledger/payouts/${id}/void`, { reason }), onSuccess: refresh }),
    adjust: useMutation({ mutationFn: (b: AdjustBody) => api.post<LedgerEntryView>('/admin/ledger/adjustments', b), onSuccess: refresh }),
    resolve: useMutation({ mutationFn: ({ id, amount, reason }: { id: string; amount: number; reason: string }) => api.post<LedgerEntryView>(`/admin/ledger/entries/${id}/resolve`, { amount, reason }), onSuccess: refresh }),
    compensate: useMutation({ mutationFn: (b: CompensationBody) => api.post<LedgerEntryView>('/admin/ledger/compensation', b), onSuccess: refresh }),
    bank: useMutation({ mutationFn: ({ providerId, ...b }: BankBody) => api.put<BankAccountView>(`/admin/ledger/providers/${providerId}/bank`, b), onSuccess: refresh }),
  }
}

/** «خروجی CSV» of «کیف پول دیجیتال سرو» (authenticated download). */
export async function downloadWalletCsv(from: string, to: string): Promise<void> {
  const { blob, filename } = await api.blob(`/admin/ledger/wallet/export?from=${from}&to=${to}`)
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob)
  const a = document.createElement('a')
  a.href = url
  a.download = filename || `sarv-wallet-${from}-${to}.csv`
  document.body.append(a)
  a.click()
  a.remove()
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 60_000)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Page shell** — `pages/ledger/LedgerPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useSearchParams } from 'react-router'
import { Chip, InfoBanner } from '@/components/brand'
import { fa } from '@/lib/format'
import { useCan } from '@/lib/permissions'
import { PageHeader } from '../../components/kit'
import { useCompensation, usePendingCredits } from '../../ledger-api'
import { BalancesTab } from './BalancesTab'
import { CompensationTab } from './CompensationTab'
import { PendingTab } from './PendingTab'
import { ProviderLedgerSheet } from './ProviderLedgerSheet'
import { WalletTab } from './WalletTab'

const TABS = [
  { key: 'balances', label: 'مانده مجریان' },
  { key: 'pending', label: 'نیازمند تعیین مبلغ' },
  { key: 'compensation', label: 'جبران خسارت مجری' },
  { key: 'wallet', label: 'کیف پول دیجیتال سرو' },
] as const
type Tab = (typeof TABS)[number]['key']

/** D31–D32 «تسویه مجریان»: what each provider earned, the weekly payouts and Digital Sarv's share. */
export function LedgerPage() {
  const canView = useCan('ledger_view')
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams()
  const tab: Tab = TABS.find((t) => t.key === params.get('tab'))?.key ?? 'balances'
  const providerId = params.get('provider') ?? undefined
  const pending = usePendingCredits(1, canView)
  const comp = useCompensation(1, canView)
  const set = (key: string, value?: string) =>
    setParams((p) => {
      const n = new URLSearchParams(p)
      if (value) n.set(key, value)
      else n.delete(key)
      return n
    }, { replace: true })
  const count = (n?: number) => (n ? ` (${fa(n)})` : '')

  if (!canView) return <InfoBanner>برای دیدن «تسویه مجریان» دسترسی «مالی و تسویه» لازم است.</InfoBanner>
  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader title="تسویه مجریان" subtitle="سهم هر مجری از سفارش‌های تحویل‌شده، پرداخت‌های هفتگی و سهم دیجیتال سرو" />
      <div className="no-scrollbar -mx-1 mb-4 flex gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto px-1 pb-1" role="tablist" aria-label="بخش">
        {TABS.map((t) => (
          <Chip key={t.key} size="sm" role="tab" aria-selected={tab === t.key} selected={tab === t.key} className="shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap"
            onClick={() => set('tab', t.key === 'balances' ? undefined : t.key)}>
            {t.label}{t.key === 'pending' ? count(pending.data?.total) : t.key === 'compensation' ? count(comp.data?.total) : ''}
          </Chip>
        ))}
      </div>
      {tab === 'balances' && <BalancesTab onOpen={(id) => set('provider', id)} />}
      {tab === 'pending' && <PendingTab onOpenProvider={(id) => set('provider', id)} />}
      {tab === 'compensation' && <CompensationTab />}
      {tab === 'wallet' && <WalletTab />}
      <ProviderLedgerSheet providerId={providerId} onClose={() => set('provider')} />
    </>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { TONES } from '@/components/brand'
import { fa, jalali, money } from '@/lib/format'
import { Tag } from '../../components/controls'
import { QueryView } from '../../components/kit'
import { AdminRow, AdminTable, Pager, SearchInput } from '../../components/table'
import { useDebounced } from '../../lib'
import { useLedgerBalances } from '../../ledger-api'

const COLUMNS = 'minmax(0,1.4fr) 150px 160px 130px 130px 110px'

export function BalancesTab({ onOpen }: { onOpen: (providerId: string) => void }) {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('')
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1)
  const q = useDebounced(search.trim())
  const query = useLedgerBalances(page, q || undefined)
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-3">
      <SearchInput value={search} onChange={(v) => { setSearch(v); setPage(1) }} placeholder="جستجوی مجری…" className="max-w-[360px]" />
      <QueryView query={query} rows={5} isEmpty={(d) => d.items.length === 0} empty={{ title: 'مجری‌ای پیدا نشد' }}>
        {(d) => (
          <AdminTable columns={COLUMNS} minWidth={880} head={['مجری', 'مانده حساب', 'قابل پرداخت این تسویه', 'نیازمند تعیین مبلغ', 'آخرین پرداخت', 'حساب بانکی']}
            footer={<Pager page={d.page} limit={d.limit} total={d.total} onPage={setPage} />}>
            {d.items.map((r) => (
              <AdminRow key={r.providerId} onClick={() => onOpen(r.providerId)}>
                <span className="min-w-0">
                  <span className="block truncate font-black">{r.name}</span>
                  {r.businessName && <span className="block truncate text-[11.5px] text-muted-2">{r.businessName}</span>}
                </span>
                <span className={r.balance.available < 0 ? 'font-black text-pink-dark' : 'font-black'}>{money(r.balance.available)}</span>
                <span className="font-bold">{money(r.balance.due)}</span>
                <span>{r.balance.pendingCount ? <Tag bg={TONES.amber.soft} fg={TONES.amber.ink}>{fa(r.balance.pendingCount)} مورد</Tag> : '—'}</span>
                <span className="text-[12.5px] text-muted-1">{r.lastPayoutAt ? jalali(r.lastPayoutAt) : '—'}</span>
                <span>{r.hasBank ? <Tag bg={TONES.green.soft} fg={TONES.green.ink}>ثبت شده</Tag> : <Tag bg={TONES.pink.soft} fg={TONES.pink.ink}>ثبت نشده</Tag>}</span>
              </AdminRow>
            ))}
          </AdminTable>
        )}
      </QueryView>
    </div>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { Banknote, PencilLine } from 'lucide-react'
import { ErrorState, LoadingBlock, TONES } from '@/components/brand'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Sheet, SheetContent, SheetDescription, SheetHeader, SheetTitle } from '@/components/ui/sheet'
import { fa, money } from '@/lib/format'
import { jalaliYmd } from '@/lib/ledger-labels'
import { useCan } from '@/lib/permissions'
import { PROVIDER_STATUS } from '@/lib/provider-labels'
import type { ProviderLedgerAdmin } from '@/lib/types'
import { Tag } from '../../components/controls'
import { useProviderLedger } from '../../ledger-api'
import { AdjustDialog } from './AdjustDialog'
import { BankCard } from './BankCard'
import { EntriesCard } from './EntriesCard'
import { PayoutDialog } from './PayoutDialog'
import { StatementCard } from './StatementCard'

export function ProviderLedgerSheet({ providerId, onClose }: { providerId?: string; onClose: () => void }) {
  return (
    <Sheet open={!!providerId} onOpenChange={(o) => !o && onClose()}>
      <SheetContent data-role="admin" side="right" className="w-full gap-0 overflow-y-auto border-line bg-shell p-0 sm:max-w-[640px]">
        {providerId && <Detail id={providerId} />}
      </SheetContent>
    </Sheet>
  )
}

function Detail({ id }: { id: string }) {
  const query = useProviderLedger(id)
  const canPay = useCan('payout_record')
  const canAdjust = useCan('ledger_adjust')
  const [payout, setPayout] = useState(false)
  const [adjust, setAdjust] = useState(false)
  const d = query.data
  const st = d ? PROVIDER_STATUS[d.provider.status] : undefined
  return (
    <>
      <SheetHeader className="border-b border-line bg-white px-5 pt-5 pb-4">
        <SheetTitle className="text-xl font-black">{d ? d.provider.name : 'مجری'}</SheetTitle>
        <SheetDescription className="text-[13px] text-muted-2">{d ? `تسویه بعدی: ${jalaliYmd(d.settlement.next)}` : 'در حال بارگذاری…'}</SheetDescription>
        {d && (
          <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            {st && <Tag bg={TONES[st.tone].soft} fg={TONES[st.tone].ink}>{st.label}</Tag>}
            {canPay && <Button size="sm" onClick={() => setPayout(true)}><Banknote className="size-4" />ثبت پرداخت</Button>}
            {canAdjust && <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={() => setAdjust(true)}><PencilLine className="size-4" />اصلاحیه</Button>}
          </div>
        )}
      </SheetHeader>
      <div className="grid gap-4 p-5">
        {query.isPending ? <LoadingBlock rows={4} /> : query.isError ? <ErrorState error={query.error} onRetry={() => void query.refetch()} /> : (
          <>
            <Kpis d={query.data} />
            <BankCard providerId={id} bank={query.data.bank} />
            <StatementCard providerId={id} cutoff={query.data.settlement.cutoff} />
            <EntriesCard providerId={id} />
          </>
        )}
      </div>
      {d && <PayoutDialog open={payout} onOpenChange={setPayout} account={d} />}
      {d && <AdjustDialog open={adjust} onOpenChange={setAdjust} providerId={id} />}
    </>
  )
}

function Kpis({ d }: { d: ProviderLedgerAdmin }) {
  const tiles = [
    { label: 'مانده حساب', value: money(d.balance.available), tone: d.balance.available < 0 ? TONES.pink : TONES.violet },
    { label: 'قابل پرداخت این تسویه', value: money(d.balance.due), tone: TONES.green },
    { label: 'نیازمند تعیین مبلغ', value: `${fa(d.balance.pendingCount)} مورد`, tone: TONES.amber },
  ]
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-2.5 sm:grid-cols-3">
      {tiles.map((t) => (
        <div key={t.label} className="rounded-[20px] p-4" style={{ background: t.tone.soft, color: t.tone.ink }}>
          <div className="text-[12.5px] opacity-80">{t.label}</div>
          <div className="mt-1 text-[20px] font-black">{t.value}</div>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Bank account** — `pages/ledger/BankCard.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { notify, toast } from '@/components/ui/sonner'
import { when } from '@/lib/format'
import { useCan } from '@/lib/permissions'
import { groupSheba, normalizeSheba, shebaProblem } from '@/lib/sheba'
import type { BankAccountView } from '@/lib/types'
import { Field, FormDialog } from '../../components/FormDialog'
import { AdminCard, CardNote, CardTitle } from '../../components/kit'
import { useLedgerMutations } from '../../ledger-api'

export function BankCard({ providerId, bank }: { providerId: string; bank: BankAccountView | null }) {
  const canEdit = useCan('payout_record')
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  return (
    <AdminCard>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <CardTitle>حساب بانکی</CardTitle>
        {canEdit && <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={() => setOpen(true)}>{bank ? 'ویرایش' : 'ثبت حساب'}</Button>}
      </div>
      {bank ? (
        <div className="mt-3 grid gap-1 text-[13px]">
          <div dir="ltr" className="text-start font-bold tracking-wide">{groupSheba(bank.sheba)}</div>
          <div className="text-muted-1">{bank.holder}{bank.bankName ? ` · ${bank.bankName}` : ''}</div>
          <div className="text-[11.5px] text-muted-2">آخرین تغییر: {when(bank.updatedAt)}</div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <CardNote>هنوز شماره شبایی ثبت نشده است؛ ثبت پرداخت بدون حساب بانکی ممکن نیست.</CardNote>
      )}
      {open && <BankForm providerId={providerId} bank={bank} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} />}
    </AdminCard>
  )
}

function BankForm({ providerId, bank, onClose }: { providerId: string; bank: BankAccountView | null; onClose: () => void }) {
  const { bank: save } = useLedgerMutations()
  const [sheba, setSheba] = useState(bank?.sheba ?? '')
  const [holder, setHolder] = useState(bank?.holder ?? '')
  const [bankName, setBankName] = useState(bank?.bankName ?? '')
  const problem = sheba.trim() ? shebaProblem(sheba) : null
  const submit = () => {
    const bad = shebaProblem(sheba)
    if (bad) return toast.error(bad)
    if (holder.trim().length < 2) return toast.error('نام صاحب حساب را وارد کنید')
    save.mutate({ providerId, sheba: normalizeSheba(sheba), holder: holder.trim(), bankName: bankName.trim() || undefined }, {
      onSuccess: () => { notify('حساب بانکی ذخیره شد'); onClose() },
    })
  }
  return (
    <FormDialog open onOpenChange={(o) => !o && onClose()} title="حساب بانکی مجری" description="پرداخت‌های تسویه به این حساب واریز می‌شود؛ تغییر آن با نام شما ثبت می‌شود." pending={save.isPending} onSubmit={submit}>
      <Field label="شماره شبا" hint={problem ? <span className="font-bold text-pink-dark">{problem}</span> : 'با IR و ۲۴ رقم؛ ارقام فارسی هم پذیرفته می‌شود'}>
        <Input dir="ltr" value={sheba} onChange={(e) => setSheba(e.target.value)} placeholder="IR…" maxLength={40} />
      </Field>
      <Field label="نام صاحب حساب"><Input value={holder} onChange={(e) => setHolder(e.target.value)} maxLength={80} /></Field>
      <Field label="نام بانک (اختیاری)"><Input value={bankName} onChange={(e) => setBankName(e.target.value)} maxLength={60} /></Field>
    </FormDialog>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Weekly statement** — `pages/ledger/StatementCard.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { money } from '@/lib/format'
import { addDaysYmd, jalaliYmd, signedMoney } from '@/lib/ledger-labels'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
import { AdminCard, CardNote, CardTitle, QueryView } from '../../components/kit'
import { useStatement } from '../../ledger-api'

export function StatementCard({ providerId, cutoff }: { providerId: string; cutoff: string }) {
  const [at, setAt] = useState(cutoff)
  const query = useStatement(providerId, at)
  return (
    <AdminCard>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <CardTitle>صورت‌حساب هفتگی</CardTitle>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <Button size="icon-sm" variant="outline" aria-label="هفته قبل" onClick={() => setAt(addDaysYmd(at, -7))}><ChevronRight className="size-4" /></Button>
          <Button size="icon-sm" variant="outline" aria-label="هفته بعد" disabled={at >= cutoff} onClick={() => setAt(addDaysYmd(at, 7))}><ChevronLeft className="size-4" /></Button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <QueryView query={query} rows={3}>
        {(s) => (
          <>
            <div className="mt-1 text-[12.5px] text-muted-2">{jalaliYmd(s.from)} تا {jalaliYmd(s.to)}</div>
            <div className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-4">
              <Stat label="مانده ابتدای هفته" value={money(s.opening)} />
              <Stat label="کارکرد هفته" value={signedMoney(s.earned)} />
              <Stat label="پرداخت‌شده" value={money(s.paid)} />
              <Stat label="مانده پایان هفته" value={money(s.closing)} strong />
            </div>
            {s.lines.length === 0 ? <CardNote>در این هفته ردیفی ثبت نشده است.</CardNote> : (
              <div className="mt-3 grid gap-1.5">
                {s.lines.map((l) => (
                  <div key={l.id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 border-t border-line-soft pt-1.5 text-[13px]">
                    <span className="min-w-0 truncate">{l.title}</span>
                    <span className="shrink-0 font-bold">{signedMoney(l.amount ?? 0)}</span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </QueryView>
    </AdminCard>
  )
}

function Stat({ label, value, strong }: { label: string; value: string; strong?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div className={cn('rounded-[16px] bg-field px-3 py-2.5', strong && 'bg-accent-soft text-accent-soft-ink')}>
      <div className="text-[11.5px] text-muted-2">{label}</div>
      <div className="mt-0.5 text-[14px] font-black">{value}</div>
    </div>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Entries with void** — `pages/ledger/EntriesCard.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { Chip, TONES } from '@/components/brand'
import { notify } from '@/components/ui/sonner'
import { fa, money, when } from '@/lib/format'
import { LEDGER_KIND_LABEL, LEDGER_KIND_TONE, PENDING_AMOUNT, signedMoney } from '@/lib/ledger-labels'
import { useCan } from '@/lib/permissions'
import type { LedgerEntryView, LedgerKind } from '@/lib/types'
import { Tag } from '../../components/controls'
import { AdminCard, CardTitle, QueryView } from '../../components/kit'
import { Pager } from '../../components/table'
import { useLedgerEntries, useLedgerMutations } from '../../ledger-api'
import { ReasonDialog } from '../dispatch/ReasonDialog'

const KINDS: (LedgerKind | 'all')[] = ['all', 'credit', 'adjustment', 'payout', 'payout_void']

export function EntriesCard({ providerId }: { providerId: string }) {
  const [kind, setKind] = useState<LedgerKind | 'all'>('all')
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1)
  const query = useLedgerEntries(providerId, { page, kind: kind === 'all' ? undefined : kind })
  const canVoid = useCan('payout_record')
  const { voidPayout } = useLedgerMutations()
  const [voiding, setVoiding] = useState<LedgerEntryView | null>(null)
  return (
    <AdminCard>
      <CardTitle>گردش حساب</CardTitle>
      <div className="no-scrollbar -mx-1 mt-3 flex gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto px-1 pb-1">
        {KINDS.map((k) => (
          <Chip key={k} size="sm" selected={kind === k} onClick={() => { setKind(k); setPage(1) }} className="shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap">
            {k === 'all' ? 'همه' : LEDGER_KIND_LABEL[k]}
          </Chip>
        ))}
      </div>
      <QueryView query={query} rows={4} isEmpty={(d) => d.items.length === 0} empty={{ title: 'ردیفی ثبت نشده است' }}>
        {(d) => (
          <>
            <div className="mt-3 grid gap-2">
              {d.items.map((e) => <EntryLine key={e.id} e={e} onVoid={canVoid && e.kind === 'payout' && !e.voided ? () => setVoiding(e) : undefined} />)}
            </div>
            <Pager page={d.page} limit={d.limit} total={d.total} onPage={setPage} />
          </>
        )}
      </QueryView>
      <ReasonDialog
        open={!!voiding}
        onOpenChange={(o) => !o && setVoiding(null)}
        title="ابطال پرداخت"
        description={voiding ? `${money(-(voiding.amount ?? 0))} · پیگیری ${fa(voiding.payout?.bankRef ?? '')} — مبلغ به مانده مجری برمی‌گردد` : undefined}
        submitLabel="ابطال پرداخت"
        pending={voidPayout.isPending}
        onSubmit={(reason) => {
          if (!voiding) return
          voidPayout.mutate({ id: voiding.id, reason }, { onSuccess: () => { notify('پرداخت باطل شد'); setVoiding(null) } })
        }}
      />
    </AdminCard>
  )
}

function EntryLine({ e, onVoid }: { e: LedgerEntryView; onVoid?: () => void }) {
  const tone = TONES[LEDGER_KIND_TONE[e.kind]]
  return (
    <div className="rounded-[16px] border border-line-soft p-3">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <Tag bg={tone.soft} fg={tone.ink}>{LEDGER_KIND_LABEL[e.kind]}</Tag>
        <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-[13.5px] font-bold">{e.title}</span>
        <span className={e.state === 'pending' ? 'font-bold text-amber-dark' : 'font-black'}>{e.state === 'pending' ? PENDING_AMOUNT : signedMoney(e.amount ?? 0)}</span>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-1 text-[11.5px] leading-6 text-muted-2">
        {when(e.occurredAt)}
        {e.createdBy?.name ? ` · ${e.createdBy.name}` : ''}
        {e.payout ? ` · پیگیری ${fa(e.payout.bankRef)}` : ''}
        {e.reason ? ` · ${e.reason}` : ''}
        {e.voided && <span className="ms-1 font-bold text-pink-dark">· باطل شد</span>}
      </div>
      {onVoid && <button type="button" onClick={onVoid} className="mt-1 cursor-pointer text-[12px] font-extrabold text-pink-dark">ابطال پرداخت</button>}
    </div>
  )
}
```
(`text-amber-dark` is a palette token in `src/index.css`.)

- [ ] **Step 7: Payout dialog** — `pages/ledger/PayoutDialog.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea'
import { notify, toast } from '@/components/ui/sonner'
import { money, toEnDigits } from '@/lib/format'
import { newRequestId, tehranYmd } from '@/lib/ledger-labels'
import { groupSheba } from '@/lib/sheba'
import type { ProviderLedgerAdmin } from '@/lib/types'
import { NumInput } from '../../components/controls'
import { Field, FormDialog } from '../../components/FormDialog'
import { CardNote } from '../../components/kit'
import { useLedgerMutations } from '../../ledger-api'

export function PayoutDialog({ open, onOpenChange, account }: { open: boolean; onOpenChange: (o: boolean) => void; account: ProviderLedgerAdmin }) {
  return open ? <PayoutForm onClose={() => onOpenChange(false)} account={account} /> : null
}

function PayoutForm({ onClose, account }: { onClose: () => void; account: ProviderLedgerAdmin }) {
  const { payout } = useLedgerMutations()
  const [requestId] = useState(newRequestId)
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(Math.max(0, account.balance.due))
  const [paidAt, setPaidAt] = useState(() => tehranYmd())
  const [bankRef, setBankRef] = useState('')
  const [note, setNote] = useState('')
  const max = account.balance.available
  const submit = () => {
    if (!account.bank) return toast.error('ابتدا شماره شبای مجری را ثبت کنید')
    if (amount < 1) return toast.error('مبلغ پرداخت را وارد کنید')
    if (amount > max) return toast.error('مبلغ پرداخت از مانده حساب مجری بیشتر است')
    if (!bankRef.trim()) return toast.error('شماره پیگیری بانکی را وارد کنید')
    payout.mutate(
      { providerId: account.provider.id, amount, paidAt, bankRef: toEnDigits(bankRef.trim()), note: note.trim() || undefined, requestId },
      { onSuccess: () => { notify('پرداخت ثبت شد'); onClose() } },
    )
  }
  return (
    <FormDialog open onOpenChange={(o) => !o && onClose()} title="ثبت پرداخت تسویه" submitLabel="ثبت پرداخت" pending={payout.isPending} onSubmit={submit}
      description={`${account.provider.name} · مانده ${money(max)} · قابل پرداخت این تسویه ${money(account.balance.due)}`}>
      <Field label="مبلغ (تومان)"><NumInput value={amount} onValueChange={setAmount} aria-label="مبلغ پرداخت" className="w-full" /></Field>
      <Field label="تاریخ واریز"><Input type="date" dir="ltr" value={paidAt} max={tehranYmd()} onChange={(e) => setPaidAt(e.target.value)} /></Field>
      <Field label="شماره پیگیری بانکی"><Input dir="ltr" value={bankRef} maxLength={60} onChange={(e) => setBankRef(e.target.value)} /></Field>
      <Field label="یادداشت (اختیاری، فقط برای مدیران)"><Textarea rows={2} value={note} maxLength={300} onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)} /></Field>
      {account.bank
        ? <CardNote>واریز به {account.bank.holder} — <span dir="ltr">{groupSheba(account.bank.sheba)}</span></CardNote>
        : <CardNote className="font-bold text-pink-dark">حساب بانکی این مجری ثبت نشده است.</CardNote>}
    </FormDialog>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Adjustment dialog** — `pages/ledger/AdjustDialog.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { Chip } from '@/components/brand'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea'
import { notify, toast } from '@/components/ui/sonner'
import { toEnDigits } from '@/lib/format'
import { newRequestId } from '@/lib/ledger-labels'
import { NumInput } from '../../components/controls'
import { Field, FormDialog } from '../../components/FormDialog'
import { useLedgerMutations } from '../../ledger-api'

export function AdjustDialog({ open, onOpenChange, providerId }: { open: boolean; onOpenChange: (o: boolean) => void; providerId: string }) {
  return open ? <AdjustForm onClose={() => onOpenChange(false)} providerId={providerId} /> : null
}

function AdjustForm({ onClose, providerId }: { onClose: () => void; providerId: string }) {
  const { adjust } = useLedgerMutations()
  const [requestId] = useState(newRequestId)
  const [sign, setSign] = useState<1 | -1>(-1)
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(0)
  const [orderCode, setOrderCode] = useState('')
  const [reason, setReason] = useState('')
  const submit = () => {
    if (amount < 1) return toast.error('مبلغ اصلاحیه نمی‌تواند صفر باشد')
    if (!reason.trim()) return toast.error('دلیل را بنویسید')
    adjust.mutate(
      { providerId, amount: sign * amount, reason: reason.trim(), orderCode: toEnDigits(orderCode.trim()) || undefined, requestId },
      { onSuccess: () => { notify('اصلاحیه ثبت شد'); onClose() } },
    )
  }
  return (
    <FormDialog open onOpenChange={(o) => !o && onClose()} title="اصلاحیه حساب مجری" submitLabel="ثبت اصلاحیه" pending={adjust.isPending} onSubmit={submit}
      description="مثلاً مرجوعی پس از تحویل. مبلغ ثبت‌شده ویرایش نمی‌شود؛ اصلاح بعدی هم یک اصلاحیه است.">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
        <Chip size="sm" selected={sign === 1} onClick={() => setSign(1)}>افزایش سهم مجری</Chip>
        <Chip size="sm" selected={sign === -1} onClick={() => setSign(-1)}>کاهش سهم مجری</Chip>
      </div>
      <Field label="مبلغ (تومان)"><NumInput value={amount} onValueChange={setAmount} aria-label="مبلغ اصلاحیه" className="w-full" /></Field>
      <Field label="کد سفارش (اختیاری)"><Input inputMode="numeric" value={orderCode} onChange={(e) => setOrderCode(e.target.value)} maxLength={20} /></Field>
      <Field label="دلیل (در حساب ثبت می‌شود)"><Textarea rows={3} value={reason} maxLength={300} onChange={(e) => setReason(e.target.value)} /></Field>
    </FormDialog>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: `AmountDialog.tsx`**
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea'
import { toast } from '@/components/ui/sonner'
import { money } from '@/lib/format'
import { NumInput } from '../../components/controls'
import { Field, FormDialog } from '../../components/FormDialog'

/** Amount + reason (pending credits, compensation). `max` = the payout basis cap (D30); null/undefined = no cap. */
export function AmountDialog({ title, description, initial, max, submitLabel, pending, onSubmit, onClose }: {
  title: string; description?: string; initial: number; max?: number | null; submitLabel: string; pending?: boolean
  onSubmit: (amount: number, reason: string) => void; onClose: () => void
}) {
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(Math.max(0, initial))
  const [reason, setReason] = useState('')
  const submit = () => {
    if (max != null && amount > max) return toast.error(`مبلغ نمی‌تواند از ${money(max)} بیشتر باشد`)
    if (!reason.trim()) return toast.error('دلیل را بنویسید')
    onSubmit(amount, reason.trim())
  }
  return (
    <FormDialog open onOpenChange={(o) => !o && onClose()} title={title} description={description} submitLabel={submitLabel} pending={pending} onSubmit={submit}>
      <Field label="مبلغ (تومان)" hint={max != null ? `حداکثر ${money(max)}` : 'صفر هم قابل ثبت است'}>
        <NumInput value={amount} onValueChange={setAmount} aria-label="مبلغ" className="w-full" />
      </Field>
      <Field label="دلیل (در حساب ثبت می‌شود)"><Textarea rows={3} value={reason} maxLength={300} onChange={(e) => setReason(e.target.value)} /></Field>
    </FormDialog>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: `PendingTab.tsx`**
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { InfoBanner } from '@/components/brand'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { notify } from '@/components/ui/sonner'
import { fa, jalali, money } from '@/lib/format'
import { useCan } from '@/lib/permissions'
import type { LedgerEntryView } from '@/lib/types'
import { QueryView } from '../../components/kit'
import { AdminRow, AdminTable, Pager } from '../../components/table'
import { useLedgerMutations, usePendingCredits } from '../../ledger-api'
import { AmountDialog } from './AmountDialog'

const COLUMNS = '120px minmax(0,1.1fr) minmax(0,1.8fr) 140px 120px'

/** D28 «نیازمند تعیین مبلغ»: delivered work without a provider price — not in the balance until an admin sets the amount. */
export function PendingTab({ onOpenProvider }: { onOpenProvider: (providerId: string) => void }) {
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1)
  const query = usePendingCredits(page)
  const canAdjust = useCan('ledger_adjust')
  const { resolve } = useLedgerMutations()
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<LedgerEntryView | null>(null)
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-3">
      <InfoBanner>این سفارش‌ها تحویل شده‌اند اما قیمت مجری برای همه اقلامشان ثبت نبود؛ تا مبلغ را تعیین نکنید در مانده مجری حساب نمی‌شوند.</InfoBanner>
      <QueryView query={query} rows={4} isEmpty={(d) => d.items.length === 0} empty={{ title: 'موردی برای تعیین مبلغ نیست' }}>
        {(d) => (
          <AdminTable columns={COLUMNS} minWidth={900} head={['سفارش', 'مجری', 'اقلام بدون قیمت', 'مبلغ پیشنهادی', '']}
            footer={<Pager page={d.page} limit={d.limit} total={d.total} onPage={setPage} />}>
            {d.items.map((e) => (
              <AdminRow key={e.id}>
                <span>
                  <span className="block font-black">{e.orderCode ? fa(e.orderCode) : '—'}</span>
                  <span className="block text-[11.5px] text-muted-2">{e.role === 'delivery' ? 'ارسال' : 'تولید'} · {jalali(e.occurredAt)}</span>
                </span>
                <button type="button" className="min-w-0 cursor-pointer truncate text-start font-bold hover:text-accent" onClick={() => onOpenProvider(e.providerId)}>{e.providerName ?? '—'}</button>
                <span className="min-w-0 text-[12px] leading-6 text-muted-1">{[...(e.basis?.missing ?? []), e.basis?.note].filter(Boolean).join('، ') || '—'}</span>
                <span className="font-bold">{e.basis?.suggested != null ? money(e.basis.suggested) : '—'}</span>
                <span>{canAdjust && <Button size="sm" onClick={() => setEditing(e)}>تعیین مبلغ</Button>}</span>
              </AdminRow>
            ))}
          </AdminTable>
        )}
      </QueryView>
      {editing && (
        <AmountDialog title={`تعیین مبلغ — ${editing.title}`} description={editing.providerName} initial={editing.basis?.suggested ?? 0} submitLabel="ثبت مبلغ" pending={resolve.isPending}
          onClose={() => setEditing(null)}
          onSubmit={(amount, reason) => resolve.mutate({ id: editing.id, amount, reason }, { onSuccess: () => { notify('مبلغ ثبت شد'); setEditing(null) } })} />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `CompensationTab.tsx`**
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { InfoBanner, TONES } from '@/components/brand'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { notify } from '@/components/ui/sonner'
import { fa, jalali, money } from '@/lib/format'
import { COMP_CAUSE_LABEL, COMP_STAGE_LABEL } from '@/lib/ledger-labels'
import { useCan } from '@/lib/permissions'
import { NO_PROVIDER_PRICE } from '@/lib/provider-labels'
import type { CompensationItem } from '@/lib/types'
import { Tag } from '../../components/controls'
import { QueryView } from '../../components/kit'
import { AdminRow, AdminTable, Pager } from '../../components/table'
import { useCompensation, useLedgerMutations } from '../../ledger-api'
import { AmountDialog } from './AmountDialog'

const COLUMNS = '110px minmax(0,1.2fr) 130px 110px 160px 110px'

/** D30 «جبران خسارت مجری»: work lost during / after production — the admin decides 0, part or all (≤ the payout basis). */
export function CompensationTab() {
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1)
  const query = useCompensation(page)
  const canAdjust = useCan('ledger_adjust')
  const { compensate } = useLedgerMutations()
  const [deciding, setDeciding] = useState<CompensationItem | null>(null)
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-3">
      <InfoBanner>سفارش‌هایی که حین یا پس از تولید لغو شدند یا از مجری گرفته شدند. مبلغ صفر هم یک تصمیم است و مورد را می‌بندد.</InfoBanner>
      <QueryView query={query} rows={4} isEmpty={(d) => d.items.length === 0} empty={{ title: 'موردی برای جبران خسارت نیست' }}>
        {(d) => (
          <AdminTable columns={COLUMNS} minWidth={900} head={['سفارش', 'مجری', 'علت', 'مرحله', 'سهم قابل پرداخت', '']}
            footer={<Pager page={d.page} limit={d.limit} total={d.total} onPage={setPage} />}>
            {d.items.map((c) => (
              <AdminRow key={`${c.jobId}-${c.providerId}`}>
                <span><span className="block font-black">{fa(c.orderCode)}</span><span className="block text-[11.5px] text-muted-2">{jalali(c.at)}</span></span>
                <span className="min-w-0 truncate font-bold">{c.providerName || '—'}</span>
                <span><Tag bg={TONES.pink.soft} fg={TONES.pink.ink}>{COMP_CAUSE_LABEL[c.cause]}</Tag></span>
                <span className="text-[12.5px]">{COMP_STAGE_LABEL[c.stage]}</span>
                <span className="font-bold">{c.payoutBasis != null ? money(c.payoutBasis) : <span className="text-muted-2">{NO_PROVIDER_PRICE}</span>}</span>
                <span>{canAdjust && <Button size="sm" onClick={() => setDeciding(c)}>تصمیم</Button>}</span>
              </AdminRow>
            ))}
          </AdminTable>
        )}
      </QueryView>
      {deciding && (
        <AmountDialog title={`جبران خسارت — سفارش ${fa(deciding.orderCode)}`} description={`${deciding.providerName} · ${COMP_CAUSE_LABEL[deciding.cause]} (${COMP_STAGE_LABEL[deciding.stage]})`}
          initial={deciding.payoutBasis ?? 0} max={deciding.payoutBasis} submitLabel="ثبت تصمیم" pending={compensate.isPending} onClose={() => setDeciding(null)}
          onSubmit={(amount, reason) => compensate.mutate(
            { jobId: deciding.jobId, providerId: deciding.providerId, amount, reason },
            { onSuccess: () => { notify('تصمیم جبران خسارت ثبت شد'); setDeciding(null) } },
          )} />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `WalletTab.tsx`**
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { Download } from 'lucide-react'
import { TONES } from '@/components/brand'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { toast } from '@/components/ui/sonner'
import { fa, money } from '@/lib/format'
import { addDaysYmd, jalaliYmd, tehranYmd } from '@/lib/ledger-labels'
import type { WalletRow } from '@/lib/types'
import { Field } from '../../components/FormDialog'
import { AdminCard, CardNote, QueryView } from '../../components/kit'
import { AdminRow, AdminTable } from '../../components/table'
import { downloadWalletCsv, useWallet } from '../../ledger-api'

const COLUMNS = 'minmax(0,1.3fr) 90px 160px 150px 160px 160px'
const days = (from: string, to: string) => Math.round((new Date(`${to}T12:00:00+03:30`).getTime() - new Date(`${from}T12:00:00+03:30`).getTime()) / 86_400_000) + 1

/** D32 «کیف پول دیجیتال سرو»: per settlement week, customers' payments − provider shares − courier pay = Digital Sarv's share. */
export function WalletTab() {
  const [from, setFrom] = useState(() => addDaysYmd(tehranYmd(), -27))
  const [to, setTo] = useState(() => tehranYmd())
  const problem = !from || !to ? 'بازه را انتخاب کنید' : to < from ? 'تاریخ پایان نباید قبل از تاریخ شروع باشد' : days(from, to) > 366 ? 'بازه گزارش حداکثر ۳۶۶ روز است' : null
  const query = useWallet(from, to, !problem)
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false)
  const exportCsv = async () => {
    setBusy(true)
    try {
      await downloadWalletCsv(from, to)
    } catch (e) {
      toast.error(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'دریافت فایل ممکن نشد')
    } finally {
      setBusy(false)
    }
  }
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-4">
      <AdminCard>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
          <Field label="از تاریخ"><Input type="date" dir="ltr" value={from} max={to} onChange={(e) => setFrom(e.target.value)} /></Field>
          <Field label="تا تاریخ"><Input type="date" dir="ltr" value={to} max={tehranYmd()} onChange={(e) => setTo(e.target.value)} /></Field>
          <Button variant="outline" disabled={!!problem || busy} onClick={() => void exportCsv()}><Download className="size-4" />خروجی CSV</Button>
        </div>
        {problem && <div className="mt-2 text-[12.5px] font-bold text-pink-dark">{problem}</div>}
      </AdminCard>
      {!problem && (
        <QueryView query={query} rows={4}>
          {(r) => (
            <>
              <div className="grid gap-2.5 sm:grid-cols-4">
                <Kpi label="دریافتی از مشتریان" value={money(r.total.charged)} tone="blue" />
                <Kpi label="سهم مجریان" value={money(r.total.providerCost)} tone="violet" />
                <Kpi label="دستمزد پیک (برآورد)" value={money(r.total.courierCost)} tone="amber" />
                <Kpi label="سهم دیجیتال سرو" value={money(r.total.share)} tone={r.total.share < 0 ? 'pink' : 'green'} />
              </div>
              <AdminTable columns={COLUMNS} minWidth={940} head={['هفته', 'سفارش‌ها', 'دریافتی از مشتریان', 'سهم مجریان', 'دستمزد پیک (برآورد)', 'سهم دیجیتال سرو']}>
                {r.rows.map((w) => <Row key={w.from} w={w} />)}
                <Row w={r.total} total />
              </AdminTable>
              <CardNote>
                دستمزد پیک برآوردی است: تعداد کارهای پیک (جمع‌آوری از منزل و تحویل) × دستمزد فعلی هر کار ({money(r.perTaskFee)})؛ پاداش‌ها حساب نمی‌شوند.
                پرداخت‌های تسویه هزینه نیستند و فقط بدهی به مجری را کم می‌کنند.
              </CardNote>
            </>
          )}
        </QueryView>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function Row({ w, total }: { w: WalletRow; total?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <AdminRow className={total ? 'bg-field font-black' : undefined}>
      <span>{total ? 'جمع' : `${jalaliYmd(w.from)} تا ${jalaliYmd(w.to)}`}</span>
      <span>{fa(w.orders)}</span>
      <span>{money(w.charged)}</span>
      <span>{money(w.providerCost)}</span>
      <span>{money(w.courierCost)}</span>
      <span className={w.share < 0 ? 'font-black text-pink-dark' : 'font-black'}>{money(w.share)}</span>
    </AdminRow>
  )
}

function Kpi({ label, value, tone }: { label: string; value: string; tone: keyof typeof TONES }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-[20px] p-4" style={{ background: TONES[tone].soft, color: TONES[tone].ink }}>
      <div className="text-[12.5px] opacity-80">{label}</div>
      <div className="mt-1 text-[20px] font-black">{value}</div>
    </div>
  )
}
```
(`money` of a negative share prints «−» via `Intl`; Persian digits throughout.)

### Task 18: «سرو مجری» «تسویه» tab, and non-print provider prices in both apps (Web; D29, D32)

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/features/provider/SettlementScreen.tsx`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/features/provider/{routes.tsx, ProviderShell.tsx, api.ts, PricesScreen.tsx}`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/features/admin/pricing-api.ts`, `apps/web/src/features/admin/pages/pricing/{ProviderLayerTab.tsx, BulkDialog.tsx}`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `useProviderSettlement`, `useProviderLedgerEntries` (Task 15), `PRICE_SERVICE_LABEL`, `PRINT_PRICE_SERVICES`, `PROVIDER_ONLY_SERVICES` (Task 15), `PriceComboService`.
- Produces: route `/provider/settlement`, the 5th provider tab «تسویه»; provider price lists and the admin «قیمت مجریان» tab list the D29 services.

- [ ] **Step 1: `SettlementScreen.tsx`**
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { Wallet } from 'lucide-react'
import { Chip, EmptyState, InfoBanner, LoadingBlock, MobileHeader, Panel, ScreenBody, ToneTag } from '@/components/brand'
import { fa, money, when } from '@/lib/format'
import { jalaliYmd, PENDING_AMOUNT, signedMoney } from '@/lib/ledger-labels'
import { groupSheba } from '@/lib/sheba'
import type { ProviderLedgerLine, ProviderLedgerSelf } from '@/lib/types'
import { useProviderLedgerEntries, useProviderSettlement } from './ledger-api'
import { ProviderError } from './ProviderError'

/** D32 «تسویه»: the provider's own balance, next settlement, bank account and statement lines — never customer prices or margins. */
export function SettlementScreen() {
  const summary = useProviderSettlement()
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1)
  const entries = useProviderLedgerEntries(page)
  const pages = entries.data ? Math.max(1, Math.ceil(entries.data.total / entries.data.limit)) : 1
  return (
    <>
      <MobileHeader title="تسویه" subtitle="مانده حساب و پرداخت‌های هفتگی دیجیتال سرو" icon={Wallet} tone="cyan" />
      <ScreenBody>
        {summary.isPending ? <LoadingBlock rows={3} /> : summary.isError ? <ProviderError error={summary.error} onRetry={() => void summary.refetch()} /> : <Summary s={summary.data} />}
        <div className="mt-4 mb-2 text-[13.5px] font-black">گردش حساب</div>
        {entries.isPending ? <LoadingBlock rows={4} />
          : entries.isError ? <ProviderError error={entries.error} onRetry={() => void entries.refetch()} />
          : entries.data.items.length === 0 ? <EmptyState title="هنوز ردیفی ثبت نشده است" hint="با تحویل هر سفارش، سهم شما این‌جا ثبت می‌شود." />
          : (
            <>
              <div className="grid gap-2">{entries.data.items.map((l) => <Line key={l.id} l={l} />)}</div>
              {pages > 1 && (
                <div className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                  <Chip size="sm" disabled={page <= 1} onClick={() => setPage((p) => p - 1)}>قبلی</Chip>
                  <span className="text-[12px] text-muted-2">صفحه {fa(page)} از {fa(pages)}</span>
                  <Chip size="sm" disabled={page >= pages} onClick={() => setPage((p) => p + 1)}>بعدی</Chip>
                </div>
              )}
            </>
          )}
      </ScreenBody>
    </>
  )
}

function Summary({ s }: { s: ProviderLedgerSelf }) {
  return (
    <>
      <div className="rounded-[24px] bg-night p-5 text-white">
        <div className="text-[12.5px] text-[#9aa2b8]">قابل پرداخت در تسویه {jalaliYmd(s.settlement.next)}</div>
        <div className="mt-1 text-[22px] font-black">{money(s.balance.due)}</div>
        <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-[12.5px] text-[#9aa2b8]">
          <span>مانده کل: <b className="text-white">{money(s.balance.available)}</b></span>
          {s.balance.pendingCount > 0 && <span>{fa(s.balance.pendingCount)} سفارش {PENDING_AMOUNT}</span>}
        </div>
      </div>
      {s.bank ? (
        <Panel className="mt-3">
          <div className="text-[12.5px] text-muted-2">واریز به حساب</div>
          <div dir="ltr" className="mt-1 text-start text-[13.5px] font-bold">{groupSheba(s.bank.sheba)}</div>
          <div className="text-[12.5px] text-muted-1">{s.bank.holder}</div>
        </Panel>
      ) : (
        <InfoBanner className="mt-3">حساب بانکی شما هنوز ثبت نشده است؛ برای ثبت شماره شبا با پشتیبانی دیجیتال سرو تماس بگیرید.</InfoBanner>
      )}
    </>
  )
}

function Line({ l }: { l: ProviderLedgerLine }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-[20px] border border-line bg-white p-3.5">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <span className="min-w-0 truncate text-[13.5px] font-bold">{l.title}</span>
        <span className={l.state === 'pending' ? 'shrink-0 text-[12.5px] font-bold text-amber-dark' : 'shrink-0 text-[13.5px] font-black'}>
          {l.state === 'pending' ? PENDING_AMOUNT : signedMoney(l.amount ?? 0)}
        </span>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-[11.5px] text-muted-2">
        <span>{when(l.at)}</span>
        {l.bankRef && <span>پیگیری {fa(l.bankRef)}</span>}
        {l.voided && <ToneTag tone="pink">باطل شد</ToneTag>}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
```

### Task 19: End-to-end tests, full verification, docs (API + Web)

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/api/test/app.e2e-spec.ts`, `docs/plan.md`, `CLAUDE.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything above; the e2e helpers `http`, `login`, `tokens`, `authH`, `placeCod`, `testProviders`, `jobOf` (M1–M3).

- [ ] **Step 1: Append to `apps/api/test/app.e2e-spec.ts`** (inside the main `describe`, before «protects admin routes»):

```ts
  // ---- v4.3 ledger and settlement (D26–D33) ---------------------------------------------------------------
  const docsSvc = (ink: 'bw' | 'color') => ({ kind: 'docs', spec: { pages: 20, scope: 'all', ink, sides: 'single', copies: 1 } });
  const tehranToday = () => new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone: 'Asia/Tehran' }).format(new Date());
  const ymdDaysAgo = (n: number) => new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone: 'Asia/Tehran' }).format(new Date(Date.now() - n * 86_400_000));
  const balanceOf = async (admin: string, providerId: string) => (await http().get(`/api/admin/ledger/providers/${providerId}`).set(authH(admin)).expect(200)).body.balance;
  const setProviderPrice = (admin: string, providerId: string, comboKey: string, price: number) =>
    http().post('/api/admin/price-book/versions').set(authH(admin))
      .send({ layer: 'provider', providerId, comboKey, mode: 'manual', price, publish: true, reason: 'e2e ledger', confirmNegativeMargin: true }).expect(201);
  /** D produces the order's job (accept → production → QC → ready); the admin hands it over (COD becomes paid, the job is delivered). */
  async function produceAndDeliver(admin: string, P: Record<string, { id: string; phone: string }>, orderId: string, confirmMissingPrice = false) {
    const job = await jobOf(admin, orderId);
    await http().post(`/api/admin/dispatch/jobs/${job.id}/assign`).set(authH(admin)).send({ providerId: P.D.id, ...(confirmMissingPrice ? { confirmMissingPrice: true } : {}) }).expect(200);
    const d = await login(P.D.phone);
    await http().post(`/api/provider/jobs/${job.id}/accept`).set(authH(d)).expect(200);
    await http().post(`/api/provider/jobs/${job.id}/status`).set(authH(d)).send({ status: 'in_production' }).expect(200);
    const detail = (await http().get(`/api/provider/jobs/${job.id}`).set(authH(d)).expect(200)).body;
    for (let i = 0; i < detail.qc.labels.length; i++) await http().patch(`/api/provider/jobs/${job.id}/qc`).set(authH(d)).send({ index: i, done: true }).expect(200);
    await http().post(`/api/provider/jobs/${job.id}/status`).set(authH(d)).send({ status: 'ready' }).expect(200);
    await http().post(`/api/admin/dispatch/jobs/${job.id}/status`).set(authH(admin)).send({ status: 'delivered', reason: 'e2e ledger' }).expect(200);
    return job.id as string;
  }

  it('D29 + D27: a thesis is priced from the provider’s non-print prices and credited once on delivery; the quote is unchanged (D22)', async () => {
    const admin = await login('09120000000');
    const cust = await login('09123456789');
    const P = await testProviders(admin);
    const draft = { children: [], services: [docsSvc('bw')], planId: 'bronze' };
    const before = (await http().post('/api/orders/quote').send(draft).expect(200)).body;
    await setProviderPrice(admin, P.D.id, 'docs|bw|single', 600);
    await setProviderPrice(admin, P.D.id, 'docs|hardcover', 60000);
    expect((await http().post('/api/orders/quote').send(draft).expect(200)).body).toEqual(before); // D22
    await http().post('/api/admin/price-book/versions').set(authH(admin))
      .send({ layer: 'customer', comboKey: 'docs|bw|single', mode: 'manual', price: 1, publish: true, reason: 'e2e' }).expect(400);
    const start = await balanceOf(admin, P.D.id);
    const order = await placeCod(cust, [docsSvc('bw')]);
    const job = await jobOf(admin, order.id);
    const e = (await http().get(`/api/admin/dispatch/jobs/${job.id}/eligibility`).set(authH(admin)).expect(200)).body;
    expect([...e.eligible, ...e.others].find((r: any) => r.providerId === P.D.id).price.subtotal).toBe(20 * 600 + 60000);
    const jobId = await produceAndDeliver(admin, P, order.id);
    expect((await balanceOf(admin, P.D.id)).available).toBe(start.available + 72000);
    const credits = (await http().get(`/api/admin/ledger/providers/${P.D.id}/entries?kind=credit`).set(authH(admin)).expect(200)).body.items;
    expect(credits.filter((x: any) => x.jobId === jobId)).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ state: 'counted', amount: 72000, role: 'production', orderCode: order.code })]);
    const d = await login(P.D.phone);
    expect((await http().get('/api/provider/ledger/entries').set(authH(d)).expect(200)).body.items[0]).toMatchObject({ kind: 'credit', amount: 72000, state: 'counted' });
    expect(JSON.stringify((await http().get(`/api/orders/${order.id}`).set(authH(cust)).expect(200)).body)).not.toMatch(/ledger|payoutBasis|providerPricing/);
  });

  it('D28: an unpriced line is assigned with confirmation and credited «نیازمند تعیین مبلغ» until an admin sets the amount, once', async () => {
    const admin = await login('09120000000');
    const cust = await login('09123456789');
    const P = await testProviders(admin);
    const start = await balanceOf(admin, P.D.id);
    const order = await placeCod(cust, [docsSvc('color')]); // D has no `docs|color|single` price
    const job = await jobOf(admin, order.id);
    const missing = await http().post(`/api/admin/dispatch/jobs/${job.id}/assign`).set(authH(admin)).send({ providerId: P.D.id }).expect(409);
    expect(missing.body.code).toBe('PROVIDER_PRICE_MISSING');
    const jobId = await produceAndDeliver(admin, P, order.id, true);
    const credit = (await http().get(`/api/admin/ledger/providers/${P.D.id}/entries?kind=credit`).set(authH(admin)).expect(200)).body.items.find((x: any) => x.jobId === jobId);
    expect(credit).toMatchObject({ state: 'pending', amount: null });
    expect(await balanceOf(admin, P.D.id)).toMatchObject({ available: start.available, pendingCount: start.pendingCount + 1 });
    await http().post(`/api/admin/ledger/entries/${credit.id}/resolve`).set(authH(admin)).send({ amount: 50000, reason: 'e2e توافق' }).expect(200);
    await http().post(`/api/admin/ledger/entries/${credit.id}/resolve`).set(authH(admin)).send({ amount: 1, reason: 'دوباره' }).expect(409);
    expect(await balanceOf(admin, P.D.id)).toMatchObject({ available: start.available + 50000, pendingCount: start.pendingCount });
  });

  it('D31: Sheba checksum, payouts never above the balance (also concurrently), a void once; the provider is told and sees a masked account (D19)', async () => {
    const admin = await login('09120000000');
    const P = await testProviders(admin);
    await http().put(`/api/admin/ledger/providers/${P.D.id}/bank`).set(authH(admin)).send({ sheba: 'IR062960000000100324200002', holder: 'آزمون دی' }).expect(400);
    await http().put(`/api/admin/ledger/providers/${P.D.id}/bank`).set(authH(admin)).send({ sheba: '۰۶۲۹۶۰۰۰۰۰۰۰۱۰۰۳۲۴۲۰۰۰۰۱', holder: 'آزمون دی' }).expect(200);
    await http().post('/api/admin/ledger/adjustments').set(authH(admin)).send({ providerId: P.D.id, amount: 100000, reason: 'e2e پایه پرداخت', requestId: crypto.randomUUID() }).expect(201);
    const { available } = await balanceOf(admin, P.D.id);
    const pay = (amount: number, requestId = crypto.randomUUID()) =>
      http().post('/api/admin/ledger/payouts').set(authH(admin)).send({ providerId: P.D.id, amount, paidAt: tehranToday(), bankRef: 'E2E-TRX', requestId });
    expect((await pay(available + 1)).status).toBe(400);
    const share = Math.floor(available * 0.6);
    const results = await Promise.all([pay(share), pay(share)]);
    expect(results.map((r) => r.status).sort()).toEqual([201, 400]);
    expect((await balanceOf(admin, P.D.id)).available).toBe(available - share);
    const payout = results.find((r) => r.status === 201)!.body;
    const same = crypto.randomUUID();
    const twice = await Promise.all([pay(1, same), pay(1, same)]);
    expect(twice.map((r) => r.status)).toEqual([201, 201]);
    expect(twice[0].body.id).toBe(twice[1].body.id); // R13
    await http().post(`/api/admin/ledger/payouts/${payout.id}/void`).set(authH(admin)).send({ reason: 'e2e ابطال' }).expect(200);
    await http().post(`/api/admin/ledger/payouts/${payout.id}/void`).set(authH(admin)).send({ reason: 'دوباره' }).expect(409);
    expect((await balanceOf(admin, P.D.id)).available).toBe(available - 1);
    const d = await login(P.D.phone);
    const inbox = (await http().get('/api/notifications/mine').set(authH(d)).expect(200)).body.items;
    expect(inbox.some((n: any) => n.event === 'payout_recorded' && n.audience === 'provider' && n.orderId === '' && n.url === '/provider/settlement')).toBe(true);
    expect(inbox.some((n: any) => n.event === 'payout_voided')).toBe(true);
    expect((await http().get('/api/provider/ledger').set(authH(d)).expect(200)).body.bank).toEqual({ sheba: `IR${'•'.repeat(20)}0001`, holder: 'آزمون دی' });
  });

  it('D33 + §64: finance permissions per route; a provider sees only its own ledger and no admin route', async () => {
    const admin = await login('09120000000');
    const P = await testProviders(admin);
    const phone = '09127777779';
    const list = (await http().get('/api/admin/staff').set(authH(admin)).expect(200)).body as any[];
    const existing = list.find((s) => s.phone === phone);
    const member = existing
      ? (await http().patch(`/api/admin/staff/${existing.id}`).set(authH(admin)).send({ permissions: ['ledger_view'] }).expect(200)).body
      : (await http().post('/api/admin/staff').set(authH(admin)).send({ phone, name: 'e2e finance', permissions: ['ledger_view'] }).expect(201)).body;
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1100));
    tokens.delete(phone);
    const staff = await login(phone);
    await http().get('/api/admin/ledger/balances').set(authH(staff)).expect(200);
    await http().get(`/api/admin/ledger/wallet?from=${ymdDaysAgo(6)}&to=${tehranToday()}`).set(authH(staff)).expect(200);
    await http().post('/api/admin/ledger/payouts').set(authH(staff)).send({}).expect(403);
    await http().post('/api/admin/ledger/adjustments').set(authH(staff)).send({}).expect(403);
    await http().post('/api/admin/ledger/entries/000000000000000000000000/resolve').set(authH(staff)).send({}).expect(403);
    await http().put(`/api/admin/ledger/providers/${P.D.id}/bank`).set(authH(staff)).send({}).expect(403);
    const c = await login(P.C.phone);
    await http().get('/api/admin/ledger/balances').set(authH(c)).expect(403);
    const cLines = (await http().get('/api/provider/ledger/entries?limit=100').set(authH(c)).expect(200)).body.items as any[];
    const dIds = new Set(((await http().get(`/api/admin/ledger/providers/${P.D.id}/entries?limit=100`).set(authH(admin)).expect(200)).body.items as any[]).map((x) => x.id));
    expect(cLines.some((l) => dIds.has(l.id))).toBe(false);
    expect(JSON.stringify(cLines)).not.toMatch(/"reason"|"basis"|"createdBy"|"providerId"|"slot"/);
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1100));
    await http().delete(`/api/admin/staff/${member.id}`).set(authH(admin)).expect(200);
    tokens.delete(phone);
  });

  it('D32: «کیف پول دیجیتال سرو» report and its CSV export', async () => {
    const admin = await login('09120000000');
    const from = ymdDaysAgo(27);
    const to = tehranToday();
    const r = (await http().get(`/api/admin/ledger/wallet?from=${from}&to=${to}`).set(authH(admin)).expect(200)).body;
    expect(r.total.share).toBe(r.total.charged - r.total.providerCost - r.total.courierCost);
    expect(r.total.providerCost).toBeGreaterThan(0); // the credits / adjustments above were counted today
    expect(r.rows.at(-1).to).toBe(to);
    const csv = await http().get(`/api/admin/ledger/wallet/export?from=${from}&to=${to}`).set(authH(admin)).expect(200);
    expect(csv.headers['content-type']).toMatch(/text\/csv/);
    expect(csv.headers['content-disposition']).toContain(`sarv-wallet-${from}-${to}.csv`);
    expect(csv.text.startsWith('﻿')).toBe(true);
    await http().get('/api/admin/ledger/wallet?from=2025-01-01&to=2026-06-01').set(authH(admin)).expect(400);
  });
```

`CLAUDE.md`: the intro mentions «تسویه مجریان» (`/admin/ledger`) and the provider app's «تسویه» tab; the dev-login line adds «نگین has a demo bank account and ledger»; add under «Rules that matter»:
```markdown
- **v4.3 ledger** (`modules/ledger`, contract «v4.3»): provider money lives only in `ledgerEntries` — append-only, every write a keyed upsert (`LedgerService.insertOnce`); a counted amount is never edited (corrections are adjustments, a payout is undone by a `payout_void`); only a pending credit's amount is set once. Admin entries go through `LedgerService.withSlot` (per-provider `rev` + unique `slot`) so a payout never exceeds the balance; balances are always derived from the entries. A job's move to `delivered` sets `ledgerPending` in the same `cas` and the ledger credits it afterwards (the dispatch tick retries); never credit by hand. Provider credit = the assignment snapshot (D26); discounts and fees are Digital Sarv's share. Finance permissions `ledger_view` / `ledger_adjust` / `payout_record`; the bank account is admin-only (`payout_record`), shown masked to the provider.
- Provider-only price-book combos (`school|…`, `docs|…`, `flyer|…`, `cart|…`, `repair|…`, `extra|…`, D29) never get a customer version; the customer quote never reads them.
```
