## Handoff to M5 (security and regression verification)

What M4 leaves for M5 to verify or finish:
- **Security (§91) of the ledger:** an e2e permission matrix for every `/admin/ledger*` route × {super-admin, `ledger_view`, `ledger_adjust`, `payout_record`, other staff, provider, courier, customer, anonymous}; IDOR on `/provider/ledger*` (no entry-id routes exist — keep it that way) and on `PUT /admin/ledger/providers/:id/bank`; that no customer, courier or provider response carries `basis`, `reason`, `createdBy`, `slot`, `key`, margins or customer prices; CSV export headers (`Content-Disposition` filename is built from validated `yyyy-mm-dd` only).
- **Bank account changes:** M4 stores who/when on the current account only. M5 should decide on a change history (append-only, like `priceAudit`) and a provider notification when the payout account changes (fraud signal), and whether a second admin must confirm a Sheba change.
- **Regression (§92):** Playwright (`playwright-core`, `msedge`) on all five apps including «تسویه مجریان» (all four tabs, provider sheet, dialogs) and «سرو مجری» → «تسویه» at 1280/390 px; the full flow assign → deliver → credit → payout → void; D22 quote byte-identity for every service kind with and without D29 provider prices.
- **R11 backfill on a production copy:** run `seed:base` on a copy of production data: jobs delivered before M4 are credited once (snapshot-priced ones counted, migrated / confirmed-missing ones pending); re-running changes nothing.
- **Deferred M2 item — recoverable pending wallet credit:** the Zibal mismatch credit (and the customer refund in `cancelAndRefund`) are still «marker/status first, then `$inc`»; a crash in between loses the wallet credit (logged at ERROR). Apply the ledger pattern: an idempotent keyed record written in the same compare-and-set as the marker, applied by a reconcile pass.
- **Other carried items:** `CentersPage.tsx` (unrouted since M2) — delete or keep; the price-book compare view and the compensation queue are unpaginated / bounded scans (compare all, queue ≤ 1000 jobs), and balances are computed for all providers in memory — add paging if providers or cases grow; the wallet report filters orders by `timeline` `$elemMatch` without an index (add `{ 'timeline.status': 1, 'timeline.at': 1 }` if a production-size check is slow).
- **Owner follow-ups (not M5 code):** the courier pay in the wallet report is an estimate with the current per-task fee (R9) — exact courier pay would need a per-task snapshot; repair provider prices are service fees only (R4); the providers' real Sheba numbers.

## Self-review

### Spec coverage

| Spec / scope item | Requirement | Task(s) |
|---|---|---|
| §41 | customer payment ≠ provider payout: provider credit from the provider price snapshot only | 5, 6, 7, 8 |
| §42 | gross margin internal: never to customers or providers | 9 (admin-only views), 13 (provider whitelist), 19 |
| §66 | no unnecessary payout complexity (no bonus/penalty/deduction) | Global Constraints, 6 (credit = snapshot basis + own delivery fee only) |
| D8 / D16 | provider share = provider price; Digital Sarv collects all and pays providers manually | 6, 11, 12 |
| D20 / D31 | weekly settlement, weekday in «تنظیمات», statement up to the cutoff minus payouts, payout ≤ balance with date + bank reference, void only by a reversing entry, Sheba bank account | 2, 3, 9, 11, 15, 16 |
| D22 | customer pricing and quote unchanged | 4 (customer layer refuses provider-only combos), 5 (pricing tests), 19 (quote `toEqual`) |
| D23 → D29 | non-print provider prices: combos, submission/approval, snapshot prices them, «قیمت مجری تنظیم نشده» only when still unpriced | 4, 5, 14, 18, 19 |
| D24 | providers see only their own data | 13, 19 |
| D25 | timings unchanged (auto-complete after delivery does not affect credits) | 8 (credit at `delivered`, not `completed`) |
| D26 | credit = assignment snapshot payout basis (+ own delivery fee); discounts/plan/coupon/rules/urgent → Digital Sarv | 6, 8 |
| D27 | credit when delivered and paid (COD at cash); one per (job, provider, role); append-only, idempotent | 2 (keys), 3 (unique index), 7, 8 |
| D28 | missing provider price → «نیازمند تعیین مبلغ», not in the balance until an admin sets it with a reason | 6, 7, 9, 10, 17, 19 |
| D30 | cancellation before production → nothing; during/after → «جبران خسارت مجری» 0/part/full ≤ basis (manual when unpriced) with a reason; refund after delivery → manual negative adjustment | 10, 16 (adjust dialog), 17 |
| D32 | provider «تسویه» (balance, lines, payouts; no customer prices/margins/others); «کیف پول دیجیتال سرو» per period with CSV | 12, 13, 17, 18, 19 |
| D33 | `ledger_view`, `ledger_adjust`, `payout_record`; super-admins have all; staff screen | 3, 9–13 (route metadata), 15 (staff group), 19 |
| D19 | provider notifications in-app + push for payouts | 3 (events, templates, order-less inbox), 11, 15 |
| Scope: atomicity | money writes are conditional/keyed; payout concurrency guard | 7 (`withSlot`), 10, 11 (concurrency tests) |
| Scope: M3 hook | flag in the delivered `cas`, never-throw hook, reconcile for misses | 8 |
| Scope: seed / e2e / docs | demo data, e2e, docs | 14, 19 |

Out of scope by design: bonuses, penalties, automatic deductions (§66, §93), automatic bank transfers (D16: manual payouts), exact courier pay (R9 estimate), splitting an order into several jobs (V1 one job per order).

### Placeholder scan
Every code step carries its code. Task 16 creates three one-line placeholder tabs (`PendingTab`, `CompensationTab`, `WalletTab`) that Task 17 replaces in the same milestone, so each task builds on its own. Web tasks have no unit-test runner in this repo; their cycle is `tsc -b` (via `pnpm --filter web build`) + lint + the controller's browser pass (Task 19 Step 3). Task 14's seed is verified by the build and one `pnpm seed` run.

### Type consistency
API names used across tasks: `LedgerKind`, `LedgerState`, `CreditRole`, `AdjustmentCause`, `CompCause`, `CompStage`, `ledgerKey.{credit, compensation, adjustment, payout, void}`, `slotOf`, `isAmount`, `entryTitle`, `settlementCutoff`, `nextSettlementYmd`, `weekEndingAt` (Task 2); `LedgerEntry`, `CreditBasis`, `PayoutInfo`, `LedgerAccount`, `BankAccount` (Task 3); `ProviderPricingLine.childIndex/childName`, `ProviderQuote.childCount`, `ProviderPricingSnapshot.childCount`, `childProviderLines`, `serviceProviderLines` (Task 5); `CreditDraft`, `creditDrafts`, `payoutFor`, `childLineQuantity` (Task 6); `Balance`, `EMPTY_BALANCE`, `NewEntry`, `LedgerService.insertOnce / withSlot(providerId, key, build) / balanceOf / balances / creditJob / reconcile / weekday / cutoff` (Task 7); `JobsService` 6th argument `ledger`, `DispatchScheduler` 6th argument `ledger`, `TickResult.ledger` (Task 8); `EntryRow`, `LedgerEntryView`, `entryView`, `ProviderLedgerLine`, `providerLine`, `LedgerReadsService.findProvider / balances / provider / entries / statement / pending / views / providerNames / providerSummary / providerEntries`, `bankView`, `SettlementInfo`, `BankAccountView`, `ProviderBalanceRow`, `ProviderLedgerAdmin`, `WeeklyStatement`, `ProviderLedgerSelf` (Tasks 9, 10, 13); `compensationCandidates`, `basisOf`, `CompensationItem`, `LedgerActionsService.resolve / compensationQueue / decideCompensation / adjust`, `needReason`, `AMOUNT_MSG` (Task 10); `LedgerNotifier.payout`, `PayoutsService.record / void / setBank` (Task 11); `walletReport`, `walletCsv`, `WalletService.report / csv` (Task 12). The web types of Task 15 mirror the contract shapes of Task 1 one-to-one (`LedgerEntryView`, `ProviderBalanceRow`, `ProviderLedgerAdmin`, `WeeklyStatement`, `CompensationItem`, `WalletReport`, `ProviderLedgerSelf`, `ProviderLedgerLine`); the web hooks (`useLedgerBalances(page, q)`, `useProviderLedger(id)`, `useLedgerEntries(id, params)`, `useStatement(id, cutoff)`, `usePendingCredits(page, enabled)`, `useCompensation(page, enabled)`, `useWallet(from, to, enabled)`, `useLedgerMutations()`, `useProviderSettlement()`, `useProviderLedgerEntries(page)`) are used with the same signatures in Tasks 16–18.

### Review Focus check
1 → Task 7 `two concurrent writers on one balance…`, Task 11 `concurrent payouts never exceed the balance` and `a retried request returns the same payout (also concurrently)…`, Task 19 D31 e2e (`[201, 400]`, same `requestId` → same id). 2 → Task 7 `never throws: a failing write is logged…`, Task 8 `a failed ledger write is retried by the tick…` and `provider delivery credits the producer … exactly once`. 3 → Task 6 `the book count changed…` and `a dropped or renamed child, or a dropped / added line → pending…`. 4 → Task 11 `a payout is voided once…` and `void and a new payout race through the slot…`, Task 19 (409 on the second void). 5 → Task 13 `entries: only the caller’s lines with whitelisted fields…` and `the controller is provider-only … read-only`, Task 9 `AdminLedgerController (D33)` route table, Task 19 D33 + §64 e2e.
